# ==============================================================================
# SESSION MANAGEMENT
# ==============================================================================
clear_all_sessions() {
  rm -f "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  rm -rf "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR" "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
}

session_touch() {
  local dir="$1"
  [ -n "$dir" ] && [ -d "$dir" ] && date +%s > "$dir/touched" 2>/dev/null
}

session_check_timeout() {
  local dir="$1" label="$2"
  [ -d "$dir" ] || return 0
  [ -f "$dir/step" ] || return 0
  local now mtime
  now=$(date +%s)
  mtime=$(cat "$dir/touched" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
  if [ -z "$mtime" ]; then mtime=$now; date +%s > "$dir/touched" 2>/dev/null; fi
  if [ $((now - mtime)) -ge "$SESSION_TIMEOUT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$dir" 2>/dev/null
    tg_send "⏰ $label: $(T session_timeout)"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

check_single_input_timeout() {
  [ -f "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" ] || return 0
  local now mtime
  now=$(date +%s)
  mtime=$(cat "$BOT_INPUT_STATE.ts" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
  if [ -z "$mtime" ]; then mtime=$now; date +%s > "$BOT_INPUT_STATE.ts" 2>/dev/null; fi
  if [ $((now - mtime)) -ge "$SESSION_TIMEOUT" ]; then
    rm -f "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" "$BOT_INPUT_STATE.ts" 2>/dev/null
    tg_send "⏰ $(T session_timeout)"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

set_single_input() {
  printf '%s' "$1" > "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" 2>/dev/null
  date +%s > "$BOT_INPUT_STATE.ts" 2>/dev/null
}

# ==============================================================================
# TRANSLATIONS
# ==============================================================================
load_language() {
  FARM_LANG="RU"
  if [ -f "$LANG_FILE" ]; then
    local l
    l=$(cat "$LANG_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
    if [ "$l" = "EN" ] || [ "$l" = "RU" ]; then FARM_LANG="$l"; fi
  fi
}

set_language() {
  local new="$1"
  if [ "$new" != "RU" ] && [ "$new" != "EN" ]; then return 1; fi
  echo "$new" > "$LANG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  FARM_LANG="$new"
}

E() { T "$1_$2"; }

error_severity() {
  case "$1" in
    E01|E02|E03|E04|E21|E24) echo "🔴" ;;
    E05|E06|E07|E08|E09|E22|E27|E28) echo "🟠" ;;
    E10|E12|E13|E14|E15|E23|E25|E26|E30) echo "🟡" ;;
    E16|E17|E18|E19|E20|E29) echo "🟢" ;;
    *) echo "⚪" ;;
  esac
}

error_severity_name() {
  case "$1" in
    E01|E02|E03|E04|E21|E24) echo "CRITICAL" ;;
    E05|E06|E07|E08|E09|E22|E27|E28) echo "HIGH" ;;
    E10|E12|E13|E14|E15|E23|E25|E26|E30) echo "MEDIUM" ;;
    E16|E17|E18|E19|E20|E29) echo "INFO" ;;
    *) echo "UNKNOWN" ;;
  esac
}

error_severity_order() {
  case "$1" in
    "🟢") echo "1" ;;
    "🟡") echo "2" ;;
    "🟠") echo "3" ;;
    "🔴") echo "4" ;;
    *) echo "9" ;;
  esac
}

sorted_error_codes() {
  local codes="E01 E02 E03 E04 E05 E06 E07 E08 E09 E10 E12 E13 E14 E15 E16 E17 E18 E19 E20 E21 E22 E23 E24 E25 E26 E27 E28 E29 E30"
  local green="" yellow="" orange="" red="" c sev
  for c in $codes; do
    sev=$(error_severity "$c")
    case "$sev" in
      "🟢") green="$green $c" ;;
      "🟡") yellow="$yellow $c" ;;
      "🟠") orange="$orange $c" ;;
      "🔴") red="$red $c" ;;
    esac
  done
  echo $green $yellow $orange $red
}

error_block_tg() {
  local code="$1" extra="${2:-}"
  local sev sev_name what why fix
  sev=$(error_severity "$code"); sev_name=$(error_severity_name "$code")
  what=$(E "$code" what); why=$(E "$code" why); fix=$(E "$code" fix)
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'
  printf '🆘 %s · %s %s\n' "$code" "$sev" "$sev_name"
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'
  printf '%s\n' "$what"
  printf '\n❓ %s\n' "$why"
  printf '🛠 %s\n' "$fix"
  [ -n "$extra" ] && printf '📄 %s\n' "$extra"
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━'
}

error_block_log() {
  local code="$1" extra="${2:-}"
  local sev sev_name what why fix
  sev=$(error_severity "$code"); sev_name=$(error_severity_name "$code")
  what=$(E "$code" what); why=$(E "$code" why); fix=$(E "$code" fix)
  {
    printf '──────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
    printf '🆘 %s · %s %s · %s\n' "$code" "$sev" "$sev_name" "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
    printf '   ❓ %s\n   🔎 %s\n   🛠 %s\n' "$what" "$why" "$fix"
    [ -n "$extra" ] && printf '   📄 %s\n' "$extra"
    printf '──────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
  }
}

send_error_tg() { log_error "$1" "${2:-}"; tg_send "$(error_block_tg "$1" "${2:-}")"; }

# ==============================================================================
# UTILITIES
# ==============================================================================
short_text() {
  local s
  s=$(printf '%s' "$1" | sed 's/\\n/\
/g')
  if [ "${#s}" -gt 4000 ]; then printf '%s' "${s:0:3950}\n…truncated"
  else printf '%s' "$s"; fi
}

json_escape() {
  local out
  out=$(printf '%s' "$1" | jq -Rs . 2>/dev/null)
  if [ -n "$out" ]; then printf '%s' "$out"; return 0; fi
  printf '"%s"' "$(printf '%s' "$1" | sed -e 's/\\/\\\\/g' -e 's/"/\\"/g' | tr '\n' ' ' | tr '\r' ' ' | tr '\t' ' ')"
}

send_tg_plain() {
  local txt
  txt=$(short_text "$1")
  curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST \
    --data-urlencode "chat_id=$TG_CHAT_ID" \
    --data-urlencode "text=$txt" \
    "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/sendMessage" >/dev/null 2>&1
}

parse_hh_mm_to_seconds() {
  local raw="$1"
  if ! echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]{1,2}:[0-9]{2}$'; then echo "-1"; return 1; fi
  local h="${raw%:*}" m="${raw#*:}"
  h=$(echo "$h" | sed 's/^0*//'); [ -z "$h" ] && h=0
  m=$(echo "$m" | sed 's/^0*//'); [ -z "$m" ] && m=0
  local sec=$((h * 3600 + m * 60))
  [ "$sec" -lt 60 ] && sec=60
  echo "$sec"
}

parse_interval_any() {
  local raw="$1" val hh mm
  [ -z "$raw" ] && { echo "-1"; return 1; }
  if echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]{1,2}:[0-9]{2}$'; then parse_hh_mm_to_seconds "$raw"; return 0; fi
  if echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]{4}$'; then
    hh="${raw:0:2}"; mm="${raw:2:2}"
    hh=$(echo "$hh" | sed 's/^0*//'); [ -z "$hh" ] && hh=0
    mm=$(echo "$mm" | sed 's/^0*//'); [ -z "$mm" ] && mm=0
    if [ "$hh" -le 23 ] 2>/dev/null && [ "$mm" -le 59 ] 2>/dev/null; then
      val=$((hh * 3600 + mm * 60)); [ "$val" -lt 60 ] && val=60
      echo "$val"; return 0
    fi
  fi
  if echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+[mM]$'; then val="${raw%[mM]}"; echo $((val * 60)); return 0; fi
  if echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+[hH]$'; then val="${raw%[hH]}"; echo $((val * 3600)); return 0; fi
  if echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+[sS]$'; then val="${raw%[sS]}"; echo "$val"; return 0; fi
  if echo "$raw" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
    if [ "$raw" -lt 1000 ]; then echo $((raw * 60)); else echo "$raw"; fi
    return 0
  fi
  echo "-1"; return 1
}

is_time_token() { echo "$1" | grep -Eq '^([0-9]{1,2}:[0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4}|[0-9]+[mMhHsS]|[0-9]{3,})$'; }
is_time_of_day_token() { echo "$1" | grep -Eq '^@[0-9]{1,2}:[0-9]{2}$'; }

format_time_left() {
  local target="$1"
  case "$target" in
    ''|*[!0-9]*) [ "${FARM_LANG:-RU}" = "EN" ] && echo "Ready" || echo "Готов"; return 0 ;;
  esac
  local now left
  now=${EPOCHSECONDS:-$(date +%s)}; left=$((target - now))
  if [ "$left" -le 0 ]; then [ "${FARM_LANG:-RU}" = "EN" ] && echo "Ready" || echo "Готов"
  else printf "%02d:%02d:%02d" $((left/3600)) $(((left%3600)/60)) $((left%60)); fi
}

format_seconds() {
  local sec="$1"
  case "$sec" in ''|*[!0-9]*) echo "0m"; return 0 ;; esac
  local h=$((sec/3600)) m=$(((sec%3600)/60))
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    if [ "$h" -gt 0 ]; then printf "%dh %02dm" "$h" "$m"; else printf "%dm" "$m"; fi
  else
    if [ "$h" -gt 0 ]; then printf "%dч %02dм" "$h" "$m"; else printf "%dм" "$m"; fi
  fi
}

trim14() {
  local s="$1"
  s="${s#"${s%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
  s="${s%"${s##*[![:space:]]}"}"
  printf '%s' "$s"
}

next_time_occurrence() {
  local hhmm="$1"
  local h="${hhmm%:*}" m="${hhmm#*:}"
  h=$((10#$h)); m=$((10#$m))
  local now hh mm ss now_sec target_sec today_start target
  now=$(date +%s)
  hh=$(date +%H); mm=$(date +%M); ss=$(date +%S)
  hh=$((10#$hh)); mm=$((10#$mm)); ss=$((10#$ss))
  now_sec=$((hh*3600 + mm*60 + ss)); target_sec=$((h*3600 + m*60))
  today_start=$((now - now_sec))
  target=$((today_start + target_sec))
  [ "$target" -le "$now" ] && target=$((target + 86400))
  echo "$target"
}

# ==============================================================================
# NETWORK
# ==============================================================================
check_telegram_connection() {
  local resp
  resp=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 5 -m 8 "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getMe" 2>/dev/null)
  if echo "$resp" | grep -q '"ok":true'; then
    echo "1" > "$NET_CHECK_CACHE_FILE"; date +%s > "$NET_CHECK_TS_FILE"
    rm -f "$TG_LAST_ERR_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    return 0
  fi
  resp=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 5 -m 8 "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getMe" 2>/dev/null)
  if echo "$resp" | grep -q '"ok":true'; then
    echo "1" > "$NET_CHECK_CACHE_FILE"; date +%s > "$NET_CHECK_TS_FILE"
    rm -f "$TG_LAST_ERR_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    return 0
  fi
  echo "0" > "$NET_CHECK_CACHE_FILE"; date +%s > "$NET_CHECK_TS_FILE"
  if [ -n "$resp" ]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$resp" > "$TG_LAST_ERR_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    if echo "$resp" | grep -q '"ok":false'; then return 2; fi
  fi
  return 1
}

pre_macro_network_check() {
  local now cache_ts cached_val rc cache_ttl
  now=$(date +%s)
  cache_ts=$(cat "$NET_CHECK_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
  cached_val=$(cat "$NET_CHECK_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  [ -z "$cache_ts" ] && cache_ts=0
  if [ -n "$cached_val" ]; then
    if [ "$cached_val" = "1" ]; then cache_ttl=$NET_CHECK_POS_TTL; else cache_ttl=$NET_CHECK_NEG_TTL; fi
    if [ $((now - cache_ts)) -lt "$cache_ttl" ]; then
      [ "$cached_val" = "1" ] && return 0 || return 1
    fi
  fi
  check_telegram_connection
  rc=$?
  if [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then return 0
  elif [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then send_error_tg E04; return 1; fi
  log_error E05; trigger_vibration_alert; return 1
}

trigger_vibration_alert() {
  log "📳 VIBRATE" "No Telegram connection"
  ( send_tg_plain "$(T vibrate_alert)\n$(T net_check_fail)" ) >/dev/null 2>&1 &
  local vib_ok=0
  if command -v termux-vibrate >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    for i in 1 2 3; do termux-vibrate -d 3000 -f >/dev/null 2>&1 && vib_ok=1; sleep 0.5; done
  fi
  if [ "$vib_ok" = "0" ] && command -v cmd >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    for i in 1 2 3; do cmd vibrator vibrate 3000 >/dev/null 2>&1 && vib_ok=1; sleep 0.5; done
  fi
  wait 2>/dev/null
}

# ==============================================================================
# CREDENTIALS
# ==============================================================================
show_setup_banner() {
  cat <<'SETUP_BANNER'

╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║     ВСТАВЬТЕ ВАШИ ДАННЫЕ / INSERT YOUR DATA                          ║
║       TG_TOKEN="   <-- ВСТАВЬТЕ СЮДА / INSERT HERE   "               ║
║       TG_CHAT_ID=" <-- ВСТАВЬТЕ СЮДА / INSERT HERE   "               ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

SETUP_BANNER
}

load_credentials() {
  local t="" c="" src=""
  if [ -f "$TG_CONFIG_FILE" ]; then
    t=$(grep '^TOKEN=' "$TG_CONFIG_FILE" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 | cut -d= -f2-)
    c=$(grep '^CHAT_ID=' "$TG_CONFIG_FILE" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 | cut -d= -f2-)
    [ -n "$t" ] && [ -n "$c" ] && src="config"
  fi
  if [ -z "$t" ] && [ -n "$TG_TOKEN" ] && [ "$TG_TOKEN" != "__INSERT_HERE__" ]; then t="$TG_TOKEN"; src="script"; fi
  if [ -z "$c" ] && [ -n "$TG_CHAT_ID" ] && [ "$TG_CHAT_ID" != "__INSERT_HERE__" ]; then c="$TG_CHAT_ID"; src="script"; fi
  if [ -n "$t" ] && [ -n "$c" ] && [ "$src" = "script" ]; then
    mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null
    printf 'TOKEN=%s\nCHAT_ID=%s\n' "$t" "$c" > "$TG_CONFIG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  fi
  if [ -z "$t" ] || [ -z "$c" ]; then
    show_setup_banner
    printf '\n\033[1;33m>>> ENTER YOUR TELEGRAM BOT TOKEN:\033[0m\n> '; read -r t
    printf '\n\033[1;33m>>> ENTER YOUR CHAT ID:\033[0m\n> '; read -r c
    t=$(printf '%s' "$t" | tr -d ' \r\n'); c=$(printf '%s' "$c" | tr -d ' \r\n')
    if [ -z "$t" ] || [ -z "$c" ]; then
      show_setup_banner; printf '\n\033[1;31m[!] No data entered. Exit.\033[0m\n'; exit 1
    fi
    mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null
    printf 'TOKEN=%s\nCHAT_ID=%s\n' "$t" "$c" > "$TG_CONFIG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  fi
  TG_TOKEN="$t"; TG_CHAT_ID="$c"
}

save_creds_to_config() {
  [ -z "$TG_TOKEN" ] || [ -z "$TG_CHAT_ID" ] && return 0
  mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  printf 'TOKEN=%s\nCHAT_ID=%s\n' "$TG_TOKEN" "$TG_CHAT_ID" > "$TG_CONFIG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
}

# ==============================================================================
# BOT REGISTRY
# ==============================================================================
read_bot_file() {
  local f="$BOT_DIR/$1/$2.txt" line=""
  if [ -f "$f" ]; then
    read -r line < "$f" 2>/dev/null
    printf '%s' "${line%$'\r'}"
  fi
}
write_bot_file() { mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$1" 2>/dev/null; printf '%s\n' "$2" > "$BOT_DIR/$1/$3.txt" 2>/dev/null; }
known_bot_ids() { ls -1 "$BOT_DIR" 2>/dev/null | grep -E '^[0-9]+$' | sort -n; }

next_bot_id() {
  local max=0 id
  for id in $(known_bot_ids); do [ "$id" -gt "$max" ] && max=$id; done
  [ "$max" -lt 3 ] && max=3; echo $((max + 1))
}

get_bot_icon() {
  local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" icon)
  if [ -n "$val" ]; then printf '%s' "$val"; return 0; fi
  case "$1" in 1) echo "📱" ;; 2) echo "💻" ;; 3) echo "🐰" ;; *) echo "🤖" ;; esac
}

get_bot_name() {
  local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" name)
  if [ -n "$val" ]; then echo "$val"; return 0; fi
  case "$1" in
    1) [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ] && echo "Phones" || echo "Телефоны" ;;
    2) [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ] && echo "Computers" || echo "Компьютеры" ;;
    3) [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ] && echo "Rabbits" || echo "Кролики" ;;
    *) echo "Bot $1" ;;
  esac
}

get_bot_domain() {
  local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" domain)
  if [ -n "$val" ]; then echo "$val"; return 0; fi
  case "$1" in
    1) echo "phonegetcardsbot" ;; 2) echo "createpcpartsbot" ;; 3) echo "cabbitget_bot" ;;
    *) echo "" ;;
  esac
}

get_bot_cmd() {
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$1" | head -n1)
  if [ -n "$first" ]; then get_batch_command "$1" "$first"
  else local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" cmd); [ -n "$val" ] && echo "$val" || echo "/start"; fi
}

get_bot_cmd_encoded() {
  local s enc; s=$(get_bot_cmd "$1")
  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    enc=$(jq -rn --arg s "$s" '$s|@uri|gsub("%2F";"/")' 2>/dev/null)
    [ -n "$enc" ] && { printf '%s' "$enc"; return 0; }
  fi
  printf '%s' "$s" | sed -e 's/ /%20/g' -e 's/#/%23/g' -e 's/&/%26/g' -e 's/?/%3F/g'
}

get_bot_interval() {
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$1" | head -n1)
  if [ -n "$first" ]; then get_batch_interval "$1" "$first"
  else
    local id="$1" val def
    case "$id" in 1) def=9030 ;; 2) def=10830 ;; 3) def=3630 ;; *) def=3600 ;; esac
    val=$(read_bot_file "$id" interval)
    if [ -z "$val" ] && [ "$id" = "1" ] && [ -f "$INTERVAL_FILE_1" ]; then
      val=$(cat "$INTERVAL_FILE_1" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n1)
    fi
    echo "$val" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || val="$def"
    [ "$val" -lt 60 ] && val=60
    echo "$val"
  fi
}

is_bot_upgradeable() {
  local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" upgradeable)
  if [ -z "$val" ]; then [ "$1" = "1" ]; return $?; fi
  [ "$val" = "1" ]
}

toggle_upgradeable() {
  if is_bot_upgradeable "$1"; then write_bot_file "$1" "0" upgradeable
  else write_bot_file "$1" "1" upgradeable; fi
}

is_bot_enabled() {
  local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" enabled)
  [ -z "$val" ] && val="1"; [ "$val" = "1" ]
}

toggle_bot() {
  if is_bot_enabled "$1"; then write_bot_file "$1" "0" enabled
  else write_bot_file "$1" "1" enabled; fi
}

is_stock_bot() {
  local val; val=$(read_bot_file "$1" stock)
  if [ -n "$val" ]; then [ "$val" = "1" ]; return $?; fi
  case "$1" in 1|2|3) return 0 ;; esac
  return 1
}

is_farm_paused() { [ -f "$PAUSE_FILE" ]; }

# ==============================================================================
# BATCH DATA MODEL
# Format: bid|interval_sec|xcount|command|type|time_hhmm|remaining
# ==============================================================================
get_bot_batches() {
  local id="$1"
  local bf="$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  if [ -f "$bf" ]; then awk -F'|' 'NF>=4 && $1!="" {print $1}' "$bf" 2>/dev/null
  else echo "b1"; fi
}

get_batch_field() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2" field="$3"
  local bf="$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  if [ -f "$bf" ]; then
    awk -F'|' -v b="$bid" -v f="$field" '$1==b {print $f; exit}' "$bf" 2>/dev/null
  else
    case "$field" in
      1) echo "b1" ;;
      2) local v; v=$(read_bot_file "$id" interval); [ -z "$v" ] && v=$DEFAULT_BATCH_INTERVAL; echo "$v" ;;
      3) echo "1" ;;
      4) local v; v=$(read_bot_file "$id" cmd); [ -z "$v" ] && v="/start"; echo "$v" ;;
      5) echo "interval" ;;
      6) echo "" ;;
      7) echo "0" ;;
    esac
  fi
}

get_batch_interval() {
  local v; v=$(get_batch_field "$1" "$2" 2)
  echo "$v" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || v=$DEFAULT_BATCH_INTERVAL
  [ "$v" -lt 60 ] && v=60; echo "$v"
}
get_batch_xcount() {
  local v; v=$(get_batch_field "$1" "$2" 3)
  echo "$v" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || v=1
  [ "$v" -lt 1 ] && v=1; echo "$v"
}
get_batch_command() { local v; v=$(get_batch_field "$1" "$2" 4); [ -z "$v" ] && v="/start"; echo "$v"; }
get_batch_type() { local v; v=$(get_batch_field "$1" "$2" 5); [ "$v" != "time" ] && v="interval"; echo "$v"; }
get_batch_time() { get_batch_field "$1" "$2" 6; }
get_batch_remaining() { local v; v=$(get_batch_field "$1" "$2" 7); echo "$v" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || v=0; echo "$v"; }

set_bot_batches() {
  local id="$1"; shift
  mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$id"
  printf '%s\n' "$@" > "$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$id" | head -n1)
  if [ -n "$first" ]; then
    write_bot_file "$id" "$(get_batch_interval "$id" "$first")" interval
    write_bot_file "$id" "$(get_batch_command "$id" "$first")" cmd
  fi
}

update_batch_field() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2" field="$3" val="$4"
  local bf="$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  [ ! -f "$bf" ] && return 1
  local tmp="$bf.tmp.$$"
  awk -F'|' -v b="$bid" -v f="$field" -v v="$val" 'BEGIN{OFS="|"} $1==b {$f=v; print; next} {print}' "$bf" > "$tmp"
  mv -f "$tmp" "$bf"
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$id" | head -n1)
  if [ "$bid" = "$first" ]; then
    [ "$field" = "2" ] && write_bot_file "$id" "$val" interval
    [ "$field" = "4" ] && write_bot_file "$id" "$val" cmd
  fi
}

delete_batch() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2"
  local bf="$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  [ ! -f "$bf" ] && return 1
  local tmp="$bf.tmp.$$"
  awk -F'|' -v b="$bid" '$1!=b' "$bf" > "$tmp"
  mv -f "$tmp" "$bf"
  rm -f "$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}_${bid}.txt" 2>/dev/null
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$id" | head -n1)
  if [ -n "$first" ]; then
    write_bot_file "$id" "$(get_batch_interval "$id" "$first")" interval
    write_bot_file "$id" "$(get_batch_command "$id" "$first")" cmd
  fi
}

add_batch_line() {
  local id="$1" line="$2"
  local bf="$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$id"
  printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$bf"
}

get_next_batch_id() {
  local id="$1" max=0 bid n
  for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$id"); do
    n=$(echo "$bid" | sed 's/^b//')
    echo "$n" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || continue
    [ "$n" -gt "$max" ] && max=$n
  done
  if [ "$max" -lt 1 ]; then echo "b1"; else echo "b$((max + 1))"; fi
}

get_batch_target() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2"
  local f="$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}_${bid}.txt"
  local val=""
  if [ -f "$f" ]; then
    read -r val < "$f" 2>/dev/null
    val="${val//[^0-9]/}"
    if [ -n "$val" ]; then echo "$val"; return 0; fi
  fi
  local type now
  now=${EPOCHSECONDS:-$(date +%s)}
  type=$(get_batch_type "$id" "$bid")
  if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then
    val=$(next_time_occurrence "$(get_batch_time "$id" "$bid")")
  else
    val=$(( now + $(get_batch_interval "$id" "$bid") ))
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$val" > "$f" 2>/dev/null
  echo "$val"
}

set_batch_target() { echo "$3" > "$TIMER_DIR/target_$1_$2.txt"; }

get_bot_target() {
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$1" | head -n1)
  [ -z "$first" ] && first="b1"
  get_batch_target "$1" "$first"
}

set_bot_target() {
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$1" | head -n1)
  [ -z "$first" ] && first="b1"
  set_batch_target "$1" "$first" "$2"
}

migrate_bot_to_batches() {
  local id="$1"
  local bf="$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  [ -f "$bf" ] && return 0
  local int cmd
  int=$(read_bot_file "$id" interval); [ -z "$int" ] && int=$DEFAULT_BATCH_INTERVAL
  cmd=$(read_bot_file "$id" cmd); [ -z "$cmd" ] && cmd="/start"
  mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$id"
  printf 'b1|%s|1|%s|interval||0\n' "$int" "$cmd" > "$bf"
  local old_t="$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}.txt" new_t="$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}_b1.txt"
  if [ -f "$old_t" ] && [ ! -f "$new_t" ]; then mv -f "$old_t" "$new_t" 2>/dev/null; fi
}

migrate_all_bots_to_batches() {
  local id
  for id in $(known_bot_ids); do migrate_bot_to_batches "$id"; done
}

# ==============================================================================
# BATCH PARSER
# ==============================================================================
parse_batches_spec() {
  local spec="$1" -a tokens
  read -r -a tokens <<< "$spec"
  local n=${#tokens[@]}
  [ "$n" -eq 0 ] && return 1
  local default_time_sec=$DEFAULT_BATCH_INTERVAL
  local default_type="interval" default_time_today="" default_remaining=0
  local idx=0
  if is_time_of_day_token "${tokens[0]}"; then
    default_type="time"; default_time_today="${tokens[0]#@}"; idx=1
  elif is_time_token "${tokens[0]}"; then
    local t; t=$(parse_interval_any "${tokens[0]}")
    [ "$t" -ge 60 ] 2>/dev/null && default_time_sec=$t
    idx=1
  fi
  local result="" cur_bid="b1" cur_type="$default_type" cur_time_sec="$default_time_sec"
  local cur_time_today="$default_time_today" cur_x="1" cur_rem="0" cur_cmd=""
  local batch_count=0 i tok
  for ((i=idx; i<n; i++)); do
    tok="${tokens[i]}"
    if echo "$tok" | grep -Eq '^b[0-9]+$'; then
      if [ -n "$cur_cmd" ]; then
        result="${result}${cur_bid}|${cur_time_sec}|${cur_x}|${cur_cmd}|${cur_type}|${cur_time_today}|${cur_rem}"$'\n'
        batch_count=$((batch_count + 1))
      fi
      cur_bid="$tok"; cur_type="$default_type"; cur_time_sec="$default_time_sec"
      cur_time_today="$default_time_today"; cur_x="1"; cur_rem="0"; cur_cmd=""
      continue
    fi
    if is_time_of_day_token "$tok"; then cur_type="time"; cur_time_today="${tok#@}"; continue; fi
    if is_time_token "$tok"; then
      local tt; tt=$(parse_interval_any "$tok")
      [ "$tt" -ge 60 ] 2>/dev/null && { cur_time_sec="$tt"; cur_type="interval"; }
      continue
    fi
    if echo "$tok" | grep -Eq '^x[0-9]+$'; then local xx="${tok#x}"; [ "$xx" -lt 1 ] && xx=1; cur_x="$xx"; continue; fi
    if echo "$tok" | grep -Eq '^r[0-9]+$'; then local rr="${tok#r}"; [ "$rr" -lt 0 ] && rr=0; cur_rem="$rr"; continue; fi
    if [ -n "$cur_cmd" ]; then
      result="${result}${cur_bid}|${cur_time_sec}|${cur_x}|${cur_cmd}|${cur_type}|${cur_time_today}|${cur_rem}"$'\n'
      batch_count=$((batch_count + 1))
      cur_bid="b$((batch_count + 1))"; cur_type="$default_type"
      cur_time_sec="$default_time_sec"; cur_time_today="$default_time_today"
      cur_x="1"; cur_rem="0"
    fi
    cur_cmd="$tok"
  done
  if [ -n "$cur_cmd" ]; then
    result="${result}${cur_bid}|${cur_time_sec}|${cur_x}|${cur_cmd}|${cur_type}|${cur_time_today}|${cur_rem}"$'\n'
    batch_count=$((batch_count + 1))
  fi
  [ "$batch_count" -eq 0 ] && return 1
  printf '%s' "$result"
}

format_batch_line() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2" type ival tval x rem cmd line tl
  type=$(get_batch_type "$id" "$bid")
  ival=$(get_batch_interval "$id" "$bid")
  tval=$(get_batch_time "$id" "$bid")
  x=$(get_batch_xcount "$id" "$bid")
  rem=$(get_batch_remaining "$id" "$bid")
  cmd=$(get_batch_command "$id" "$bid")
  tl=$(format_time_left "$(get_batch_target "$id" "$bid")")
  if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then line="  ${bid}: 🕐 ${tval} x${x} → ${cmd}"
  else line="  ${bid}: ⏱ $(format_seconds "$ival") x${x} → ${cmd}"; fi
  [ "$rem" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null && line="${line} · ${rem}$(T cmds_left)"
  line="${line}  ⏳ ${tl}"
  printf '%s' "$line"
}

# ==============================================================================
# AUTOSTART
# ==============================================================================
is_autostart_enabled() { local v; v=$(cat "$AUTOSTART_ENABLED_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n'); [ "$v" = "1" ]; }
get_autostart_interval() {
  local v; v=$(cat "$AUTOSTART_INTERVAL_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
  echo "$v" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || v=3600
  [ "$v" -lt 60 ] && v=60; echo "$v"
}
set_autostart_enabled() { printf '%s\n' "$1" > "$AUTOSTART_ENABLED_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }
set_autostart_interval() { printf '%s\n' "$1" > "$AUTOSTART_INTERVAL_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }
mark_pause_ts() { date +%s > "$AUTOSTART_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }
clear_pause_ts() { rm -f "$AUTOSTART_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }

check_autostart_resume() {
  is_farm_paused || return 0
  is_autostart_enabled || return 0
  local ts interval now
  ts=$(cat "$AUTOSTART_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
  if [ -z "$ts" ]; then mark_pause_ts; return 0; fi
  [ "$ts" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null || return 0
  interval=$(get_autostart_interval); now=$(date +%s)
  if [ $((now - ts)) -ge "$interval" ]; then
    rm -f "$PAUSE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; clear_pause_ts
    log "⏰ AUTOSTART" "Farm auto-resumed after $((now - ts))s pause."
    tg_send "$(T autostart_fired)\n$(T resumed_msg)"
  fi
}

# ==============================================================================
# QUEUE
# ==============================================================================
get_queue() {
  local q="" out="" id="" new_q="" cur_q=""
  if [ -f "$QUEUE_FILE" ]; then
    read -r q < "$QUEUE_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    q="${q//$'\r'/}"
  fi
  out=""
  for id in $q; do
    if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ]; then
      case " $out " in *" $id "*) ;; *) out="$out $id" ;; esac
    fi
  done
  for id in $(known_bot_ids); do
    case " $out " in *" $id "*) ;; *) out="$out $id" ;; esac
  done
  out="${out# }"
  new_q="$out"
  cur_q="$q"; cur_q="${cur_q# }"
  if [ "$cur_q" != "$new_q" ]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$new_q" > "$QUEUE_FILE.tmp.$$" 2>/dev/null
    mv -f "$QUEUE_FILE.tmp.$$" "$QUEUE_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  fi
  echo "$out"
}

move_bot_in_queue() {
  local id="$1" dir="$2" -a q
  read -r -a q <<< "$(get_queue)"
  local i idx=-1 found=0
  for ((i=0; i<${#q[@]}; i++)); do
    if [ "${q[i]}" = "$id" ]; then idx=$i; found=1; fi
  done
  [ "$found" -eq 0 ] && return 0
  if [ "$dir" = "up" ]; then
    if [ "$idx" -gt 0 ]; then local tmp="${q[idx-1]}"; q[idx-1]="${q[idx]}"; q[idx]="$tmp"; fi
  else
    if [ "$idx" -lt $(( ${#q[@]} - 1 )) ]; then local tmp="${q[idx+1]}"; q[idx+1]="${q[idx]}"; q[idx]="$tmp"; fi
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "${q[*]}" > "$QUEUE_FILE.tmp.$$" 2>/dev/null
  mv -f "$QUEUE_FILE.tmp.$$" "$QUEUE_FILE" 2>/dev/null
}

get_selected_timer_bot() {
  local v; v=$(cat "$SELECTED_TIMER_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  if [ -n "$v" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$v" ]; then echo "$v"; return 0; fi
  get_queue | awk '{print $1}'
}

set_selected_timer_bot() { [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$1" ] && echo "$1" > "$SELECTED_TIMER_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }

# ==============================================================================
# BOT INITIALISATION
# ==============================================================================
init_one_stock_bot() {
  local id="$1" name="$2" icon="$3" domain="$4" cmd="$5" interval="$6" upg="$7"
  [ -f "${STOCK_REMOVED_PREFIX}${id}.txt" ] && return 0
  if [ ! -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/domain.txt" ]; then
    mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$id" 2>/dev/null
    write_bot_file "$id" "$name" name
    write_bot_file "$id" "$icon" icon
    write_bot_file "$id" "$domain" domain
    write_bot_file "$id" "$cmd" cmd
    if [ "$id" = "1" ] && [ -f "$INTERVAL_FILE_1" ]; then
      cat "$INTERVAL_FILE_1" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n1 > "$BOT_DIR/1/interval.txt"
      [ ! -s "$BOT_DIR/1/interval.txt" ] && write_bot_file "$id" "$interval" interval
    else
      [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/interval.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "$interval" interval
    fi
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/upgradeable.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "$upg" upgradeable
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/enabled.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "1" enabled
    write_bot_file "$id" "1" stock
    local bint; bint=$(get_bot_interval "$id" 2>/dev/null); [ -z "$bint" ] && bint="$interval"
    printf 'b1|%s|1|%s|interval||0\n' "$bint" "$cmd" > "$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt"
  else
    write_bot_file "$id" "1" stock
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/icon.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "$icon" icon
    local cur; cur=$(read_bot_file "$id" name)
    if [ -n "$cur" ] && [ "${cur#"$icon"}" != "$cur" ]; then
      cur=$(trim14 "${cur#"$icon"}"); [ -n "$cur" ] && write_bot_file "$id" "$cur" name
    fi
    [ -s "$BOT_DIR/$id/name.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "$name" name
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/interval.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "$interval" interval
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/upgradeable.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "$upg" upgradeable
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/enabled.txt" ] || write_bot_file "$id" "1" enabled
  fi
  migrate_bot_to_batches "$id"
}

init_bot_registry() {
  mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR" "$VERSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  init_one_stock_bot 1 "Телефоны" "📱" "phonegetcardsbot" "/tcard" 9030 1
  init_one_stock_bot 2 "Компьютеры" "💻" "createpcpartsbot" "/part" 10830 0
  init_one_stock_bot 3 "Кролики" "🐰" "cabbitget_bot" "Крол" 3630 0
  local id
  for id in $(known_bot_ids); do
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/enabled.txt" ] || echo 1 > "$BOT_DIR/$id/enabled.txt"
    [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/icon.txt" ] || echo "🤖" > "$BOT_DIR/$id/icon.txt"
    migrate_bot_to_batches "$id"
  done
  get_queue >/dev/null 2>&1
}

# ==============================================================================
# PENDING ACTIONS
# ==============================================================================
set_pending_action() {
  printf '%s\n' "$2" > "${PENDING_ACTION_PREFIX}$1.txt" 2>/dev/null
  printf '%s\n' "$3" > "${PENDING_VALUE_PREFIX}$1.txt" 2>/dev/null
}
clear_pending_action() { rm -f "${PENDING_ACTION_PREFIX}$1.txt" "${PENDING_VALUE_PREFIX}$1.txt" 2>/dev/null; }

apply_bot_name() {
  local id="$1" val="$2" icon name
  val=$(trim14 "$val"); read -r icon name <<< "$val"; name=$(trim14 "$name")
  if [ -z "$icon" ] || [ -z "$name" ]; then tg_send "$(T format_emoji_name)"; return 1; fi
  write_bot_file "$id" "$icon" icon
  write_bot_file "$id" "$name" name
  tg_send "$(T bot_updated) $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id")"
}

apply_bot_cmd() {
  local id="$1" val="$2" first
  val=$(trim14 "$val")
  if [ -z "$val" ]; then tg_send "$(T empty_command)"; return 1; fi
  first=$(get_bot_batches "$id" | head -n1); [ -z "$first" ] && first="b1"
  [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt" ] && update_batch_field "$id" "$first" 4 "$val"
  write_bot_file "$id" "$val" cmd
  tg_send "$(T command_changed) $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id") → $val"
}

apply_bot_user() {
  local id="$1" val="$2" new other
  val=$(trim14 "$val"); new="${val#@}"
  if [ -z "$new" ]; then tg_send "$(T specify_username)"; return 1; fi
  for other in $(known_bot_ids); do
    [ "$other" = "$id" ] && continue
    if [ "$(get_bot_domain "$other")" = "$new" ]; then tg_send "$(T username_in_use) $other."; return 1; fi
  done
  write_bot_file "$id" "$new" domain
  tg_send "$(T username_changed) @$new"
}

apply_bot_icon() {
  local id="$1" val="$2" icon
  val=$(trim14 "$val"); read -r icon _ <<< "$val"
  if [ -z "$icon" ]; then tg_send "$(T specify_emoji)"; return 1; fi
  write_bot_file "$id" "$icon" icon
  tg_send "$(T emoji_updated)"
}

_apply_interval() {
  local id="$1" val="$2" first
  first=$(get_bot_batches "$id" | head -n1); [ -z "$first" ] && first="b1"
  [ -f "$BOT_DIR/$id/batches.txt" ] && update_batch_field "$id" "$first" 2 "$val"
  write_bot_file "$id" "$val" interval
  [ "$id" = "1" ] && echo "$val" > "$INTERVAL_FILE_1" 2>/dev/null
  set_batch_target "$id" "$first" $(( $(date +%s) + val ))
  set_selected_timer_bot "$id"
  tg_send "$(T new_interval_set) $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id"): $(format_seconds "$val")."
}

delete_bot() {
  local id="$1" nm
  [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] || return 1
  nm=$(get_bot_name "$id")
  if is_stock_bot "$id"; then touch "${STOCK_REMOVED_PREFIX}${id}.txt" 2>/dev/null; fi
  rm -rf "$BOT_DIR/$id"
  rm -f "$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}_"*.txt 2>/dev/null
  rm -f "$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}.txt" 2>/dev/null
  rm -f "${PENDING_ACTION_PREFIX}${id}.txt" "${PENDING_VALUE_PREFIX}${id}.txt" 2>/dev/null
  if [ "$(cat "$SELECTED_TIMER_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')" = "$id" ]; then rm -f "$SELECTED_TIMER_FILE" 2>/dev/null; fi
  get_queue >/dev/null 2>&1
  log "🗑 DEL" "Bot deleted: $nm (ID $id)"
  tg_send "$(T bot_deleted_msg) $nm (ID $id)"
}

execute_bot_action() {
  local id="$1" action="$2" val="$3" state
  case "$action" in
    delete) delete_bot "$id" ;;
    name) apply_bot_name "$id" "$val" ;;
    cmd) apply_bot_cmd "$id" "$val" ;;
    user) apply_bot_user "$id" "$val" ;;
    icon) apply_bot_icon "$id" "$val" ;;
    interval) _apply_interval "$id" "$val" ;;
    upgrade)
      toggle_upgradeable "$id"
      if is_bot_upgradeable "$id"; then state="$(T allowed)"; else state="$(T forbidden)"; fi
      tg_send "$(T interval_change_now) $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id") → $state." ;;
    *) tg_send "$(T unknown_action)" ;;
  esac
}

send_stock_confirm() {
  local id="$1" action="$2" word km
  if [ "$action" = "delete" ]; then word="$(T confirm_stock_delete)"; else word="$(T confirm_stock_change)"; fi
  km=$(printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"pcy_%s"},{"text":%s,"callback_data":"pcn_%s"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"bcfg_%s"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_yes)")" "$id" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_no)")" "$id" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")" "$id")
  tg_send_with_km "$word\n\n$(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id") (ID $id)" "$km"
}

request_or_do_bot_action() {
  local id="$1" action="$2" val="$3"
  [ ! -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] && { tg_send "$(T bot_not_found)"; return 1; }
  if is_stock_bot "$id"; then
    set_pending_action "$id" "$action" "$val"; send_stock_confirm "$id" "$action"
  else execute_bot_action "$id" "$action" "$val"; fi
}

confirm_or_toggle_upgrade() {
  local id="$1" state
  [ ! -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] && return 1
  if is_stock_bot "$id"; then set_pending_action "$id" "upgrade" ""; send_stock_confirm "$id" "upgrade"; return 0; fi
  toggle_upgradeable "$id"
  if is_bot_upgradeable "$id"; then state="$(T allowed)"; else state="$(T forbidden)"; fi
  tg_send "$(T interval_change_now) $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id") → $state."
}

# ==============================================================================
# INPUT REQUESTS
# ==============================================================================
request_bot_input() {
  local id="$1" action="$2" prompt="" km
  [ ! -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] && return 1
  case "$action" in
    name) prompt="$(T input_prompt_name)" ;;
    cmd) prompt="$(T input_prompt_cmd)" ;;
    user) prompt="$(T input_prompt_user)" ;;
    interval) prompt="$(T input_prompt_interval)" ;;
    delay) prompt="$(T input_prompt_delay)" ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
  set_single_input "$id:$action"
  km=$(printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"bcfg_%s"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_bot)")" "$id")
  tg_send_with_km "$prompt\n\n$(T input_active)" "$km"
}

request_autostart_input() {
  set_single_input "sys:autostart"
  local km
  km=$(printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"menu_autostart"}]]}' "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")")
  tg_send_with_km "$(T input_prompt_autostart)\n\n$(T input_active)" "$km"
}

handle_system_input() {
  local action="$1" val="$2" new
  case "$action" in
    autostart)
      new=$(parse_interval_any "$val")
      if [ "$new" -lt 60 ]; then log_error E29; tg_send "$(T invalid_interval_examples)"; return 1; fi
      set_autostart_interval "$new"
      tg_send "$(T autostart_interval_set) $(format_seconds "$new")."
      tg_autostart_menu ;;
    *) tg_send "$(T unknown_action)" ;;
  esac
}

handle_bot_input() {
  local id="$1" action="$2" val="$3" new now first
  [ ! -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] && { tg_send "$(T bot_not_found)"; return 1; }
  case "$action" in
    name) apply_bot_name "$id" "$val" ;;
    cmd) apply_bot_cmd "$id" "$val" ;;
    user) apply_bot_user "$id" "$val" ;;
    interval) set_interval_for_bot "$id" "$val" ;;
    delay)
      new=$(parse_interval_any "$val")
      if [ "$new" -lt 60 ]; then tg_send "$(T invalid_interval_examples)"; return 1; fi
      first=$(get_bot_batches "$id" | head -n1); [ -z "$first" ] && first="b1"
      now=$(date +%s)
      set_batch_target "$id" "$first" $((now + new))
      set_selected_timer_bot "$id"
      tg_send "⏳ $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id"): $(T delayed_by) $(format_seconds "$new") $(T one_cycle)" ;;
    *) tg_send "$(T unknown_action)"; return 1 ;;
  esac
  tg_bot_cfg_menu "$id"
}

handle_batch_edit_input() {
  local id="$1" action="$2" bid="$3" text="$4" n
  [ ! -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] && { tg_send "$(T bot_not_found)"; return 1; }
  [ -z "$bid" ] && { tg_send "$(T bot_not_found)"; return 1; }
  case "$action" in
    cbcmd)
      local c; c=$(trim14 "$text"); [ -z "$c" ] && c="/start"
      update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 4 "$c"
      tg_send "$(T cmds_saved)"
      tg_cmds_menu "$id" ;;
    cbtime)
      if [[ "$text" = @* ]]; then
        local t="${text#@}"
        if echo "$t" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]{1,2}:[0-9]{2}$'; then
          local h="${t%:*}" m="${t#*:}"
          h=$((10#$h)); m=$((10#$m))
          if [ "$h" -ge 0 ] && [ "$h" -le 23 ] && [ "$m" -ge 0 ] && [ "$m" -le 59 ]; then
            t=$(printf "%02d:%02d" "$h" "$m")
            update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 5 "time"
            update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 6 "$t"
            update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 2 "0"
            rm -f "$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}_${bid}.txt" 2>/dev/null
            get_batch_target "$id" "$bid" >/dev/null 2>&1
            tg_send "$(T cmds_saved)"
          else
            tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"
          fi
        else
          tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"
        fi
      else
        n=$(parse_interval_any "$text")
        if [ "$n" -ge 60 ]; then
          update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 2 "$n"
          update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 5 "interval"
          update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 6 ""
          rm -f "$TIMER_DIR/target_${id}_${bid}.txt" 2>/dev/null
          get_batch_target "$id" "$bid" >/dev/null 2>&1
          tg_send "$(T cmds_saved)"
        else tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; fi
      fi
      tg_cmds_menu "$id" ;;
    cbx)
      n=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n'); n="${n#x}"; n="${n#X}"
      if echo "$n" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && [ "$n" -ge 1 ]; then
        update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 3 "$n"; tg_send "$(T cmds_saved)"
      else tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; fi
      tg_cmds_menu "$id" ;;
    cbrem)
      n=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n'); n="${n#r}"; n="${n#R}"
      if echo "$n" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
        update_batch_field "$id" "$bid" 7 "$n"; tg_send "$(T cmds_saved)"
      else tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; fi
      tg_cmds_menu "$id" ;;
    *) tg_send "$(T unknown_action)" ;;
  esac
}

set_interval_for_bot() {
  local id="$1" raw="$2" new
  new=$(parse_interval_any "$raw")
  if [ "$new" -lt 60 ]; then tg_send "$(T invalid_interval_examples)"; return 1; fi
  if ! is_bot_upgradeable "$id"; then tg_send "$(T interval_change_forbidden)"; return 1; fi
  if is_stock_bot "$id"; then
    set_pending_action "$id" "interval" "$new"; send_stock_confirm "$id" "interval"; return 0
  fi
  _apply_interval "$id" "$new"
}

# ==============================================================================
# ADD BOT (text)
# ==============================================================================
add_bot_from_text() {
  local text="$1" body="${text#* }"
  [ "$body" = "$text" ] && { tg_send "$(build_addbot_help)"; return 1; }
  local -a parts
  read -r -a parts <<< "$body"
  [ "${#parts[@]}" -lt 3 ] && { tg_send "$(build_addbot_help)"; return 1; }
  local username="${parts[0]}" upgrade=0 idx=1
  if [ "${parts[idx]}" = "0" ] || [ "${parts[idx]}" = "1" ]; then upgrade="${parts[idx]}"; idx=$((idx + 1)); fi
  local rest="" i
  for ((i=idx; i<${#parts[@]}; i++)); do rest="${rest} ${parts[i]}"; done
  rest=$(trim14 "$rest")
  [[ "$rest" != *.* ]] && { tg_send "$(build_addbot_help)"; return 1; }
  local name_part="${rest%%.*}" spec_part="${rest#*.}"
  name_part=$(trim14 "$name_part"); spec_part=$(trim14 "$spec_part")
  if [ -z "$name_part" ] || [ -z "$spec_part" ]; then tg_send "$(build_addbot_help)"; return 1; fi
  local icon name
  read -r icon name <<< "$name_part"; name=$(trim14 "$name")
  if [ -z "$icon" ] || [ -z "$name" ]; then tg_send "$(T name_format_required)"; return 1; fi
  local batches_out; batches_out=$(parse_batches_spec "$spec_part")
  [ -z "$batches_out" ] && { tg_send "$(build_addbot_help)"; return 1; }
  local domain="${username#@}" id
  for id in $(known_bot_ids); do
    if [ "$(get_bot_domain "$id")" = "$domain" ]; then tg_send "$(T bot_already_added) $id."; return 1; fi
  done
  id=$(next_bot_id)
  mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$id"
  write_bot_file "$id" "$name" name
  write_bot_file "$id" "$icon" icon
  write_bot_file "$id" "$domain" domain
  write_bot_file "$id" "$upgrade" upgradeable
  write_bot_file "$id" "1" enabled
  write_bot_file "$id" "0" stock
  set_bot_batches "$id" $batches_out
  local first_line; first_line=$(echo "$batches_out" | head -n1)
  local bint bcmd
  bint=$(echo "$first_line" | cut -d'|' -f2); bcmd=$(echo "$first_line" | cut -d'|' -f4)
  write_bot_file "$id" "$bint" interval
  write_bot_file "$id" "$bcmd" cmd
  get_queue >/dev/null 2>&1
  local upg_word
  [ "$upgrade" = "1" ] && upg_word="$(T allowed)" || upg_word="$(T forbidden)"
  local msg="$(T bot_added_title)\n\n$(T id_label) $id\n$(T username_label) @$domain\n$(get_bot_icon "$id") $(T name_label) $(get_bot_name "$id")\n$(T interval_change_label) $upg_word\n\n📦 Batches:"
  local line bid bint2 bx bcmd2 _type _time
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    [ -z "$line" ] && continue
    bid=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f1); bint2=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f2)
    bx=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f3); bcmd2=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f4)
    _type=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f5); _time=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f6)
    if [ "$_type" = "time" ]; then msg="${msg}\n  ${bid}: 🕐 ${_time} x${bx} → ${bcmd2}"
    else msg="${msg}\n  ${bid}: ⏱ $(format_seconds "$bint2") x${bx} → ${bcmd2}"; fi
  done <<< "$batches_out"
  tg_send "$msg"
}

# ==============================================================================
# ADD BOT WIZARD
# ==============================================================================
addbot_session_clear() { rm -rf "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null; }
addbot_session_reset() {
  rm -rf "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  mkdir -p "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  printf 'username' > "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/step"
  date +%s > "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/touched" 2>/dev/null
}
addbot_session_exists() { [ -f "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/step" ]; }
addbot_set() {
  printf '%s' "$2" > "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/$1" 2>/dev/null
  date +%s > "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/touched" 2>/dev/null
}
addbot_get() { cat "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/$1" 2>/dev/null || true; }

addbot_step_back_kb() {
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"addbot_back"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"addbot_cancel"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_cancel)")"
}

addbot_start() {
  addbot_session_reset
  local hint body
  hint="$(T addbot_help_hint)"
  body="$(T addbot_step_user)\n\n${hint}"
  tg_send_with_km "$body" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)"
}

addbot_prompt_current_batch_x() {
  local n step
  n=$(addbot_get cur_batch)
  step="$(T addbot_step_batch_x)"; step="${step//\{N\}/$n}"
  tg_send_with_km "${step}\n\nℹ️ /cmd" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)"
}
addbot_prompt_current_batch_time() {
  local n step
  n=$(addbot_get cur_batch)
  step="$(T addbot_step_batch_time)"; step="${step//\{N\}/$n}"
  tg_send_with_km "$step" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)"
}
addbot_prompt_current_batch_cmd() {
  local n step
  n=$(addbot_get cur_batch)
  step="$(T addbot_step_batch_cmd)"; step="${step//\{N\}/$n}"
  tg_send_with_km "$step" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)"
}

addbot_has_dup_intervals() {
  local bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  [ ! -f "$bf" ] && return 1
  local dups
  dups=$(awk -F'|' '{print $2"|"$5"|"$6}' "$bf" 2>/dev/null | sort | uniq -d | head -n1)
  [ -n "$dups" ]
}

addbot_restore_batch() {
  local bid="$1" bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  [ -f "$bf" ] || return 0
  local line t x
  line=$(grep "^${bid}|" "$bf" 2>/dev/null | head -n1)
  [ -z "$line" ] && return 0
  t=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f2); x=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f3)
  addbot_set cur_time "$t"; addbot_set cur_x "$x"
}

addbot_save_batch_line() {
  local bid="$1" bt="$2" bx="$3" cmd="$4" type="${5:-interval}" tt="${6:-}" rem="${7:-0}"
  local bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  local tmp="$bf.tmp.$$"
  [ -f "$bf" ] || : > "$bf"
  local full="${bid}|${bt}|${bx}|${cmd}|${type}|${tt}|${rem}"
  if grep -q "^${bid}|" "$bf" 2>/dev/null; then
    awk -F'|' -v b="$bid" -v line="$full" 'BEGIN{OFS="|"} $1==b {print line; next} {print}' "$bf" > "$tmp"
    mv -f "$tmp" "$bf"
  else printf '%s\n' "$full" >> "$bf"; fi
}

addbot_show_queue_menu() {
  local bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  [ ! -f "$bf" ] && return 0
  local text line bid bint bx bcmd type tt
  text="$(T addbot_dup_time)\n\n"
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    [ -z "$line" ] && continue
    bid=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f1); bint=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f2)
    bx=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f3); bcmd=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f4)
    type=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f5); tt=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f6)
    if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then text="${text}  ${bid}: 🕐 ${tt} x${bx} → ${bcmd}\n"
    else text="${text}  ${bid}: ⏱ $(format_seconds "$bint") x${bx} → ${bcmd}\n"; fi
  done < "$bf"
  local kb=""
  while IFS= read -r bid; do
    [ -z "$bid" ] && continue
    kb="${kb}[{\"text\":\"⬆️ ${bid}\",\"callback_data\":\"abq_up_${bid}\"},{\"text\":\"⬇️ ${bid}\",\"callback_data\":\"abq_dn_${bid}\"}],"
  done < <(awk -F'|' '{print $1}' "$bf" 2>/dev/null)
  kb="${kb}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_save)"),\"callback_data\":\"abq_save\"}],"
  kb="${kb}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)"),\"callback_data\":\"addbot_back\"},{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_cancel)"),\"callback_data\":\"addbot_cancel\"}]"
  tg_send_with_km "$text" "{\"inline_keyboard\":[${kb}]}"
}

addbot_show_confirm() {
  local u i n bf line bid bint bx bcmd type tt text
  u=$(addbot_get username); i=$(addbot_get icon); n=$(addbot_get name)
  bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  text="$(T addbot_confirm_title)\n\n$(T username_label) @${u#@}\n$(T name_label) $i $n\n\n📦 Batches:\n"
  if [ -f "$bf" ]; then
    while IFS= read -r line; do
      [ -z "$line" ] && continue
      bid=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f1); bint=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f2)
      bx=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f3); bcmd=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f4)
      type=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f5); tt=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f6)
      if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then text="${text}  ${bid}: 🕐 ${tt} x${bx} → ${bcmd}\n"
      else text="${text}  ${bid}: ⏱ $(format_seconds "$bint") x${bx} → ${bcmd}\n"; fi
    done < "$bf"
  fi
  tg_send_with_km "$text" "{\"inline_keyboard\":[[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_save)"),\"callback_data\":\"abq_save\"}],[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)"),\"callback_data\":\"addbot_back\"},{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_cancel)"),\"callback_data\":\"addbot_cancel\"}]]}"
}

addbot_session_move_batch() {
  local bid="$1" dir="$2" bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  [ ! -f "$bf" ] && return 0
  local -a lines
  mapfile -t lines < "$bf"
  local n=${#lines[@]} i idx=-1
  for ((i=0; i<n; i++)); do
    local b; b=$(echo "${lines[i]}" | cut -d'|' -f1)
    if [ "$b" = "$bid" ]; then idx=$i; break; fi
  done
  [ "$idx" -lt 0 ] && return 0
  local tmp
  if [ "$dir" = "up" ]; then
    if [ "$idx" -gt 0 ]; then tmp="${lines[idx-1]}"; lines[idx-1]="${lines[idx]}"; lines[idx]="$tmp"; fi
  else
    if [ "$idx" -lt $((n - 1)) ]; then tmp="${lines[idx+1]}"; lines[idx+1]="${lines[idx]}"; lines[idx]="$tmp"; fi
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "${lines[@]}" > "$bf"
}

addbot_go_back() {
  local step n
  step=$(addbot_get step)
  case "$step" in
    username) addbot_session_clear; tg_send "$(T addbot_cancelled)"; tg_menu; return 0 ;;
    name) addbot_set step username; tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_step_user)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)" ;;
    bcount) addbot_set step name; tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_step_name)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)" ;;
    batch_x)
      n=$(addbot_get cur_batch)
      if [ "$n" = "1" ]; then addbot_set step bcount; tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_step_bcount)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)"
      else
        n=$((n - 1)); addbot_set cur_batch "$n"; addbot_restore_batch "b${n}"
        addbot_set step batch_cmd; addbot_prompt_current_batch_cmd
      fi ;;
    batch_time) addbot_set step batch_x; addbot_prompt_current_batch_x ;;
    batch_cmd) addbot_set step batch_time; addbot_prompt_current_batch_time ;;
    queue|confirm)
      n=$(addbot_get bcount); [ -z "$n" ] && n=1
      addbot_set cur_batch "$n"; addbot_restore_batch "b${n}"
      addbot_set step batch_cmd; addbot_prompt_current_batch_cmd ;;
    *) addbot_session_clear; tg_menu ;;
  esac
}

handle_addbot_text_input() {
  local text="$1" step
  session_touch "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR"
  step=$(addbot_get step)
  case "$step" in
    username)
      local u id
      u=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n'); u="${u#@}"
      if [ -z "$u" ]; then tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_step_user)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)"; return 0; fi
      for id in $(known_bot_ids); do
        if [ "$(get_bot_domain "$id")" = "$u" ]; then
          tg_send "$(T bot_already_added) $id."; addbot_session_clear; return 0
        fi
      done
      addbot_set username "$u"; addbot_set step name
      tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_step_name)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)" ;;
    name)
      local val icon name
      val=$(trim14 "$text"); read -r icon name <<< "$val"; name=$(trim14 "$name")
      if [ -z "$icon" ] || [ -z "$name" ]; then tg_send "$(T name_format_required)"; return 0; fi
      addbot_set icon "$icon"; addbot_set name "$name"; addbot_set step bcount
      tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_step_bcount)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)" ;;
    bcount)
      local n
      n=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n')
      if ! echo "$n" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || [ "$n" -lt 1 ] || [ "$n" -gt 20 ]; then
        tg_send "$(T addbot_bad_bcount)"; return 0
      fi
      addbot_set bcount "$n"; addbot_set cur_batch "1"
      : > "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
      addbot_set step batch_x; addbot_prompt_current_batch_x ;;
    batch_x)
      local v
      v=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n'); v="${v#x}"; v="${v#X}"
      if ! echo "$v" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || [ "$v" -lt 1 ]; then tg_send "$(T addbot_bad_x)"; return 0; fi
      addbot_set cur_x "$v"; addbot_set step batch_time; addbot_prompt_current_batch_time ;;
    batch_time)
      local t
      t=$(parse_interval_any "$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n')")
      if [ "$t" -lt 60 ]; then tg_send "$(T invalid_interval_examples)"; return 0; fi
      addbot_set cur_time "$t"; addbot_set step batch_cmd; addbot_prompt_current_batch_cmd ;;
    batch_cmd)
      local cmd n bx bt total
      cmd=$(trim14 "$text"); [ -z "$cmd" ] && cmd="/start"
      n=$(addbot_get cur_batch); bx=$(addbot_get cur_x); [ -z "$bx" ] && bx=1
      bt=$(addbot_get cur_time); [ -z "$bt" ] && bt=$DEFAULT_BATCH_INTERVAL
      addbot_save_batch_line "b${n}" "$bt" "$bx" "$cmd" "interval" "" "0"
      total=$(addbot_get bcount); echo "$total" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || total=1
      if [ "$n" -lt "$total" ]; then
        n=$((n + 1)); addbot_set cur_batch "$n"; addbot_set step batch_x; addbot_prompt_current_batch_x
      else
        if addbot_has_dup_intervals; then addbot_set step queue; addbot_show_queue_menu
        else addbot_set step confirm; addbot_show_confirm; fi
      fi ;;
    queue) tg_send_with_km "$(T addbot_dup_time)" "$(addbot_step_back_kb)" ;;
    confirm) addbot_show_confirm ;;
    *) addbot_session_clear ;;
  esac
}

addbot_finalize() {
  local u i n bf id line bid bint2 bx bcmd2 _type _time msg
  u=$(addbot_get username); i=$(addbot_get icon); n=$(addbot_get name)
  bf="$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR/batches"
  if [ -z "$u" ] || [ -z "$i" ] || [ -z "$n" ] || [ ! -s "$bf" ]; then
    tg_send "$(T addbot_cancelled)"; addbot_session_clear; return 1
  fi
  for id in $(known_bot_ids); do
    if [ "$(get_bot_domain "$id")" = "$u" ]; then tg_send "$(T bot_already_added) $id."; addbot_session_clear; return 1; fi
  done
  id=$(next_bot_id)
  mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$id"
  write_bot_file "$id" "$n" name
  write_bot_file "$id" "$i" icon
  write_bot_file "$id" "$u" domain
  write_bot_file "$id" "1" upgradeable
  write_bot_file "$id" "1" enabled
  write_bot_file "$id" "0" stock
  local -a lines
  mapfile -t lines < "$bf"
  set_bot_batches "$id" "${lines[@]}"
  local first_line="${lines[0]}" bint bcmd
  bint=$(echo "$first_line" | cut -d'|' -f2); bcmd=$(echo "$first_line" | cut -d'|' -f4)
  write_bot_file "$id" "$bint" interval
  write_bot_file "$id" "$bcmd" cmd
  get_queue >/dev/null 2>&1
  msg="$(T bot_added_title)\n\n$(T id_label) $id\n$(T username_label) @$u\n$(get_bot_icon "$id") $(T name_label) $(get_bot_name "$id")\n\n📦 Batches:"
  for line in "${lines[@]}"; do
    [ -z "$line" ] && continue
    bid=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f1); bint2=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f2)
    bx=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f3); bcmd2=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f4)
    _type=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f5); _time=$(echo "$line" | cut -d'|' -f6)
    if [ "$_type" = "time" ]; then msg="${msg}\n  ${bid}: 🕐 ${_time} x${bx} → ${bcmd2}"
    else msg="${msg}\n  ${bid}: ⏱ $(format_seconds "$bint2") x${bx} → ${bcmd2}"; fi
  done
  tg_send "$msg"
  addbot_session_clear
}

# ==============================================================================
# BOT COMMANDS WIZARD
# ==============================================================================
addcmd_session_reset() {
  rm -rf "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  mkdir -p "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  date +%s > "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR/touched" 2>/dev/null
}
addcmd_session_exists() { [ -f "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR/step" ]; }
addcmd_set() {
  printf '%s' "$2" > "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR/$1" 2>/dev/null
  date +%s > "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR/touched" 2>/dev/null
}
addcmd_get() { cat "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR/$1" 2>/dev/null || true; }

addcmd_start() {
  local id="$1"
  addcmd_session_reset
  addcmd_set bot "$id"
  addcmd_set step type
  tg_send_with_km "$(T cmds_title)\n\n$(T cmds_type_title)" "$(build_addcmd_type_kb "$id")"
}

build_addcmd_type_kb() {
  local id="$1"
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_t_int"},{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_t_time"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_t_xt"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"bcmds_%s"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T cmds_type_interval)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T cmds_type_time)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T cmds_type_xtimes)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")" "$id"
}

build_addcmd_subtype_kb() {
  local id="$1"
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_s_int"},{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_s_time"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"bcmds_%s"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T cmds_type_interval)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T cmds_type_time)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")" "$id"
}

addcmd_build_kb_back() {
  local id="$1" cb="$2"
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"%s_%s"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")" "$cb" "$id"
}

handle_addcmd_callback() {
  local cb="$1" msg_id="$2" id
  case "$cb" in
    acd_t_int)
      id=$(addcmd_get bot)
      addcmd_set isxt "0"
      addcmd_set step value; addcmd_set type "interval"
      tg_edit_text_km "$msg_id" "$(T cmds_interval_prompt)" "$(addcmd_build_kb_back "$id" bcmds)" ;;
    acd_t_time)
      id=$(addcmd_get bot)
      addcmd_set isxt "0"
      addcmd_set step value; addcmd_set type "time"
      tg_edit_text_km "$msg_id" "$(T cmds_time_prompt)" "$(addcmd_build_kb_back "$id" bcmds)" ;;
    acd_t_xt)
      id=$(addcmd_get bot)
      addcmd_set isxt "1"; addcmd_set step subtype
      tg_edit_text_km "$msg_id" "$(T cmds_subtype_prompt)" "$(build_addcmd_subtype_kb "$id")" ;;
    acd_s_int)
      id=$(addcmd_get bot)
      addcmd_set isxt "1"
      addcmd_set type "interval"; addcmd_set step value
      tg_edit_text_km "$msg_id" "$(T cmds_interval_prompt)" "$(addcmd_build_kb_back "$id" bcmds)" ;;
    acd_s_time)
      id=$(addcmd_get bot)
      addcmd_set isxt "1"
      addcmd_set type "time"; addcmd_set step value
      tg_edit_text_km "$msg_id" "$(T cmds_time_prompt)" "$(addcmd_build_kb_back "$id" bcmds)" ;;
    acd_save) addcmd_save_and_close "$msg_id" ;;
    acd_cancel)
      id=$(addcmd_get bot); addcmd_session_reset
      if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ]; then tg_edit_cmds_menu "$msg_id" "$id"; fi ;;
  esac
}

addcmd_save_and_close() {
  local msg_id="$1" id type time_val xcount rem cmd ival bid
  id=$(addcmd_get bot)
  [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$id" ] || return 1
  type=$(addcmd_get type); [ -z "$type" ] && type="interval"
  time_val=$(addcmd_get time_val)
  ival=$(addcmd_get ival); [ -z "$ival" ] && ival=$DEFAULT_BATCH_INTERVAL
  xcount=$(addcmd_get xcount); [ -z "$xcount" ] && xcount=1
  rem=$(addcmd_get rem); [ -z "$rem" ] && rem=0
  cmd=$(addcmd_get cmd); [ -z "$cmd" ] && cmd="/start"
  bid=$(get_next_batch_id "$id")
  add_batch_line "$id" "${bid}|${ival}|${xcount}|${cmd}|${type}|${time_val}|${rem}"
  get_batch_target "$id" "$bid" >/dev/null 2>&1
  addcmd_session_reset
  tg_send "$(T cmds_saved)"
  tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$msg_id" "$id"
}

handle_addcmd_text() {
  local text="$1" step id
  session_touch "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR"
  step=$(addcmd_get step); id=$(addcmd_get bot)
  case "$step" in
    value)
      local type
      type=$(addcmd_get type)
      if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then
        local t="${text#@}"
        if ! echo "$t" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]{1,2}:[0-9]{2}$'; then tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; return 0; fi
        local h="${t%:*}" m="${t#*:}"
        h=$((10#$h)); m=$((10#$m))
        if [ "$h" -lt 0 ] || [ "$h" -gt 23 ] || [ "$m" -lt 0 ] || [ "$m" -gt 59 ]; then tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; return 0; fi
        t=$(printf "%02d:%02d" "$h" "$m")
        addcmd_set time_val "$t"; addcmd_set ival "0"
      else
        local t; t=$(parse_interval_any "$text")
        if [ "$t" -lt 60 ]; then tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; return 0; fi
        addcmd_set ival "$t"; addcmd_set time_val ""
      fi
      addcmd_set step xcount
      tg_send "$(T cmds_xcount_prompt)" ;;
    xcount)
      local n
      n=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n'); n="${n#x}"; n="${n#X}"; [ -z "$n" ] && n=1
      if ! echo "$n" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || [ "$n" -lt 1 ]; then tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; return 0; fi
      addcmd_set xcount "$n"
      if [ "$(addcmd_get isxt)" = "1" ]; then addcmd_set step remaining; tg_send "$(T cmds_remaining_prompt)"
      else addcmd_set rem "0"; addcmd_set step cmd; tg_send "$(T cmds_cmd_prompt)"; fi ;;
    remaining)
      local n
      n=$(printf '%s' "$text" | tr -d ' \r\n'); n="${n#r}"; n="${n#R}"; [ -z "$n" ] && n=0
      if ! echo "$n" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then tg_send "$(T cmds_invalid)"; return 0; fi
      addcmd_set rem "$n"; addcmd_set step cmd; tg_send "$(T cmds_cmd_prompt)" ;;
    cmd)
      local cmd type time_val xcount rem summary km
      cmd=$(trim14 "$text"); [ -z "$cmd" ] && cmd="/start"
      addcmd_set cmd "$cmd"
      type=$(addcmd_get type); time_val=$(addcmd_get time_val)
      xcount=$(addcmd_get xcount); rem=$(addcmd_get rem)
      if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then summary="🕐 ${time_val} x${xcount} → ${cmd}"
      else summary="⏱ $(format_seconds "$(addcmd_get ival)") x${xcount} → ${cmd}"; fi
      [ "$rem" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null && summary="${summary} · ${rem}$(T cmds_left)"
      addcmd_set step confirm
      km=$(printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_save"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_cancel"}]]}' \
        "$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_save)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_cancel)")")
      tg_send_with_km "${summary}" "$km" ;;
    confirm)
      local cmd type time_val xcount rem summary km
      cmd=$(addcmd_get cmd); type=$(addcmd_get type)
      time_val=$(addcmd_get time_val); xcount=$(addcmd_get xcount); rem=$(addcmd_get rem)
      if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then summary="🕐 ${time_val} x${xcount} → ${cmd}"
      else summary="⏱ $(format_seconds "$(addcmd_get ival)") x${xcount} → ${cmd}"; fi
      [ "$rem" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null && summary="${summary} · ${rem}$(T cmds_left)"
      km=$(printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_save"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"acd_cancel"}]]}' \
        "$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_save)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_addbot_cancel)")")
      tg_send_with_km "ℹ️ ${summary}\n\n$(T input_active)" "$km" ;;
    *) addcmd_session_reset ;;
  esac
}

build_cmds_menu_text() {
  local id="$1" bid text any=0
  text="$(T cmds_title): $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id")\n\n"
  for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$id"); do
    any=1; text="${text}$(format_batch_line "$id" "$bid")\n"
  done
  [ "$any" = "0" ] && text="${text}$(T cmds_empty)\n"
  printf '%s' "$text"
}

build_cmds_menu_keyboard() {
  local id="$1" bid
  start_kb
  for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$id"); do
    add_kb_btn "✏️ $bid" "cedit_${id}_${bid}" 2
  done
  add_kb_btn "$(T cmds_add)" "cadd_$id" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_bot)" "bcfg_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 2
  finish_kb
}

tg_cmds_menu() {
  local id="$1"
  echo "$id" > "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_send_with_km "$(build_cmds_menu_text "$id")" "$(build_cmds_menu_keyboard "$id")"
}
tg_edit_cmds_menu() {
  local mid="$1" id="$2"
  echo "$id" > "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_edit_text_km "$mid" "$(build_cmds_menu_text "$id")" "$(build_cmds_menu_keyboard "$id")"
}

build_cbatch_text() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2" type ival tval x rem cmd
  type=$(get_batch_type "$id" "$bid")
  ival=$(get_batch_interval "$id" "$bid")
  tval=$(get_batch_time "$id" "$bid")
  x=$(get_batch_xcount "$id" "$bid")
  rem=$(get_batch_remaining "$id" "$bid")
  cmd=$(get_batch_command "$id" "$bid")
  printf '%s %s\n' "$(T cmds_edit_title)" "$bid"
  if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then printf '🕐 %s\n' "$tval"
  else printf '⏱ %s\n' "$(format_seconds "$ival")"; fi
  printf '🔁 x%s\n' "$x"
  printf '📝 %s\n' "$cmd"
  [ "$rem" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null && printf '🔄 %s %s\n' "$rem" "$(T cmds_left)"
}

build_cbatch_keyboard() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2"
  start_kb
  add_kb_btn "$(T cmds_change_cmd)" "cbe_cmd_${id}_${bid}" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T cmds_change_time)" "cbe_time_${id}_${bid}" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T cmds_change_xcount)" "cbe_x_${id}_${bid}" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T cmds_change_remaining)" "cbe_rem_${id}_${bid}" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_del)" "cbe_del_${id}_${bid}" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back)" "bcmds_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 2
  finish_kb
}

tg_cbatch_menu() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2"
  tg_send_with_km "$(build_cbatch_text "$id" "$bid")" "$(build_cbatch_keyboard "$id" "$bid")"
}
tg_edit_cbatch_menu() {
  local mid="$1" id="$2" bid="$3"
  tg_edit_text_km "$mid" "$(build_cbatch_text "$id" "$bid")" "$(build_cbatch_keyboard "$id" "$bid")"
}

# ==============================================================================
# BATTERY
# ==============================================================================
try_battery_nonroot() {
  local bat val f
  for f in /sys/class/power_supply/*/capacity; do
    if [ -f "$f" ]; then
      val=$(cat "$f" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
      echo "$val" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && { echo "$val"; return 0; }
    fi
  done
  bat=$(dumpsys battery 2>/dev/null | grep -i "level:" | head -n 1 | grep -o "[0-9]*" | head -n 1 | tr -d ' \r\n')
  echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && { echo "$bat"; return 0; }
  return 1
}

try_charging_nonroot() {
  local f val out
  for f in /sys/class/power_supply/*/status; do
    if [ -f "$f" ]; then
      val=$(cat "$f" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
      case "$val" in Charging|Full) echo "1"; return 0 ;; esac
    fi
  done
  for f in /sys/class/power_supply/*/online; do
    if [ -f "$f" ]; then
      val=$(cat "$f" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
      [ "$val" = "1" ] && { echo "1"; return 0; }
    fi
  done
  out=$(dumpsys battery 2>/dev/null)
  if [ -n "$out" ]; then
    if echo "$out" | grep -Eiq 'AC powered: true|USB powered: true|Wireless powered: true|status: (2|5)'; then echo "1"
    else echo "0"; fi
    return 0
  fi
  return 1
}

parse_root_power_tmp() {
  local out bat ch now
  [ -f "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" ] || return 0
  out=$(cat "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null); rm -f "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  [ -z "$out" ] && return 0
  now=$(date +%s)
  bat=$(echo "$out" | grep -i "level:" | head -n 1 | grep -o "[0-9]*" | head -n 1 | tr -d ' \r\n')
  if echo "$out" | grep -Eiq 'AC powered: true|USB powered: true|Wireless powered: true|status: (2|5)'; then ch=1; else ch=0; fi
  if echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
    echo "$bat" > "$BAT_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CACHE_TS_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_ROOT_TS_FILE"
  fi
  echo "$ch" > "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CHARGE_TS_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$POWER_FORCE_GUARD"
}

update_root_power_cache() {
  local now
  rm -f "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then
    if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then timeout 5 dumpsys battery > "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null
    else dumpsys battery > "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null; fi
  else
    if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then timeout 6 su -c "dumpsys battery > $BAT_ROOT_TMP 2>/dev/null" 2>/dev/null
    else su -c "dumpsys battery > $BAT_ROOT_TMP 2>/dev/null" 2>/dev/null; fi
  fi
  parse_root_power_tmp
  now=$(date +%s); echo "$now" > "$POWER_FORCE_GUARD" 2>/dev/null
}

ensure_power_force() {
  local now guard_ts bat ch
  now=$(date +%s)
  guard_ts=$(cat "$POWER_FORCE_GUARD" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$guard_ts" ] && guard_ts=0
  [ $((now - guard_ts)) -lt "$POWER_FORCE_GUARD_TTL" ] && return 0
  update_root_power_cache
  bat=$(cat "$BAT_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  ch=$(cat "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  if echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && { [ "$ch" = "0" ] || [ "$ch" = "1" ]; }; then return 0; fi
  bat=$(try_battery_nonroot); ch=$(try_charging_nonroot)
  if echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then echo "$bat" > "$BAT_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CACHE_TS_FILE"; fi
  if [ "$ch" = "0" ] || [ "$ch" = "1" ]; then echo "$ch" > "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CHARGE_TS_FILE"; fi
  echo "$now" > "$POWER_FORCE_GUARD"
}

get_battery_level() {
  local now cached_val cached_ts bat
  now=$(date +%s)
  cached_val=$(cat "$BAT_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  cached_ts=$(cat "$BAT_CACHE_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$cached_ts" ] && cached_ts=0
  if [ -n "$cached_val" ] && [ "$cached_ts" -gt 0 ] && [ $((now - cached_ts)) -lt "$BAT_CACHE_TTL" ]; then echo "$cached_val"; return 0; fi
  bat=$(try_battery_nonroot)
  if echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
    echo "$bat" > "$BAT_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CACHE_TS_FILE"; echo "$bat"; return 0
  fi
  if echo "$cached_val" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then echo "$now" > "$BAT_CACHE_TS_FILE"; echo "$cached_val"
  else echo "unknown" > "$BAT_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CACHE_TS_FILE"; echo "unknown"; fi
}

get_charging_status() {
  local now cached_val cached_ts val
  now=$(date +%s)
  cached_val=$(cat "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  cached_ts=$(cat "$BAT_CHARGE_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$cached_ts" ] && cached_ts=0
  if { [ "$cached_val" = "0" ] || [ "$cached_val" = "1" ]; } && [ "$cached_ts" -gt 0 ] && [ $((now - cached_ts)) -lt "$BAT_CACHE_TTL" ]; then echo "$cached_val"; return 0; fi
  val=$(try_charging_nonroot)
  if [ "$val" = "0" ] || [ "$val" = "1" ]; then echo "$val" > "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CHARGE_TS_FILE"; echo "$val"; return 0; fi
  if [ "$cached_val" = "0" ] || [ "$cached_val" = "1" ]; then echo "$now" > "$BAT_CHARGE_TS_FILE"; echo "$cached_val"
  else echo "0" > "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE"; echo "$now" > "$BAT_CHARGE_TS_FILE"; echo "0"; fi
}

get_battery_level_force() {
  ensure_power_force
  local val; val=$(cat "$BAT_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  echo "$val" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && echo "$val" || echo "unknown"
}

get_charging_status_force() {
  ensure_power_force
  local val; val=$(cat "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  [ "$val" = "0" ] || [ "$val" = "1" ] && echo "$val" || echo "0"
}

# ==============================================================================
# LOGGING
# ==============================================================================
log() {
  local tag="$1" msg="$2" cur_ts cur_time log_line
  if [ -z "$msg" ]; then msg="$tag"; tag="⚙️ INFO"; fi
  tag="${tag//[$'\n\r\t']/   }"
  msg="${msg//[$'\n\r\t']/   }"
  cur_ts=${EPOCHSECONDS:-$(date +%s)}
  printf -v cur_time '%(%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S)T' "$cur_ts" 2>/dev/null || cur_time=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
  log_line="${cur_time} [${tag}] ${msg}"
  printf '%s\n' "$log_line" >> "$LOG_FILE"
  if [ -w /dev/tty ]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$log_line" > /dev/tty 2>/dev/null || echo "$log_line"
  else
    echo "$log_line"
  fi
}

log_error() {
  local code="$1" extra="${2:-}" what; what=$(E "$code" what)
  if [ -n "$extra" ]; then log "🆘 ${code}" "$what — $extra"; else log "🆘 ${code}" "$what"; fi
  error_block_log "$code" "$extra" >> "$CRASH_LOG" 2>/dev/null
}

log_collection() {
  printf '%s | %s | @%s | %s | %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$1" "$2" "$3" "${4:-}" >> "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY"
}

# ==============================================================================
# MACRO REPORTS
# ==============================================================================
macro_batch_outcome() {
  local domain="$1" bid="$2" ok_line attempt
  ok_line=$(grep -E "BATCH_SENT(_AFTER_RETRY)? .*domain=@${domain} .*batch=${bid}( |$)" "$MACRO_LOG" 2>/dev/null | tail -n 1)
  if [ -n "$ok_line" ]; then
    attempt=$(echo "$ok_line" | grep -oE 'attempt=[0-9]+' | head -n1 | cut -d= -f2); [ -z "$attempt" ] && attempt=1
    if [ "$attempt" -gt 1 ]; then echo "$attempt"; return 1; fi
    echo "1"; return 0
  fi
  grep -qE "BATCH_FAILED .*domain=@${domain} .*batch=${bid}( |$)" "$MACRO_LOG" 2>/dev/null && return 2
  return 3
}

macro_batch_duration() {
  local dur
  dur=$(grep -E "BATCH_DURATION.*domain=@${1} .*batch=${2}( |$)" "$MACRO_LOG" 2>/dev/null | tail -n 1 | grep -oE 'seconds=[0-9]+' | cut -d= -f2)
  [ -n "$dur" ] && echo "$dur" || echo ""
}

build_batch_line_short() {
  local id="$1" bid="$2" icon name domain attempt dur rc bx type tt when
  icon=$(get_bot_icon "$id"); name=$(get_bot_name "$id"); domain=$(get_bot_domain "$id")
  attempt=$(macro_batch_outcome "$domain" "$bid"); rc=$?
  dur=$(macro_batch_duration "$domain" "$bid"); [ -z "$dur" ] && dur="—" || dur="${dur}s"
  bx=$(get_batch_xcount "$id" "$bid")
  type=$(get_batch_type "$id" "$bid"); tt=$(get_batch_time "$id" "$bid")
  if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then when="🕐${tt}"; else when="⏱$(get_batch_interval "$id" "$bid")s"; fi
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    case "$rc" in
      0) printf '🟢 %s %s [%s x%s %s] | attempt 1 | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$dur" ;;
      1) printf '🟡 %s %s [%s x%s %s] | attempt %s | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$attempt" "$dur" ;;
      2) printf '🔴 %s %s [%s x%s %s] | failed | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$dur" ;;
      *) printf '⚪ %s %s [%s x%s %s] | no data | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$dur" ;;
    esac
  else
    case "$rc" in
      0) printf '🟢 %s %s [%s x%s %s] | 1-я попытка | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$dur" ;;
      1) printf '🟡 %s %s [%s x%s %s] | %s-я попытка | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$attempt" "$dur" ;;
      2) printf '🔴 %s %s [%s x%s %s] | провал | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$dur" ;;
      *) printf '⚪ %s %s [%s x%s %s] | нет данных | %s' "$icon" "$name" "$bid" "$bx" "$when" "$dur" ;;
    esac
  fi
}

build_bot_notify_block() {
  local pair out=""
  for pair in "$@"; do out="${out}$(build_batch_line_short "${pair%:*}" "${pair#*:}")\n"; done
  printf '%b' "$out"
}

generate_macro_report() {
  local -a pairs; pairs=("$@")
  local report="" pair b bid ok1=0 ok2=0 fail_n=0 unknown_n=0 total=0
  report="━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
  report="${report}📊 MACRO REPORT · $FARM_VERSION\n"
  report="${report}━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
  report="${report}🕒 $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n\n"
  for pair in "${pairs[@]}"; do
    total=$((total + 1))
    b="${pair%:*}"; bid="${pair#*:}"
    local icon name domain attempt dur rc bx type tt when
    icon=$(get_bot_icon "$b"); name=$(get_bot_name "$b"); domain=$(get_bot_domain "$b")
    bx=$(get_batch_xcount "$b" "$bid")
    attempt=$(macro_batch_outcome "$domain" "$bid"); rc=$?
    dur=$(macro_batch_duration "$domain" "$bid"); [ -z "$dur" ] && dur="—" || dur="${dur}s"
    type=$(get_batch_type "$b" "$bid"); tt=$(get_batch_time "$b" "$bid")
    if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then when="🕐${tt}"; else when="⏱$(get_batch_interval "$b" "$bid")s"; fi
    case "$rc" in
      0) report="${report}🟢 ${icon} ${name} [${bid} x${bx} ${when}] | attempt 1 | ${dur}\n"; ok1=$((ok1 + 1)) ;;
      1) report="${report}🟡 ${icon} ${name} [${bid} x${bx} ${when}] | attempt ${attempt} | ${dur}\n"; ok2=$((ok2 + 1)) ;;
      2) report="${report}🔴 ${icon} ${name} [${bid} x${bx} ${when}] | failed | ${dur}\n"; fail_n=$((fail_n + 1)) ;;
      *) report="${report}⚪ ${icon} ${name} [${bid} x${bx} ${when}] | no data | ${dur}\n"; unknown_n=$((unknown_n + 1)) ;;
    esac
  done
  report="${report}\n📈 Total: 🟢 ${ok1} | 🟡 ${ok2} | 🔴 ${fail_n}"
  [ "$unknown_n" -gt 0 ] && report="${report} | ⚪ ${unknown_n}"
  report="${report}  (batches: ${total})"
  printf '%b' "$report" > "$MACRO_REPORT"
}

# ==============================================================================
# WATCHDOG HELPERS
# ==============================================================================
kill_old_instances() {
  local cur_pid=$$ old_pid old_core_pid p cmd
  if [ -f "$DASHBOARD_TICK_PID_FILE" ]; then
    p=$(cat "$DASHBOARD_TICK_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -dc '0-9')
    [ -n "$p" ] && kill -9 "$p" 2>/dev/null
  fi
  if [ -f "$PID_FILE" ]; then
    old_pid=$(cat "$PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -dc '0-9')
    if [ -n "$old_pid" ] && [ "$old_pid" != "$cur_pid" ]; then kill -9 "$old_pid" 2>/dev/null; fi
  fi
  if [ -f "$CORE_PID_FILE" ]; then
    old_core_pid=$(cat "$CORE_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -dc '0-9')
    if [ -n "$old_core_pid" ] && [ "$old_core_pid" != "$cur_pid" ]; then kill -9 "$old_core_pid" 2>/dev/null; fi
  fi
  for p in $(pgrep -f "farm_root" 2>/dev/null); do
    if [ "$p" != "$cur_pid" ] && [ "$p" != "$PPID" ]; then kill -9 "$p" 2>/dev/null; fi
  done
  for p in $(pgrep -f "farm_V" 2>/dev/null); do
    if [ "$p" != "$cur_pid" ] && [ "$p" != "$PPID" ]; then kill -9 "$p" 2>/dev/null; fi
  done
  for p in /proc/[0-9]*; do
    [ -d "$p" ] || continue
    local pid="${p##*/}"
    [ "$pid" = "$cur_pid" ] || [ "$pid" = "$PPID" ] && continue
    if [ -r "$p/cmdline" ]; then
      cmd=$(tr '\0' ' ' < "$p/cmdline" 2>/dev/null)
      case "$cmd" in
        *farm_root*|*farm_V2.*)
          kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null
          ;;
      esac
    fi
  done
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" != "0" ]; then
    su -c "pkill -9 -f farm_root; pkill -9 -f farm_V" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
  rm -f "$PID_FILE" "$CORE_PID_FILE" "$RUNNING_LOCK" "$MACRO_RUNNER" "$DASHBOARD_TICK_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  rm -f "$TIMER_DIR"/prev_target_*.txt 2>/dev/null
}

make_immortal() {
  log "🛡 PROTECT" "OOM-protection..."
  rm -f "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then
    pgrep -f com.termux 2>/dev/null | xargs -I {} sh -c 'echo -1000 > /proc/{}/oom_score_adj 2>/dev/null' 2>/dev/null
    dumpsys battery > "$BAT_ROOT_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  else
    if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      timeout 10 su -c "pgrep -f com.termux | xargs -I {} sh -c 'echo -1000 > /proc/{}/oom_score_adj 2>/dev/null' 2>/dev/null; dumpsys battery > $BAT_ROOT_TMP 2>/dev/null" 2>/dev/null
    else
      su -c "pgrep -f com.termux | xargs -I {} sh -c 'echo -1000 > /proc/{}/oom_score_adj 2>/dev/null' 2>/dev/null; dumpsys battery > $BAT_ROOT_TMP 2>/dev/null" 2>/dev/null
    fi
  fi
  parse_root_power_tmp
  termux-wake-lock 2>/dev/null
}
