# ==============================================================================
# TELEGRAM API
# ==============================================================================
tg_api() { curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$2" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; }

tg_send() {
  local txt payload; txt=$(short_text "$1")
  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    payload=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --arg txt "$txt" '{chat_id: $cid, text: $txt}')
    tg_api "sendMessage" "$payload"
  else send_tg_plain "$txt"; fi
}

tg_send_with_km() {
  local TEXT KM PAYLOAD; TEXT=$(short_text "$1"); KM="$2"; [ -z "$KM" ] && KM='{"inline_keyboard":[]}'
  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --arg txt "$TEXT" --argjson km "$KM" '{chat_id: $cid, text: $txt, reply_markup: $km}')
  else PAYLOAD="{\"chat_id\":$TG_CHAT_ID,\"text\":$(json_escape "$TEXT"),\"reply_markup\":$KM}"; fi
  curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$PAYLOAD" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/sendMessage" >/dev/null 2>&1
}

tg_send_with_km_get_id() {
  local TEXT KM PAYLOAD RESP; TEXT=$(short_text "$1"); KM="$2"; [ -z "$KM" ] && KM='{"inline_keyboard":[]}'
  command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --arg txt "$TEXT" --argjson km "$KM" '{chat_id: $cid, text: $txt, reply_markup: $km}')
  RESP=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$PAYLOAD" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/sendMessage" 2>/dev/null)
  echo "$RESP" | jq -r '.result.message_id // empty' 2>/dev/null
}

tg_send_get_id() {
  local TEXT PAYLOAD RESP; TEXT=$(short_text "$1")
  command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --arg txt "$TEXT" '{chat_id: $cid, text: $txt}')
  RESP=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$PAYLOAD" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/sendMessage" 2>/dev/null)
  echo "$RESP" | jq -r '.result.message_id // empty' 2>/dev/null
}

delete_message_later() {
  local mid="$1" delay="${2:-3}"
  [ -z "$mid" ] && return 0
  echo "$mid" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || return 0
  ( sleep "$delay"; curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"chat_id\":\"$TG_CHAT_ID\",\"message_id\":$mid}" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/deleteMessage" >/dev/null 2>&1 ) &
}

tg_answer_cb() {
  local PAYLOAD
  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cb "$1" --arg txt "$2" '{callback_query_id: $cb, text: $txt}')
    tg_api "answerCallbackQuery" "$PAYLOAD"
  else
    curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST --data-urlencode "callback_query_id=$1" --data-urlencode "text=$2" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/answerCallbackQuery" >/dev/null 2>&1
  fi
}

tg_edit_text_km() {
  local msg_id="$1" text="$2" km="$3" PAYLOAD RESP
  if [ -z "$msg_id" ] || ! echo "$msg_id" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    tg_send_with_km "$text" "$km"; return 0
  fi
  text=$(short_text "$text")
  PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --argjson mid "$msg_id" --arg txt "$text" --argjson km "$km" '{chat_id: $cid, message_id: $mid, text: $txt, reply_markup: $km}')
  RESP=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$PAYLOAD" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/editMessageText" 2>/dev/null)
  if ! echo "$RESP" | grep -Eq '"ok":[[:space:]]*true'; then
    if ! echo "$RESP" | grep -q 'message is not modified'; then tg_send_with_km "$text" "$km"; fi
  fi
}

tg_send_progress_get_id() {
  local RESP PAYLOAD TEXT
  TEXT="$(T running)"
  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --arg txt "$TEXT" '{chat_id: $cid, text: $txt}')
    RESP=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$PAYLOAD" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/sendMessage" 2>/dev/null)
    echo "$RESP" | jq -r '.result.message_id // empty' 2>/dev/null
  fi
}

# ==============================================================================
# KEYBOARD BUILDER
# ==============================================================================
start_kb() { KB_ROWS=""; KB_ROW=""; KB_COL=0; }
add_kb_btn() {
  local text="$1" cb="$2" cols="${3:-2}" btn
  btn="{\"text\":$(json_escape "$text"),\"callback_data\":\"$cb\"}"
  if [ -z "$KB_ROW" ]; then KB_ROW="$btn"; else KB_ROW="$KB_ROW,$btn"; fi
  KB_COL=$((KB_COL + 1))
  if [ "$KB_COL" -ge "$cols" ]; then KB_ROWS="${KB_ROWS}[${KB_ROW}],"; KB_ROW=""; KB_COL=0; fi
}
add_kb_break() { if [ -n "$KB_ROW" ]; then KB_ROWS="${KB_ROWS}[${KB_ROW}],"; KB_ROW=""; KB_COL=0; fi; }
finish_kb() { add_kb_break; KB_ROWS="${KB_ROWS%,}"; printf '{"inline_keyboard":[%s]}' "$KB_ROWS"; }
build_back_keyboard() { printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_menu)")"; }

# ==============================================================================
# SAFE MODE
# ==============================================================================
version_has_safe_mode_v2() { local file="$1"; [ -f "$file" ] || return 1; grep -q "^# SAFE_MODE_LOGIC_V2" "$file" 2>/dev/null; }

find_previous_safe_v2_version() {
  local cur_hash=""
  [ -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" ] && cur_hash=$(md5sum "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}')
  local f h
  for f in $(get_version_list); do
    [ -f "$f" ] || continue
    if [ -n "$cur_hash" ]; then h=$(md5sum "$f" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}'); [ "$h" = "$cur_hash" ] && continue; fi
    version_has_safe_mode_v2 "$f" && { echo "$f"; return 0; }
  done
  return 1
}

enter_safe_mode() {
  local code="${1:-E06}"
  mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  printf '%s\n' "$FARM_LANG" > "$SAFE_MODE_LANG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  printf '%s\n' "$code" > "$SAFE_MODE_ERROR_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  printf '%s\n' "$(date +%s)" > "$SAFE_MODE_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  log "🛟 SAFE" "Entering safe mode. Trigger code=$code"
  if [ -f "$SAFE_MODE_ROLLBACK_FILE" ]; then log "🛟 SAFE" "Rollback already attempted — staying in safe mode."; return 0; fi
  local prev; prev=$(find_previous_safe_v2_version)
  if [ -n "$prev" ] && [ -f "$prev" ]; then
    if cp -f "$prev" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null; then
      chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
      printf 'done\n' > "$SAFE_MODE_ROLLBACK_FILE"
      log "🛟 SAFE" "Rolled back to $prev — restarting."
      send_tg_plain "$(T safe_mode_enter_notice)\n$(T safe_mode_rollback_done)"
      return 77
    fi
  fi
  printf 'failed\n' > "$SAFE_MODE_ROLLBACK_FILE"
  log "🛟 SAFE" "No compatible version for rollback. Staying in safe mode."
  return 0
}

clear_safe_mode_state() { rm -f "$SAFE_MODE_FILE" "$SAFE_MODE_ERROR_FILE" "$SAFE_MODE_ROLLBACK_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }

# ==============================================================================
# MAIN MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_main_menu_text() {
  local state
  if is_farm_paused; then state="$(T state_paused)"; else state="$(T state_active)"; fi
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    cat <<EOF
$(T main_title) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)

$state

━━━ CONTROL ━━━
▶️ /farm — full cycle
🚀 /farm<ID> — selective
⏸ /pause | ▶️ /start_farm
🛑 Emergency stop
⏰ /autostart
🔄 /res [<ID>] — reset timers

━━━ BOTS ━━━
👥 /bots, ➕ /addbot
📝 Commands — cooldown, daily time, X-times
📋 /queue

━━━ REPORTS ━━━
🔔 /rep HH:MM

━━━ LOGS ━━━
📊 /status | 📝 /macrolog | 📜 /logs

━━━ UPDATE ━━━
📦 /update — full script OR short patch
🗂 /versions | 💾 /saveversion | 📤 /exp

📁 DB: $DATA_ROOT
EOF
  else
    cat <<EOF
$(T main_title) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)

$state

━━━ УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ━━━
▶️ /farm — полный цикл
🚀 /farm<ID> — выборочно
⏸ /pause | ▶️ /start_farm
🛑 Экстренный стоп
⏰ /autostart
🔄 /res [<ID>] — сброс таймеров

━━━ БОТЫ ━━━
👥 /bots, ➕ /addbot
📝 Команды — перезарядка, время суток, X-разовые
📋 /queue

━━━ ОТЧЁТЫ ━━━
🔔 /rep ЧЧ:ММ

━━━ ЛОГИ ━━━
📊 /status | 📝 /macrolog | 📜 /logs

━━━ ОБНОВЛЕНИЕ ━━━
📦 /update — полный скрипт ИЛИ короткий патч
🗂 /versions | 💾 /saveversion | 📤 /exp

📁 БД: $DATA_ROOT
EOF
  fi
}

build_main_menu_keyboard() {
  local pause_btn pause_cb
  if is_farm_paused; then pause_btn="$(T btn_farm_start)"; pause_cb="farm_start"
  else pause_btn="$(T btn_farm_stop)"; pause_cb="farm_stop"; fi
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[
[{"text": %s, "callback_data": "emergency_stop"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "force_full_farm"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "%s"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_autostart"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_run"}, {"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_bots"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_reports"}, {"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_addbot"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_update"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_errhelp"}, {"text": "%s", "callback_data": "get_status"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "get_macro_log"}, {"text": "%s", "callback_data": "get_logs"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "menu_lang"}, {"text": "%s", "callback_data": "show_commands"}]
]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_emergency_stop)")" \
    "$(T btn_full_run)" \
    "$pause_btn" "$pause_cb" \
    "$(T btn_autostart)" \
    "$(T btn_selective)" "$(T btn_bots)" \
    "$(T btn_reports)" "$(T btn_addbot)" \
    "$(T btn_update)" \
    "$(T btn_errhelp)" "$(T btn_status)" \
    "$(T btn_marolog)" "$(T btn_logs)" \
    "$(T btn_lang)" "$(T btn_all_cmds)"
}

tg_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_main_menu_text)" "$(build_main_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_main_menu() {
  rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_main_menu_text)" "$(build_main_menu_keyboard)"
}

# ==============================================================================
# SAFE MODE MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_safe_mode_text() {
  local code sev sev_name what why fix rb rb_status
  code=$(cat "$SAFE_MODE_ERROR_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n'); [ -z "$code" ] && code="E06"
  sev=$(error_severity "$code"); sev_name=$(error_severity_name "$code")
  what=$(E "$code" what); why=$(E "$code" why); fix=$(E "$code" fix)
  rb=$(cat "$SAFE_MODE_ROLLBACK_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  case "$rb" in done) rb_status="$(T safe_mode_rollback_done)" ;; failed) rb_status="$(T safe_mode_rollback_failed)" ;; *) rb_status="—" ;; esac
  printf '%s\n' "$(T safe_mode_title)"
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'
  printf '%s %s · %s\n' "$sev" "$code" "$sev_name"
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n'
  printf '❓ %s\n\n🔎 %s\n\n🛠 %s\n\n' "$what" "$why" "$fix"
  printf '📌 %s\n%s\n\n' "$(T safe_mode_reason)" "$rb_status"
  printf 'ℹ️ %s\n\n%s\n' "$(T safe_mode_intro)" "$(T safe_mode_wait)"
}

build_safe_mode_keyboard() {
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "safe_update"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "safe_macrolog"}, {"text": "%s", "callback_data": "safe_logs"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "safe_errhelp"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "safe_exit"}]
]}' \
    "$(T btn_safe_update)" \
    "$(T btn_safe_macrolog)" "$(T btn_safe_logs)" \
    "$(T btn_safe_errhelp)" \
    "$(T btn_safe_exit)"
}

tg_safe_mode_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_safe_mode_text)" "$(build_safe_mode_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_safe_mode_menu() { tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_safe_mode_text)" "$(build_safe_mode_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# AUTOSTART MENU

build_autostart_menu_text() {
  local state_str interval
  if is_autostart_enabled; then state_str="$(T autostart_on)"; else state_str="$(T autostart_off)"; fi
  interval=$(get_autostart_interval)
  printf '%s\n\n%s: %s\n%s: %s\n\n%s\n\n%s' \
    "$(T autostart_title)" "$(T autostart_status)" "$state_str" \
    "$(T autostart_interval)" "$(format_seconds "$interval")" \
    "$(T autostart_hint)" "$(T autostart_choose)"
}

build_autostart_menu_keyboard() {
  local toggle_text toggle_cb
  if is_autostart_enabled; then toggle_text="🔴 $(T btn_off)"; toggle_cb="autostart_disable"
  else toggle_text="🟢 $(T btn_on)"; toggle_cb="autostart_enable"; fi
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "%s"}],
[{"text": "⏱ 5m", "callback_data": "autostart_set_300"}, {"text": "⏱ 15m", "callback_data": "autostart_set_900"}, {"text": "⏱ 30m", "callback_data": "autostart_set_1800"}],
[{"text": "⏱ 1h", "callback_data": "autostart_set_3600"}, {"text": "⏱ 2h", "callback_data": "autostart_set_7200"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "autostart_input"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "main_menu"}]
]}' \
    "$toggle_text" "$toggle_cb" \
    "$(T btn_autostart_input)" \
    "$(T btn_back_menu)"
}

tg_autostart_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_autostart_menu_text)" "$(build_autostart_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_autostart_menu() {
  rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_autostart_menu_text)" "$(build_autostart_menu_keyboard)"
}

# ==============================================================================
# ERROR HELP (sorted by severity then number)
# ==============================================================================
build_error_help_text() {
  local head out c
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    head="🆘 Error code help ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n\nSeverity: 🟢 info · 🟡 medium · 🟠 high · 🔴 critical\nSorted by severity, then by number. Tap a code below.\n\n"
  else
    head="🆘 Справка по кодам ошибок ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n\nСерьёзность: 🟢 инфо · 🟡 средняя · 🟠 высокая · 🔴 критично\nСортировка по серьёзности, затем по номеру.\n\n"
  fi
  out="$head"
  for c in $(sorted_error_codes); do
    out="${out}$(error_severity "$c") ${c} — $(E "$c" what)\n"
  done
  printf '%b' "$out"
}

build_error_help_keyboard() {
  local kb='{"inline_keyboard":[' row="" cnt=0 c
  for c in $(sorted_error_codes); do
    row="${row}{\"text\":\"${c}\",\"callback_data\":\"err_show_${c}\"},"
    cnt=$((cnt + 1))
    if [ $((cnt % 5)) -eq 0 ]; then kb="${kb}[${row%,}],"; row=""; fi
  done
  [ -n "$row" ] && kb="${kb}[${row%,}],"
  kb="${kb}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_menu)"),\"callback_data\":\"main_menu\"}]]}"
  printf '%s' "$kb"
}

tg_error_help_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_error_help_text)" "$(build_error_help_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_error_help_menu() {
  rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_error_help_text)" "$(build_error_help_keyboard)"
}

build_error_detail_text() {
  local code="$1" sev sev_name
  sev=$(error_severity "$code"); sev_name=$(error_severity_name "$code")
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n🆘 %s · %s %s\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n%s\n\n❓ Why:\n%s\n\n🛠 How to fix:\n%s\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n' \
      "$code" "$sev" "$sev_name" "$(E "$code" what)" "$(E "$code" why)" "$(E "$code" fix)"
  else
    printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n🆘 %s · %s %s\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n%s\n\n❓ Почему:\n%s\n\n🛠 Как исправить:\n%s\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n' \
      "$code" "$sev" "$sev_name" "$(E "$code" what)" "$(E "$code" why)" "$(E "$code" fix)"
  fi
}

build_error_detail_keyboard() {
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"menu_errhelp"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_menu)")"
}

tg_error_detail() { tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_error_detail_text "$2")" "$(build_error_detail_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# LANGUAGE MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_lang_menu_text() {
  local cur
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then cur="🇬🇧 English"
    printf '🌐 Language selection\n\nCurrent: %s\n\n• 🇷🇺 Russian\n• 🇬🇧 English\n' "$cur"
  else cur="🇷🇺 Русский"
    printf '🌐 Выбор языка\n\nТекущий язык: %s\n\n• 🇷🇺 Русский\n• 🇬🇧 English\n' "$cur"; fi
}
build_lang_menu_keyboard() {
  local kb_ru kb_en
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then kb_ru="$(T lang_set_ru)"; kb_en="✅ $(T lang_set_en)"
  else kb_ru="✅ $(T lang_set_ru)"; kb_en="$(T lang_set_en)"; fi
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"set_lang_RU"}],[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"set_lang_EN"}],[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
    "$kb_ru" "$kb_en" "$(T btn_back_menu)"
}
tg_lang_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_lang_menu_text)" "$(build_lang_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_lang_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_lang_menu_text)" "$(build_lang_menu_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# RUN MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_run_menu_text() {
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then printf '🚀 Selective run (without waiting for timer):'
  else printf '🚀 Выборочный запуск без ожидания таймера:'; fi
}
build_run_menu_keyboard() {
  local id label; start_kb
  for id in $(get_queue); do
    if is_bot_enabled "$id"; then label="$(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id")"
    else label="❌ $(get_bot_name "$id")"; fi
    add_kb_btn "$label" "run_bot_$id" 2
  done
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_run_all)" "force_full_farm" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 1
  finish_kb
}
tg_run_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_run_menu_text)" "$(build_run_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_run_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_run_menu_text)" "$(build_run_menu_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# BOTS MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_bots_menu_text() {
  local q text idx=1 id
  q=$(get_queue)
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then text="$(T bots_title) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n\n"
  else text="$(T bots_title) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n\n"; fi
  if [ -z "$q" ]; then text="${text}$(T no_bots_in_queue)"
  else
    for id in $q; do
      local status icon name count=0 bid
      if is_bot_enabled "$id"; then status="🟢"; else status="🔴"; fi
      icon=$(get_bot_icon "$id"); name=$(get_bot_name "$id")
      for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$id"); do count=$((count + 1)); done
      text="${text}${idx}) ${status} ${icon} ${name}  •  📦${count}\n"
      for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$id"); do
        text="${text}$(format_batch_line "$id" "$bid")\n"
      done
      idx=$((idx + 1))
    done
  fi
  printf '%s' "$text"
}
build_bots_menu_keyboard() {
  local id label; start_kb
  for id in $(get_queue); do
    if is_bot_enabled "$id"; then label="🟢 $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id")"
    else label="🔴 $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id")"; fi
    add_kb_btn "$label" "bcfg_$id" 2
  done
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_queue)" "menu_queue" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_addbot)" "menu_addbot" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 1
  finish_kb
}
tg_bots_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_bots_menu_text)" "$(build_bots_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_bots_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_bots_menu_text)" "$(build_bots_menu_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# BOT CFG MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_bot_cfg_text() {
  local id="$1" upg_status en_status stock_status batches blist="" bid
  batches=$(get_bot_batches "$id")
  for bid in $batches; do blist="${blist}$(format_batch_line "$id" "$bid")\n"; done
  local first; first=$(echo "$batches" | head -n1); [ -z "$first" ] && first="b1"
  if is_bot_upgradeable "$id"; then upg_status="$(T bot_cfg_interval_allowed)"; else upg_status="$(T bot_cfg_interval_forbidden)"; fi
  if is_bot_enabled "$id"; then en_status="$(T bot_cfg_enabled)"; else en_status="$(T bot_cfg_disabled)"; fi
  if is_stock_bot "$id"; then stock_status="$(T bot_cfg_type_stock)"; else stock_status="$(T bot_cfg_type_custom)"; fi
  printf '%s\n\n%s %s (ID %s)\n\n🆔 ID: %s\n🌐 @%s\n%s\n%s\n%s\n\n%s\n%s\n%s (%s): %s\n' \
    "$(T bot_cfg_title)" "$(get_bot_icon "$id")" "$(get_bot_name "$id")" "$id" \
    "$id" "$(get_bot_domain "$id")" "$stock_status" "$upg_status" "$en_status" \
    "$(T bot_cfg_batches)" "$blist" \
    "$(T bot_cfg_next)" "$first" "$(format_time_left "$(get_batch_target "$id" "$first")")"
  printf "\n%s\n" "$(T bot_cfg_interval_hint)"
}

build_bot_cfg_keyboard() {
  local id="$1" en_btn upg_btn
  if is_bot_enabled "$id"; then en_btn="$(T btn_on)"; else en_btn="$(T btn_off)"; fi
  if is_bot_upgradeable "$id"; then upg_btn="$(T btn_interval_yes)"; else upg_btn="$(T btn_interval_no)"; fi
  start_kb
  add_kb_btn "$en_btn" "btgl_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$upg_btn" "bupg_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_name_emoji)" "biname_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_commands)" "bcmds_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_username)" "biuser_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_interval)" "bint_$id" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_delay_15)" "delay_${id}_900" 3
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_delay_30)" "delay_${id}_1800" 3
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_delay_1h)" "delay_${id}_3600" 3
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_reset_timer_short)" "res_$id" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_del)" "bdel_$id" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_queue)" "menu_queue" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_bots)" "menu_bots" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 2
  finish_kb
}

tg_bot_cfg_menu() {
  echo "$1" > "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_send_with_km "$(build_bot_cfg_text "$1")" "$(build_bot_cfg_keyboard "$1")"
}
tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu() {
  [ -n "$2" ] && echo "$2" > "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_bot_cfg_text "$2")" "$(build_bot_cfg_keyboard "$2")"
}

# ==============================================================================
# QUEUE MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_queue_menu_text() {
  local q idx=1 text
  q=$(get_queue)
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then text="📋 Queue order ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n\n"
  else text="📋 Порядок очереди ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n\n"; fi
  if [ -z "$q" ]; then text="${text}$(T no_bots_in_queue)\n"
  else
    local id status
    for id in $q; do
      if is_bot_enabled "$id"; then status="🟢"; else status="🔴"; fi
      text="${text}${idx}) ${status} $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id") (@$(get_bot_domain "$id"))\n"
      idx=$((idx + 1))
    done
  fi
  printf '%s' "$text"
}
build_queue_menu_keyboard() {
  local id; start_kb
  for id in $(get_queue); do
    add_kb_btn "⬆️ $(get_bot_icon "$id") $(get_bot_name "$id")" "qup_$id" 2
    add_kb_btn "⬇️" "qdn_$id" 2
  done
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_bots)" "menu_bots" 2
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 2
  finish_kb
}
tg_queue_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_queue_menu_text)" "$(build_queue_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_queue_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_queue_menu_text)" "$(build_queue_menu_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# REPORTS MENU
# ==============================================================================
build_reports_menu_text() {
  local cur_rep cur_rep_str time_to_rep cur_clock
  cur_rep=$(cat "$STATE_INTERVAL" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1); [ -z "$cur_rep" ] && cur_rep=$DEFAULT_REPORT_INTERVAL
  cur_rep_str=$(format_seconds "$cur_rep")
  [ -z "${NEXT_REPORT:-}" ] && NEXT_REPORT=$(date +%s)
  time_to_rep=$(format_time_left "$NEXT_REPORT")
  cur_clock=$(date '+%H:%M:%S')
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    printf '%s:\n🕒 %s\n⚙️ Interval: %s\n⏳ Until report: %s\n\n/rep HH:MM — e.g. /rep 01:00\n/rep 30m — 30 min\n/rep 3600 — 3600 sec' \
      "$(T reports_title)" "$cur_clock" "$cur_rep_str" "$time_to_rep"
  else
    printf '%s:\n🕒 %s\n⚙️ Частота: %s\n⏳ До отчёта: %s\n\n/rep ЧЧ:ММ — например /rep 01:00\n/rep 30m — 30 мин\n/rep 3600 — 3600 сек' \
      "$(T reports_title)" "$cur_clock" "$cur_rep_str" "$time_to_rep"
  fi
}
build_reports_menu_keyboard() {
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"menu_reports"}],[{"text":"⏱ 5 min","callback_data":"rep_300"},{"text":"⏱ 15 min","callback_data":"rep_900"},{"text":"⏱ 30 min","callback_data":"rep_1800"}],[{"text":"⏱ 1 h","callback_data":"rep_3600"},{"text":"⏱ 2 h","callback_data":"rep_7200"}],[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
      "$(T btn_refresh)" "$(T btn_back_menu)"
  else
    printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"menu_reports"}],[{"text":"⏱ 5 мин","callback_data":"rep_300"},{"text":"⏱ 15 мин","callback_data":"rep_900"},{"text":"⏱ 30 мин","callback_data":"rep_1800"}],[{"text":"⏱ 1 час","callback_data":"rep_3600"},{"text":"⏱ 2 часа","callback_data":"rep_7200"}],[{"text":"%s","callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
      "$(T btn_refresh)" "$(T btn_back_menu)"
  fi
}
tg_reports_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_reports_menu_text)" "$(build_reports_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_reports_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_reports_menu_text)" "$(build_reports_menu_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# ADD BOT HELP
# ==============================================================================
build_addbot_help() {
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    cat <<'EOF'
➕ Add bot (multi-batch)

📝 One-liner: /addbot @username [1|0] 🐥 Name. <batch_spec>

Examples:
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Chicks. /claim
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Chicks. 02:30 /claim /crack
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Chicks. b1 02:30 /command b2 03:00 x2 /crack
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Chicks. @14:30 /daily
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Chicks. 02:30 r5 /claim

Rules:
• @HH:MM — daily time trigger
• rN — X-times (removed after N triggers; 0 = unlimited)
• Time: 02:30 · 0230 · 90m · 2h · 5400
EOF
  else
    cat <<'EOF'
➕ Добавление бота (multi-batch)

📝 Одной строкой: /addbot @username [1|0] 🐥 Имя. <спецификация_батчей>

Примеры:
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Цыплята. /claim
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Цыплята. 02:30 /claim /crack
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Цыплята. b1 02:30 /command b2 03:00 x2 /crack
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Цыплята. @14:30 /daily
• /addbot @cool 1 🐥 Цыплята. 02:30 r5 /claim

Правила:
• @ЧЧ:ММ — ежедневное срабатывание по времени
• rN — X-разовый (удалится после N срабатываний; 0 = без ограничений)
• Время: 02:30 · 0230 · 90m · 2h · 5400
EOF
  fi
}

# ==============================================================================
# STATUS
# ==============================================================================
build_status_keyboard() {
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"refresh_status"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_refresh)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_menu)")"
}
build_status_text() {
  local BAT CHARGING NOW UPTIME H M BAT_TEXT q bots_info idx b b_status cur_rep rep_str TEXT
  BAT=$(get_battery_level_force); CHARGING=$(get_charging_status_force)
  NOW=$(date +%s); UPTIME=$((NOW - START_TIME)); H=$((UPTIME/3600)); M=$(((UPTIME%3600)/60))
  if [ "$BAT" = "unknown" ]; then BAT_TEXT="?"; else BAT_TEXT="${BAT}%"; fi
  [ "$CHARGING" = "1" ] && BAT_TEXT="⚡ ${BAT_TEXT}"
  q=$(get_queue); bots_info=""; idx=1
  for b in $q; do
    if is_bot_enabled "$b"; then b_status="🟢"; else b_status="🔴"; fi
    local count=0 bid
    for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do count=$((count + 1)); done
    bots_info="${bots_info}${idx}) ${b_status} $(get_bot_icon "$b") $(get_bot_name "$b") [📦${count}]\n"
    for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do
      bots_info="${bots_info}$(format_batch_line "$b" "$bid")\n"
    done
    idx=$((idx + 1))
  done
  cur_rep=$(cat "$STATE_INTERVAL" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1); [ -z "$cur_rep" ] && cur_rep=$DEFAULT_REPORT_INTERVAL
  rep_str=$(format_seconds "$cur_rep")
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    TEXT="$(T status_title) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n🔋 Battery: $BAT_TEXT\n⏱ Uptime: ${H}h ${M}m\n🔔 Report: ${rep_str}\n📁 $DATA_ROOT\n\n📋 Queue:\n$bots_info"
  else
    TEXT="$(T status_title) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)\n🔋 Батарея: $BAT_TEXT\n⏱ Аптайм: ${H}ч ${M}м\n🔔 Частота отчетов: ${rep_str}\n📁 $DATA_ROOT\n\n📋 Очередь:\n$bots_info"
  fi
  echo "$BAT" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && [ "$BAT" -le 5 ] && TEXT="$TEXT\n$(T critical_battery)"
  printf '%s' "$TEXT"
}
tg_status_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_status_text)" "$(build_status_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_status_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_status_text)" "$(build_status_keyboard)"; }
tg_status_send_or_edit() {
  local status_mid new_mid
  status_mid=$(cat "$STATUS_MSG_ID_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
  if [ -n "$status_mid" ]; then tg_edit_status_message "$status_mid" "$(build_status_text)" "$(build_status_keyboard)" && return 0; fi
  new_mid=$(tg_send_with_km_get_id "$(build_status_text)" "$(build_status_keyboard)")
  [ -n "$new_mid" ] && echo "$new_mid" > "$STATUS_MSG_ID_FILE" 2>/dev/null
}
tg_edit_status_message() {
  local mid="$1" text="$2" km="$3" RESP PAYLOAD
  [ -z "$mid" ] && return 1
  echo "$mid" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || return 1
  command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 1
  text=$(short_text "$text")
  PAYLOAD=$(jq -n --arg cid "$TG_CHAT_ID" --argjson mid "$mid" --arg txt "$text" --argjson km "$km" '{chat_id: $cid, message_id: $mid, text: $txt, reply_markup: $km}')
  RESP=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 3 -m 8 -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "$PAYLOAD" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/editMessageText" 2>/dev/null)
  echo "$RESP" | grep -Eq '"ok":[[:space:]]*true' && return 0
  echo "$RESP" | grep -q 'message is not modified' && return 0
  return 1
}

# ==============================================================================
# MACRO LOG
# ==============================================================================
build_macro_log_text() {
  local text id icon name any=0 page per total_history total_pages from_line to_line
  page=$(cat "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$page" ] && page=0
  per=5
  text="$(T macrolog_title) $FARM_VERSION\n"
  text="${text}🕒 $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n\n━━━ 🤖 BOTS ━━━\n"
  for id in $(get_queue); do
    any=1; icon=$(get_bot_icon "$id"); name=$(get_bot_name "$id")
    text="${text}${icon} ${name}\n"
    local bid
    for bid in $(get_bot_batches "$id"); do text="${text}$(format_batch_line "$id" "$bid")\n"; done
  done
  [ "$any" = "0" ] && text="${text}(no bots)\n"
  text="${text}\n━━━ 📊 LAST RUN ━━━\n"
  if [ -s "$MACRO_REPORT" ]; then
    local body total_line
    body=$(grep -E '\[b[0-9]+\]' "$MACRO_REPORT" 2>/dev/null)
    total_line=$(grep -E '^📈 Total' "$MACRO_REPORT" 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -n "$body" ]; then text="${text}${body}\n"; else text="${text}(no data)\n"; fi
    [ -n "$total_line" ] && text="${text}\n${total_line}\n"
  else text="${text}(empty report)\n"; fi
  total_history=$(wc -l < "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$total_history" ] && total_history=0
  total_pages=$(( (total_history + per - 1) / per )); [ "$total_pages" -eq 0 ] && total_pages=1
  if [ "$page" -ge "$total_pages" ]; then page=$((total_pages - 1)); echo "$page" > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; fi
  [ "$page" -lt 0 ] && page=0
  text="${text}\n━━━ 📅 HISTORY · page $((page+1))/$total_pages ━━━\n"
  if [ "$total_history" -eq 0 ]; then text="${text}(no records)\n"
  else
    to_line=$(( total_history - page * per )); from_line=$(( to_line - per + 1 ))
    [ "$from_line" -lt 1 ] && from_line=1
    [ "$to_line" -lt 1 ] && to_line=0
    local hist_out=""
    while IFS='|' read -r ts bname bdom bstatus bextra; do
      [ -z "$ts" ] && continue
      bname=$(echo "$bname" | sed -e 's/^ *//' -e 's/ *$//'); bdom=$(echo "$bdom" | sed -e 's/^ *//' -e 's/ *$//')
      bstatus=$(echo "$bstatus" | sed -e 's/^ *//' -e 's/ *$//'); bextra=$(echo "$bextra" | sed -e 's/^ *//' -e 's/ *$//')
      local ts_short="${ts##* }" emo="⚪"
      case "$bstatus" in OK) emo="🟢" ;; WARN) emo="🟡" ;; FAIL) emo="🔴" ;; esac
      hist_out="${hist_out}${emo} ${ts_short} ${bname} ${bdom}  (${bextra})\n"
    done < <(sed -n "${from_line},${to_line}p" "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null)
    [ -z "$hist_out" ] && hist_out="(no records)\n"
    text="${text}${hist_out}"
  fi
  printf '%s' "$text"
}
build_macro_log_keyboard() {
  local kb_rows="" nav_row="" page per total_history total_pages
  page=$(cat "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$page" ] && page=0
  per=5
  total_history=$(wc -l < "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$total_history" ] && total_history=0
  total_pages=$(( (total_history + per - 1) / per )); [ "$total_pages" -eq 0 ] && total_pages=1
  if [ "$total_pages" -gt 1 ]; then
    [ "$page" -gt 0 ] && nav_row="${nav_row}{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_newer)"),\"callback_data\":\"macro_page_newer\"},"
    [ "$page" -lt $((total_pages - 1)) ] && nav_row="${nav_row}{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_older)"),\"callback_data\":\"macro_page_older\"},"
  fi
  [ -n "$nav_row" ] && kb_rows="${kb_rows}[${nav_row%,}],"
  kb_rows="${kb_rows}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_refresh)"),\"callback_data\":\"get_macro_log\"}],"
  kb_rows="${kb_rows}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_macro_clear_report)"),\"callback_data\":\"clear_macro_report\"},{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_macro_clear_history)"),\"callback_data\":\"clear_collections\"}],"
  kb_rows="${kb_rows}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_macro_clear_all)"),\"callback_data\":\"clear_macro_all\"}],"
  kb_rows="${kb_rows}[{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_menu)"),\"callback_data\":\"main_menu\"}]"
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[%s]}' "$kb_rows"
}
tg_macro_log_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_macro_log_text)" "$(build_macro_log_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_macro_log_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_macro_log_text)" "$(build_macro_log_keyboard)"; }

# ==============================================================================
# CRASH LOGS
# ==============================================================================
build_logs_text() {
  local page total per start end content
  page=$(cat "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$page" ] && page=0
  total=$(wc -l < "$CRASH_LOG" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$total" ] && total=0
  per=25
  end=$(( total - page*per )); start=$(( end - per ))
  [ "$end" -gt "$total" ] && end=$total
  [ "$start" -lt 0 ] && start=0
  [ "$end" -lt 0 ] && end=0
  if [ "$total" -eq 0 ]; then content="$(T no_crashes)"
  else content=$(sed -n "$((start+1)),${end}p" "$CRASH_LOG" 2>/dev/null); [ -z "$content" ] && content="(no lines)"; fi
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    printf '%s %s\nPage %d • total lines: %d\n\n%s' "$(T logs_title)" "$FARM_VERSION" "$((page+1))" "$total" "$content"
  else
    printf '%s %s\nСтр. %d • всего строк: %d\n\n%s' "$(T logs_title)" "$FARM_VERSION" "$((page+1))" "$total" "$content"
  fi
}
build_logs_keyboard() {
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"logs_older"},{"text":%s,"callback_data":"logs_newer"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"clear_crash"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"get_logs"},{"text":%s,"callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_older)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_newer)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_clear)")" \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_refresh)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back_menu)")"
}
tg_logs_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_logs_text)" "$(build_logs_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_logs_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_logs_text)" "$(build_logs_keyboard)"; }

# Auto reports send NEW message
generate_report() { log "📊 REPORT" "Auto status report — new message."; tg_status_menu; }

# ==============================================================================
# UPDATE FEATURE VALIDATION
# ==============================================================================
validate_update_features() {
  local file="$1" missing="" m
  local markers="parse_batches_spec get_bot_batches get_batch_interval get_batch_xcount get_batch_command set_bot_batches get_batch_target BATCH_REPEAT_DELAY addbot_session_reset addbot_session_clear handle_addbot_text_input addbot_has_dup_intervals addbot_go_back addbot_save_batch_line poll_emergency_stop emergency_stop_last_opened LAST_OPENED_DOMAIN_FILE validate_update_features batches.txt SAFE_MODE_LOGIC_V2 enter_safe_mode find_previous_safe_v2_version version_has_safe_mode_v2 build_safe_mode_text apply_patch_file detect_patch_or_script FARM_PATCH_V1 get_batch_type get_batch_time get_batch_remaining next_time_occurrence handle_addcmd_text handle_batch_edit_input session_check_timeout clear_all_sessions sorted_error_codes"
  for m in $markers; do
    grep -q "$m" "$file" 2>/dev/null || missing="$missing $m"
  done
  [ -n "$missing" ] && { echo "$missing"; return 1; }
  return 0
}

# ==============================================================================
# UPDATE MENU
# ==============================================================================
get_version_list() { ls -1t "$VERSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null | grep -E '\.(txt|sh)$' | while read -r f; do echo "$VERSION_DIR/$f"; done; }

read_backup_version() {
  local v; v=$(grep -m1 '^FARM_VERSION=' "$1" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 | cut -d= -f2- | tr -d '"' | tr -d "'" | tr -d ' ')
  [ -z "$v" ] && v=$(basename "$1" | cut -d_ -f1); echo "$v"
}
read_backup_date() { grep -m1 '^# FARM_META' "$1" 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/.*|date=\(.*\)|note=.*/\1/p'; }

save_current_version() {
  local note="${1:-manual}" ts dest saved_date
  ts=$(date '+%Y%m%d_%H%M%S'); saved_date=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
  mkdir -p "$VERSION_DIR" 2>/dev/null
  [ ! -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" ] && return 1
  dest="$VERSION_DIR/${FARM_VERSION}_${ts}.txt"
  cp -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" "$dest" 2>/dev/null || return 1
  sed -i "1a # FARM_META|version=${FARM_VERSION}|date=${saved_date}|note=${note}" "$dest" 2>/dev/null
  return 0
}

set_update_await() { date +%s > "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; }

update_allowed() {
  local caption="$1" name="$2" now ts
  if [ -f "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" ]; then
    ts=$(cat "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$ts" ] && ts=0
    now=$(date +%s)
    [ $((now - ts)) -lt "$UPDATE_AWAIT_TTL" ] && return 0
  fi
  echo "$caption" | grep -Eiq 'update|обнов|install|установи|ставь|patch|патч' && return 0
  echo "$name" | grep -Eqi '^farm.*\.(txt|sh)$|^patch.*\.(txt|sh)$' && return 0
  return 1
}

build_update_menu_text() {
  local await_status="⏸ off" ts now cnt
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    if [ -f "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" ]; then
      ts=$(cat "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$ts" ] && ts=0
      now=$(date +%s)
      [ $((now - ts)) -lt "$UPDATE_AWAIT_TTL" ] && await_status="🟢 active ($(( (UPDATE_AWAIT_TTL - (now - ts)) / 60 )) min left)"
    fi
    cnt=$(get_version_list | wc -l | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$cnt" ] && cnt=0
    printf '%s\n\nWaiting: %s\nSaved versions: %s\n\nAccepts FULL script OR SHORT patch (FARM_PATCH_V1).\nPatch gets $TARGET_SCRIPT env var pointing to the farm script.' \
      "$(T btn_update) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)" "$await_status" "$cnt"
  else
    await_status="⏸ не активно"
    if [ -f "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" ]; then
      ts=$(cat "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$ts" ] && ts=0
      now=$(date +%s)
      [ $((now - ts)) -lt "$UPDATE_AWAIT_TTL" ] && await_status="🟢 активно (осталось $(( (UPDATE_AWAIT_TTL - (now - ts)) / 60 )) мин)"
    fi
    cnt=$(get_version_list | wc -l | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$cnt" ] && cnt=0
    printf '%s\n\nСтатус: %s\nСохранённых версий: %s\n\nПринимается ПОЛНЫЙ скрипт ИЛИ КОРОТКИЙ патч (FARM_PATCH_V1).\nПатч получает переменную $TARGET_SCRIPT — путь к скрипту фермы.' \
      "$(T btn_update) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)" "$await_status" "$cnt"
  fi
}

build_update_upload_help() {
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    printf '📥 Update upload\n\nA) FULL SCRIPT:\n   FARM_VERSION= and getUpdates required.\n\nB) SHORT PATCH (FARM_PATCH_V1):\n   First line: # FARM_PATCH_V1\n   Patch receives $TARGET_SCRIPT (path to farm script).\n   Example: sed -i "s/old/new/g" "$TARGET_SCRIPT"\n   Farm auto-backs-up, verifies syntax, restarts.\n   On any failure — file restored from backup.\n'
  else
    printf '📥 Загрузка обновления\n\nA) ПОЛНЫЙ СКРИПТ:\n   Нужны FARM_VERSION= и getUpdates.\n\nB) КОРОТКИЙ ПАТЧ (FARM_PATCH_V1):\n   Первая строка: # FARM_PATCH_V1\n   Патч получает переменную $TARGET_SCRIPT — путь к скрипту фермы.\n   Пример: sed -i "s/old/new/g" "$TARGET_SCRIPT"\n   Ферма сама делает бэкап, проверяет синтаксис, перезапускает.\n   При любой ошибке — файл восстанавливается из бэкапа.\n'
  fi
}

build_update_menu_keyboard() {
  printf '{"inline_keyboard":[
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "update_upload"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "update_save"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "exp_tg"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "update_versions"}],
[{"text": "%s", "callback_data": "main_menu"}]
]}' \
    "$(T btn_update_upload)" "$(T btn_update_save)" \
    "$(T btn_update_send_tg)" "$(T btn_update_versions)" \
    "$(T btn_back_menu)"
}
tg_update_menu() { tg_send_with_km "$(build_update_menu_text)" "$(build_update_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_update_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_update_menu_text)" "$(build_update_menu_keyboard)"; }

# Versions with pagination
build_versions_menu_text() {
  local files f i=1 text v d page total per total_pages
  page=$(cat "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$page" ] && page=0
  per=5
  total=$(get_version_list | wc -l | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$total" ] && total=0
  total_pages=$(( (total + per - 1) / per )); [ "$total_pages" -eq 0 ] && total_pages=1
  if [ "$page" -ge "$total_pages" ]; then page=$((total_pages - 1)); echo "$page" > "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; fi
  [ "$page" -lt 0 ] && page=0
  files=$(get_version_list | sed -n "$((page*per+1)),$((page*per+per))p")
  if [ "$FARM_LANG" = "EN" ]; then
    text="🗂 Saved versions  (page $((page+1))/$total_pages, total $total):\n"
    if [ -z "$files" ]; then text="${text}No saved versions."
    else
      for f in $files; do
        v=$(read_backup_version "$f"); d=$(read_backup_date "$f")
        if [ -n "$d" ]; then text="${text}${i}) 📦 $v — $d\n"; else text="${text}${i}) 📦 $v\n"; fi
        i=$((i + 1))
      done
      text="${text}\nTap to rollback."
    fi
  else
    text="🗂 Сохранённые версии  (стр. $((page+1))/$total_pages, всего $total):\n"
    if [ -z "$files" ]; then text="${text}Нет сохранённых версий."
    else
      for f in $files; do
        v=$(read_backup_version "$f"); d=$(read_backup_date "$f")
        if [ -n "$d" ]; then text="${text}${i}) 📦 $v — $d\n"; else text="${text}${i}) 📦 $v\n"; fi
        i=$((i + 1))
      done
      text="${text}\nНажмите кнопку, чтобы откатиться."
    fi
  fi
  printf '%s' "$text"
}
build_versions_menu_keyboard() {
  local files f i v page total per total_pages idx_base
  page=$(cat "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$page" ] && page=0
  per=5
  total=$(get_version_list | wc -l | tr -d ' '); [ -z "$total" ] && total=0
  total_pages=$(( (total + per - 1) / per )); [ "$total_pages" -eq 0 ] && total_pages=1
  files=$(get_version_list | sed -n "$((page*per+1)),$((page*per+per))p")
  start_kb; i=1
  for f in $files; do
    v=$(read_backup_version "$f"); idx_base=$((page*per + i))
    add_kb_btn "↩️ $v" "rbidx_$idx_base" 1
    i=$((i + 1))
  done
  if [ "$total_pages" -gt 1 ]; then
    local nav_row=""
    [ "$page" -gt 0 ] && nav_row="${nav_row}{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_newer)"),\"callback_data\":\"versions_newer\"},"
    [ "$page" -lt $((total_pages - 1)) ] && nav_row="${nav_row}{\"text\":$(json_escape "$(T btn_logs_older)"),\"callback_data\":\"versions_older\"},"
    [ -n "$nav_row" ] && KB_ROWS="${KB_ROWS}[${nav_row%,}],"
  fi
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_update_save)" "update_save" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_update)" "menu_update" 1
  add_kb_btn "$(T btn_back_menu)" "main_menu" 1
  finish_kb
}
tg_versions_menu() { echo 0 > "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_send_with_km "$(build_versions_menu_text)" "$(build_versions_menu_keyboard)"; }
tg_edit_versions_menu() { rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_text_km "$1" "$(build_versions_menu_text)" "$(build_versions_menu_keyboard)"; }

update_restart() { dash_cleanup 2>/dev/null; restore_dashboard 2>/dev/null; rm -f "$CORE_PID_FILE" "$RUNNING_LOCK" 2>/dev/null; exit 77; }

# ==============================================================================
# PATCH SUPPORT
# ==============================================================================
detect_patch_or_script() {
  local file="$1"
  if grep -q '^# FARM_PATCH_V1' "$file" 2>/dev/null; then echo "patch"; return 0; fi
  if grep -q 'FARM_VERSION=' "$file" 2>/dev/null && grep -q 'getUpdates' "$file" 2>/dev/null; then echo "script"; return 0; fi
  echo "invalid"; return 1
}

apply_patch_file() {
  local patch_file="$1" backup pre_hash post_hash err feats prc
  [ ! -s "$patch_file" ] && { tg_send "$(T upd_file_empty)"; return 1; }
  backup="$VERSION_DIR/${FARM_VERSION}_prepatch_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).txt"
  cp -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" "$backup" 2>/dev/null || { tg_send "$(T upd_backup_failed)"; return 1; }
  pre_hash=$(md5sum "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}')
  log "🧩 PATCH" "Applying patch $patch_file (backup=$backup)"
  (
    export TARGET_SCRIPT="$SCRIPT_PATH"
    export FARM_SCRIPT="$SCRIPT_PATH"
    export TARGET="$SCRIPT_PATH"
    cd "$(dirname "$SCRIPT_PATH")" || true
    bash "$patch_file" "$SCRIPT_PATH"
  ) >> "$PATCH_LOG" 2>&1
  prc=$?
  post_hash=$(md5sum "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}')
  if [ "$prc" -ne 0 ]; then
    log "🧩 PATCH" "Patch exited rc=$prc — restoring backup"
    cp -f "$backup" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
    chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
    tg_send "$(T patch_failed) (rc=$prc)"
    return 1
  fi
  if [ "$pre_hash" = "$post_hash" ]; then
    log "🧩 PATCH" "Patch reported success but script unchanged."
    tg_send "$(T patch_no_change)"
    return 1
  fi
  err=$(bash -n "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>&1)
  if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    log "🧩 PATCH" "Syntax broken — restoring backup"
    cp -f "$backup" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
    chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
    tg_send "$(T patch_syntax_fail)\n\n$(echo "$err" | tail -n 5)"
    return 1
  fi
  feats=$(validate_update_features "$SCRIPT_PATH")
  if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    log "🧩 PATCH" "Features missing:$feats — restoring"
    cp -f "$backup" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
    chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
    tg_send "$(T patch_failed)\nmissing:$feats"
    return 1
  fi
  save_creds_to_config
  rm -f "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" "$UPDATE_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  log "🧩 PATCH" "Patch applied successfully."
  tg_send "$(T patch_applied)"
  update_restart
}

install_update_from_file() {
  local src="$1" err self_out rc feats kind
  [ ! -s "$src" ] && { tg_send "$(T upd_file_empty)"; return 1; }
  kind=$(detect_patch_or_script "$src")
  case "$kind" in
    patch) log "🧩 PATCH" "Detected patch file."; tg_send "$(T patch_detected)"; apply_patch_file "$src"; return $? ;;
    script) log "📦 UPDATE" "Detected full script." ;;
    *) log_error E19 "not a farm script or patch"; tg_send "$(T patch_invalid)"; return 1 ;;
  esac
  err=$(bash -n "$src" 2>&1)
  if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then log_error E19 "syntax"; tg_send "$(error_block_tg E19 "syntax")"; return 1; fi
  feats=$(validate_update_features "$src")
  if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then log_error E19 "missing features:$feats"; tg_send "$(T upd_missing_features)\n\n$feats"; return 1; fi
  if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    self_out=$(FARM_QUICK_SELFTEST=1 timeout 120 bash "$src" --selftest 2>&1); rc=$?
  else
    self_out=$(FARM_QUICK_SELFTEST=1 bash "$src" --selftest 2>&1); rc=$?
  fi
  if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ]; then log_error E19 "selftest"; tg_send "$(error_block_tg E19 "selftest")"; return 1; fi
  save_creds_to_config
  save_current_version "pre-update" || { tg_send "$(T upd_backup_failed)"; return 1; }
  cp -f "$src" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null || { tg_send "$(T upd_write_failed)"; return 1; }
  chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
  rm -f "$AWAIT_UPDATE_FILE" "$UPDATE_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  log "📦 UPDATE" "New version installed."
  tg_send "$(T upd_verified_installed)"
  update_restart
}

rollback_by_index() {
  local idx="$1" -a files
  echo "$idx" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || return 1
  mapfile -t files < <(get_version_list)
  if [ "$idx" -lt 1 ] || [ "$idx" -gt "${#files[@]}" ]; then tg_send "$(T upd_version_missing)"; return 1; fi
  local file="${files[idx-1]}"
  [ ! -f "$file" ] && { tg_send "$(T upd_version_missing)"; return 1; }
  cp -f "$file" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null || { tg_send "$(T upd_rollback_write_failed)"; return 1; }
  chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
  log "↩️ ROLLBACK" "Rollback to $(read_backup_version "$file")"
  tg_send "$(T upd_rollback_done) $(read_backup_version "$file")"
  update_restart
}

handle_document() {
  local doc_id="$1" doc_name="$2" caption="$3" resp file_path attempt=0
  if ! update_allowed "$caption" "$doc_name"; then tg_send "$(T upd_first_upload)"; return 0; fi
  while [ $attempt -lt 3 ]; do
    attempt=$((attempt + 1))
    resp=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 8 -m 20 --get --data-urlencode "file_id=$doc_id" "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getFile" 2>/dev/null)
    file_path=$(echo "$resp" | jq -r '.result.file_path // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    [ -n "$file_path" ] && [ "$file_path" != "null" ] && break
    sleep 1
  done
  if [ -z "$file_path" ] || [ "$file_path" = "null" ]; then tg_send "$(T upd_getfile_failed)"; return 1; fi
  rm -f "$UPDATE_TMP" 2>/dev/null
  attempt=0
  while [ $attempt -lt 3 ]; do
    attempt=$((attempt + 1))
    curl -s -L --connect-timeout 10 -m 300 --retry 2 --retry-delay 2 -o "$UPDATE_TMP" "https://api.telegram.org/file/bot$TG_TOKEN/$file_path" 2>/dev/null
    [ -s "$UPDATE_TMP" ] && break
    sleep 2
  done
  if [ ! -s "$UPDATE_TMP" ]; then log_error E19 "download failed"; tg_send "$(T upd_download_failed)"; return 1; fi
  tg_send "$(T upd_received)"
  install_update_from_file "$UPDATE_TMP"
}

farm_export_send_document() {
  local file="$1" name="$2" caption="$3" resp
  [ -f "$file" ] || return 1
  resp=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 5 -m 120 \
    -F "chat_id=$TG_CHAT_ID" \
    -F "document=@${file};filename=${name}" \
    -F "caption=$caption" \
    "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/sendDocument" 2>/dev/null)
  echo "$resp" | grep -q '"ok":true'
}

export_current_version_to_telegram() {
  local src ts out_name tmp
  src="$SCRIPT_PATH"
  [ ! -f "$src" ] && src="$(realpath "$0" 2>/dev/null || readlink -f "$0" 2>/dev/null || echo "$HOME_DIR/farm_root.txt")"
  [ ! -f "$src" ] && { send_tg_plain "$(T upd_export_no_file)"; return 1; }
  ts=$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)
  out_name="farm_${FARM_VERSION:-FARM}_${ts}.txt"
  tmp="$STATE_DIR/export_tmp_$$.txt"
  cp -f "$src" "$tmp" 2>/dev/null || { send_tg_plain "$(T upd_export_prepare_failed)"; return 1; }
  if farm_export_send_document "$tmp" "$out_name" "📦 ${FARM_VERSION:-unknown}"; then
    send_tg_plain "$(T upd_export_sent) $out_name"
  else send_tg_plain "$(T upd_export_send_failed)"; fi
  rm -f "$tmp" 2>/dev/null
}

# ==============================================================================
# SELF DIAG
# ==============================================================================
run_self_diagnostics() {
  local mode="${1:-check}" report="" critical=0 dep out syn
  report="🩺 Diagnostics $FARM_VERSION | mode=$mode | $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n"
  for dep in bash curl jq grep sed awk; do
    if command -v "$dep" >/dev/null 2>&1; then report="${report}✅ $dep $(T diag_dep_found)\n"
    else
      if [ "$mode" = "fix" ]; then
        command -v pkg >/dev/null 2>&1 && pkg install -y "$dep" >/dev/null 2>&1
        command -v apt >/dev/null 2>&1 && apt install -y "$dep" >/dev/null 2>&1
      fi
      if command -v "$dep" >/dev/null 2>&1; then report="${report}🛠 $dep $(T diag_dep_installed)\n"
      else critical=1; report="${report}❌ $dep $(T diag_dep_missing) — E03\n"; log_error E03 "missing dependency: $dep"; fi
    fi
  done
  if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then report="${report}✅ timeout $(T diag_dep_found)\n"
  else
    [ "$mode" = "fix" ] && command -v pkg >/dev/null 2>&1 && pkg install -y coreutils >/dev/null 2>&1
    if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then report="${report}🛠 timeout $(T diag_dep_installed)\n"
    else report="${report}⚠️ timeout missing (optional)\n"; fi
  fi
  if [ -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" ]; then
    syn=$(bash -n "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>&1)
    if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then report="${report}$(T diag_syntax_ok)\n"
    else critical=1; report="${report}❌ E02 $(E E02 what)\n$syn\n"; log_error E02 "syntax"; fi
  else critical=1; report="${report}❌ E01 $(E E01 what) path=$SCRIPT_PATH\n"; log_error E01 "$SCRIPT_PATH"; fi
  if [ -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" ]; then
    local _mf
    _mf=$(validate_update_features "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -z "$_mf" ]; then report="${report}$(T diag_batch_ok)\n"
    else critical=1; report="${report}$(T diag_batch_no):$_mf\n"; log_error E19 "self:$_mf"; fi
  fi
  if touch "$STATE_DIR/diag_test" 2>/dev/null; then rm -f "$STATE_DIR/diag_test"; report="${report}$(T diag_state_writable)\n"
  else critical=1; report="${report}❌ STATE not writable\n"; fi
  if [ -n "$TG_TOKEN" ] && [ -n "$TG_CHAT_ID" ]; then report="${report}✅ TG_TOKEN & TG_CHAT_ID set\n"
  else critical=1; report="${report}❌ E04 $(E E04 what)\n"; log_error E04 "config missing"; fi
  if get_queue >/dev/null 2>&1; then report="${report}✅ Queue normalized\n"
  else report="${report}⚠️ E15 $(E E15 what)\n"; log_error E15; fi
  if [ "${FARM_QUICK_SELFTEST:-0}" = "1" ]; then
    report="${report}ℹ️ Quick mode: net, root, battery skipped\n"
  else
    out=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 4 -m 8 "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getMe" 2>/dev/null)
    if echo "$out" | grep -q '"ok":true'; then report="${report}$(T diag_tg_ok)\n"
    else report="${report}$(T diag_tg_fail) — E05\n"; fi
    if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then out=$(timeout 6 su -c id 2>/dev/null); else out=$(su -c id 2>/dev/null); fi
    if echo "$out" | grep -q 'uid=0'; then report="${report}$(T diag_root_ok)\n"
    else report="${report}$(T diag_root_no)\n"; fi
    if command -v input >/dev/null 2>&1 || [ -x /system/bin/input ]; then report="${report}$(T diag_input_ok)\n"
    else report="${report}$(T diag_input_no) — E08\n"; fi
    local bat ch
    bat=$(get_battery_level_force 2>/dev/null); ch=$(get_charging_status_force 2>/dev/null)
    if echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
      if [ "$ch" = "1" ]; then report="${report}$(T diag_bat_ok): ${bat}% $(T diag_charging)\n"
      else report="${report}$(T diag_bat_ok): ${bat}%\n"; fi
    else report="${report}$(T diag_bat_no)\n"; fi
  fi
  local pf pid fixed=0
  for pf in "$PID_FILE" "$CORE_PID_FILE" "$DASHBOARD_TICK_PID_FILE"; do
    pid=$(cat "$pf" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1)
    if [ -n "$pid" ] && ! kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then rm -f "$pf" 2>/dev/null; fixed=1; fi
  done
  [ "$fixed" = "1" ] && { report="${report}🛠 Stale PIDs removed — E17\n"; log_error E17; }
  printf '%b' "$report" > "$DIAG_REPORT"
  printf '%b' "$report"
  return "$critical"
}

# ==============================================================================
# LOW BATTERY
# ==============================================================================
LOW_BAT_COOLDOWN=120
check_low_battery() {
  local bat ch now last
  bat=$(try_battery_nonroot 2>/dev/null)
  if ! echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then bat=$(cat "$BAT_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n'); fi
  echo "$bat" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' || return 0
  [ "$bat" -gt 10 ] && return 0
  ch=$(try_charging_nonroot 2>/dev/null)
  [ -z "$ch" ] && ch=$(cat "$BAT_CHARGE_CACHE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  [ "$ch" = "1" ] && return 0
  now=$(date +%s)
  last=$(cat "$LOW_BAT_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$last" ] && last=0
  [ $((now - last)) -lt "$LOW_BAT_COOLDOWN" ] && return 0
  echo "$now" > "$LOW_BAT_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  log_error E16 "battery ${bat}%"
  tg_send "$(error_block_tg E16 "battery ${bat}%")"
}

# ==============================================================================
# MACRO RUNNER
# ==============================================================================
generate_macro_runner() {
  cat <<'RUNNER_HEAD' > "$MACRO_RUNNER"
export PATH=/system/bin:/system/xbin:/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin:$PATH
echo "Macro started at $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
REPEAT_DELAY=__REPEAT_DELAY__
LAST_OPENED_DOMAIN_FILE="__LAST_OPENED_FILE__"

wake_screen() {
  local state
  state=$(dumpsys power 2>/dev/null | grep -E "mWakefulness=" | head -n1)
  if echo "$state" | grep -q "Awake"; then return 0; fi
  termux-wake-up >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  input keyevent 224 >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  sleep 0.4
  wm dismiss-keyguard >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  sleep 0.3
}

check_text_in_input() {
  local needle="$1" dump="/sdcard/.farm_ui_dump.xml"
  rm -f "$dump" 2>/dev/null
  if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then timeout 6 uiautomator dump "$dump" >/dev/null 2>&1
  else uiautomator dump "$dump" >/dev/null 2>&1; fi
  [ -s "$dump" ] || return 1
  tr '>' '\n' < "$dump" | grep -iE 'EditText|editable="true"' | grep -F "text=\"$needle\"" >/dev/null 2>&1
}

send_command_to_bot() {
  local domain="$1" cmd_enc="$2" cmd_text="$3"
  local attempt=0 max=3 success=0 last_domain
  last_domain=$(cat "$LAST_OPENED_DOMAIN_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  while [ $attempt -lt $max ]; do
    attempt=$((attempt + 1))
    echo "TRY_RU: попытка $attempt для @$domain | EN: ATTEMPT=$attempt domain=@$domain"
    wake_screen
    if [ $attempt -gt 1 ] || [ "$last_domain" = "$domain" ]; then
      if [ "$last_domain" = "$domain" ]; then echo "REOPEN_RU: тот же бот @$domain | EN: SAME_BOT_RESTART"
      else echo "RETRY_RU: повтор @$domain | EN: RETRY_RESTART attempt=$attempt"; fi
      am force-stop "$TG_PKG" >/dev/null 2>&1
      sleep 1.0
    else echo "SWITCH_RU: другой бот @$domain | EN: BOT_SWITCH"; fi
    am start -a android.intent.action.VIEW -d "tg://resolve?domain=$domain&text=$cmd_enc" "$TG_PKG" >/dev/null 2>&1
    if [ "$?" != "0" ]; then echo "RETRY_RU: am start fail | EN: AM_START_FAILED"; sleep 0.8; continue; fi
    echo "$domain" > "$LAST_OPENED_DOMAIN_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    sleep 1.5
    local text_ok=0
    if check_text_in_input "$cmd_text"; then text_ok=1
    else
      sleep 0.8
      if check_text_in_input "$cmd_text"; then text_ok=1; fi
    fi
    if [ "$text_ok" = "1" ]; then echo "OK_RU: текст в поле | EN: TEXT_IN_INPUT"
    else
      echo "RETRY_RU: вводим вручную | EN: TEXT_NOT_FOUND_FALLBACK"
      local w h tx ty esc
      w=$(wm size 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*x[0-9]*' | head -n1 | cut -dx -f1); [ -z "$w" ] && w=1080
      h=$(wm size 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*x[0-9]*' | head -n1 | cut -dx -f2); [ -z "$h" ] && h=1920
      tx=$((w / 2)); ty=$((h - 150))
      input tap "$tx" "$ty" >/dev/null 2>&1; sleep 0.4
      esc=$(printf '%s' "$cmd_text" | sed 's/ /%s/g')
      input text "$esc" >/dev/null 2>&1; sleep 0.4
      if ! check_text_in_input "$cmd_text"; then echo "RETRY_RU: вставка fail | EN: TEXT_INSERT_RETRY"; sleep 0.5; continue; fi
      echo "OK_RU: введено вручную | EN: TEXT_MANUAL_VERIFIED"
    fi
    input keyevent 66 >/dev/null 2>&1; sleep 0.3
    input keyevent 66 >/dev/null 2>&1; sleep 0.5
    if check_text_in_input "$cmd_text"; then echo "RETRY_RU: текст остался | EN: TEXT_STILL_IN_INPUT_RETRY"; sleep 0.5; continue
    else
      success=1
      if [ "$attempt" -gt 1 ]; then echo "OK_RU: отправлено со $attempt-й | EN: COMMAND_SENT_AFTER_RETRY attempt=$attempt"
      else echo "OK_RU: отправлено с 1-й | EN: COMMAND_SENT"; fi
      break
    fi
  done
  if [ "$success" -ne 1 ]; then echo "FAIL_RU: fail @$domain | EN: COMMAND_SEND_FAILED attempts=$max"; return 1; fi
  return 0
}

TG_PKG=$(pm list packages 2>/dev/null | grep -i telegram | grep -v webview | head -n 1 | cut -d: -f2 | tr -d '\r' | tr -d ' ')
[ -z "$TG_PKG" ] && TG_PKG="org.telegram.messenger"
echo "Telegram package: $TG_PKG"
RUNNER_HEAD
  sed -i "s|__REPEAT_DELAY__|$BATCH_REPEAT_DELAY|g; s|__LAST_OPENED_FILE__|$LAST_OPENED_DOMAIN_FILE|g" "$MACRO_RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
  local pair bid batch domain cmd_enc cmd_text name bx
  for pair in "$@"; do
    bid="${pair%:*}"; batch="${pair#*:}"
    domain=$(get_bot_domain "$bid")
    cmd_text=$(get_batch_command "$bid" "$batch")
    if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      cmd_enc=$(jq -rn --arg s "$cmd_text" '$s|@uri|gsub("%2F";"/")' 2>/dev/null)
    else cmd_enc=$(printf '%s' "$cmd_text" | sed -e 's/ /%20/g' -e 's/#/%23/g' -e 's/&/%26/g' -e 's/?/%3F/g'); fi
    bx=$(get_batch_xcount "$bid" "$batch"); name=$(get_bot_name "$bid")
    cat <<RUNNER_BOT >> "$MACRO_RUNNER"
echo "=== Bot $name (@$domain) batch $batch x$bx ==="
BATCH_START_TS=\$(date +%s)
_bx_i=0
_batch_rc=0
while [ \$_bx_i -lt $bx ]; do
  _bx_i=\$((_bx_i + 1))
  send_command_to_bot "$domain" "$cmd_enc" "$cmd_text" || _batch_rc=1
  if [ \$_bx_i -lt $bx ]; then sleep \$REPEAT_DELAY; fi
done
BATCH_END_TS=\$(date +%s)
echo "BATCH_DURATION|bot=$bid|batch=$batch|domain=@$domain|seconds=\$((BATCH_END_TS - BATCH_START_TS))|rc=\$_batch_rc|xcount=$bx"
if [ "\$_batch_rc" = "0" ]; then echo "BATCH_SENT domain=@$domain batch=$batch attempt=1"
else echo "BATCH_FAILED domain=@$domain batch=$batch"; fi
RUNNER_BOT
  done
  cat <<'RUNNER_TAIL' >> "$MACRO_RUNNER"
echo "Macro finished at $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
RUNNER_TAIL
  chmod 755 "$MACRO_RUNNER"
}

farm_anim_disable() {
  [ -f "$HOME_DIR/.farm_disable_anim" ] || return 0
  command -v settings >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then
    settings put global window_animation_scale 0 2>/dev/null || true
    settings put global transition_animation_scale 0 2>/dev/null || true
    settings put global animator_duration_scale 0 2>/dev/null || true
  else
    su -c "settings put global window_animation_scale 0; settings put global transition_animation_scale 0; settings put global animator_duration_scale 0" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  fi
}
farm_anim_restore() {
  [ -f "$HOME_DIR/.farm_disable_anim" ] || return 0
  command -v settings >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then
    settings put global window_animation_scale 1 2>/dev/null || true
    settings put global transition_animation_scale 1 2>/dev/null || true
    settings put global animator_duration_scale 1 2>/dev/null || true
  else
    su -c "settings put global window_animation_scale 1; settings put global transition_animation_scale 1; settings put global animator_duration_scale 1" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  fi
}

# ==============================================================================
# EMERGENCY STOP
# ==============================================================================
poll_emergency_stop() {
  local offset updates max_id cb_id n i data
  offset=$(cat "$OFFSET_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1); [ -z "$offset" ] && offset=0
  updates=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 2 -m 3 "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getUpdates?offset=$offset&limit=10&timeout=0" 2>/dev/null)
  echo "$updates" | grep -q '"update_id"' || return 1
  command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 1
  max_id=$(echo "$updates" | jq -r '.result[-1].update_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
  [ -n "$max_id" ] && [ "$max_id" != "null" ] && echo $((max_id + 1)) > "$OFFSET_FILE"
  n=$(echo "$updates" | jq -r '.result | length' 2>/dev/null); [ -z "$n" ] && n=0
  for ((i=0; i<n; i++)); do
    data=$(echo "$updates" | jq -r ".result[$i].callback_query.data // empty" 2>/dev/null)
    cb_id=$(echo "$updates" | jq -r ".result[$i].callback_query.id // empty" 2>/dev/null)
    case "$data" in
      emergency_stop)
        [ -n "$cb_id" ] && [ "$cb_id" != "null" ] && tg_answer_cb "$cb_id" "🛑"
        send_emergency_stop_confirm ;;
      emergency_stop_yes)
        [ -n "$cb_id" ] && [ "$cb_id" != "null" ] && tg_answer_cb "$cb_id" "🛑"
        return 0 ;;
      emergency_stop_no)
        [ -n "$cb_id" ] && [ "$cb_id" != "null" ] && tg_answer_cb "$cb_id" "❌" ;;
    esac
  done
  return 1
}

reset_all_timers() {
  local now=$(date +%s) b batch int_v type
  for b in $(known_bot_ids); do
    for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do
      type=$(get_batch_type "$b" "$batch")
      if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then
        set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" "$(next_time_occurrence "$(get_batch_time "$b" "$batch")")"
      else
        int_v=$(get_batch_interval "$b" "$batch")
        set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((now + int_v))
      fi
    done
  done
}

kill_macro_process() {
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then pkill -9 -f "macro_exec.sh" 2>/dev/null
  else
    if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then timeout 5 su -c "pkill -9 -f macro_exec.sh" 2>/dev/null
    else su -c "pkill -9 -f macro_exec.sh" 2>/dev/null; fi
  fi
}

send_emergency_stop_confirm() {
  local word km
  word="$(T confirm_emergency_stop)"
  km=$(printf '{"inline_keyboard":[[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"emergency_stop_yes"},{"text":%s,"callback_data":"emergency_stop_no"}],[{"text":%s,"callback_data":"main_menu"}]]}' \
    "$(json_escape "$(T btn_yes)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_no)")" "$(json_escape "$(T btn_back)")")
  tg_send_with_km "$word" "$km"
}

emergency_stop_handler() {
  log "🛑 EMERGENCY" "Emergency stop — killing macro and resetting timers."
  kill_macro_process
  rm -f "$RUNNING_LOCK" "$MACRO_RUNNER" "$MACRO_BG_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  farm_anim_restore 2>/dev/null || true
  reset_all_timers
  tg_send "$(T emergency_stop_done)"
}

# ==============================================================================
# MACRO EXECUTION
# ==============================================================================
execute_macro() {
  [ "$#" -eq 0 ] && { log "⚠️" "No batches"; return 1; }
  if [ -f "$RUNNING_LOCK" ]; then
    local lock_ts cur_ts
    lock_ts=$(cat "$RUNNING_LOCK" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1); cur_ts=$(date +%s)
    if [ -n "$lock_ts" ] && [ $((cur_ts - lock_ts)) -lt 40 ]; then log "🔒" "Macro already running."; return 1; fi
  fi
  date +%s > "$RUNNING_LOCK"
  local start_now finish_now pair b batch old_t int_val bot_names_str type
  start_now=$(date +%s)
  for pair in "$@"; do
    b="${pair%:*}"; batch="${pair#*:}"
    old_t=$(get_batch_target "$b" "$batch")
    echo "$old_t" > "${PREV_TARGET_PREFIX}${b}_${batch}.txt" 2>/dev/null
    type=$(get_batch_type "$b" "$batch")
    if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((start_now + 90))
    else
      int_val=$(get_batch_interval "$b" "$batch")
      set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((start_now + int_val + 60))
    fi
  done
  bot_names_str=""
  for pair in "$@"; do bot_names_str="$bot_names_str ${pair}"; done
  log "🚀 MACRO" "Start collection for:$bot_names_str"
  : > "$MACRO_LOG"
  farm_anim_disable
  generate_macro_runner "$@"
  if [ "$(id -u 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then sh "$MACRO_RUNNER" >> "$MACRO_LOG" 2>&1 &
  else su -c "sh $MACRO_RUNNER" >> "$MACRO_LOG" 2>&1 & fi
  local MACRO_PID=$!
  echo "$MACRO_PID" > "$MACRO_BG_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  local stopped=0
  while kill -0 "$MACRO_PID" 2>/dev/null; do
    if poll_emergency_stop; then
      stopped=1; kill -9 "$MACRO_PID" 2>/dev/null; kill_macro_process; break
    fi
    sleep 1
  done
  wait "$MACRO_PID" 2>/dev/null
  local MACRO_RC=$?
  rm -f "$MACRO_BG_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  if [ "$stopped" = "1" ]; then
    log "🛑 EMERGENCY" "Macro aborted mid-execution by user."
    rm -f "$MACRO_RUNNER" "$RUNNING_LOCK" 2>/dev/null
    farm_anim_restore; reset_all_timers
    tg_send "$(T emergency_stop_done)"
    return 3
  fi
  rm -f "$MACRO_RUNNER"; farm_anim_restore
  generate_macro_report "$@"
  cat "$MACRO_LOG" >> "$LOG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  parse_root_power_tmp
  finish_now=$(date +%s)
  local failed_bots=""
  for pair in "$@"; do
    local bname bdom status="OK" rem
    b="${pair%:*}"; batch="${pair#*:}"
    bname=$(get_bot_name "$b"); bdom=$(get_bot_domain "$b")
    if grep -qE "BATCH_FAILED .*domain=@${bdom} .*batch=${batch}( |$)" "$MACRO_LOG" 2>/dev/null; then
      status="FAIL"; failed_bots="$failed_bots ${b}:${batch}"
      log_error E10 "bot @$bdom batch=$batch"
    fi
    log_collection "$bname[$batch]" "$bdom" "$status" "dur=$((finish_now - start_now))s"
    type=$(get_batch_type "$b" "$batch")
    rm -f "${PREV_TARGET_PREFIX}${b}_${batch}.txt" 2>/dev/null
    if [ "$status" = "FAIL" ]; then
      set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((finish_now + 300))
      log "🔁 RETRY" "Batch $b:$batch failed, retry in 5 min."
    else
      if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then
        local next_target
        next_target=$(next_time_occurrence "$(get_batch_time "$b" "$batch")")
        set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" "$next_target"
      else
        int_val=$(get_batch_interval "$b" "$batch")
        set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((finish_now + int_val))
      fi
      rem=$(get_batch_remaining "$b" "$batch")
      if [ "$rem" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
        rem=$((rem - 1)); update_batch_field "$b" "$batch" 7 "$rem"
        if [ "$rem" -le 0 ]; then
          log "🗑 XT" "Batch $b:$batch completed (X-times exhausted). Removing."
          delete_batch "$b" "$batch"
          tg_send "🗑 X-times batch ${batch} for $(get_bot_icon "$b") $(get_bot_name "$b") completed & removed."
        fi
      fi
    fi
  done
  rm -f "$RUNNING_LOCK"
  if [ -n "$failed_bots" ]; then log "✅ MACRO" "Done. Failed:$failed_bots"; return 2
  else log "✅ MACRO" "Done."; fi
  return 0
}

do_farm_full() {
  log "🌾 CYCLE" "Full cycle start..."
  if ! pre_macro_network_check; then log "🌐 NET" "Full cycle skipped."; tg_send "$(error_block_tg E05)"; return 1; fi
  local q enabled_pairs b batch
  q=$(get_queue); enabled_pairs=""
  for b in $q; do
    if is_bot_enabled "$b"; then
      for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do enabled_pairs="$enabled_pairs ${b}:${batch}"; done
    fi
  done
  enabled_pairs=$(echo "$enabled_pairs" | xargs 2>/dev/null)
  [ -z "$enabled_pairs" ] && { tg_send "$(T all_off)"; return; }
  set -- $enabled_pairs
  execute_macro "$@"
  local rc=$?
  [ "$rc" = "3" ] && return 3
  local per_bot; per_bot=$(build_bot_notify_block "$@")
  if [ "$rc" = "2" ]; then tg_send "$(T cycle_partial)\n\n${per_bot}\n📝 $(T btn_marolog)"
  else tg_send "$(T cycle_done)\n\n${per_bot}"; fi
}

do_farm_single() {
  local bot_id="$1" b_name int_str per_bot pair_list batch
  [ ! -d "$BOT_DIR/$bot_id" ] && { tg_send "$(T bot_not_found) ($bot_id)"; return; }
  if ! pre_macro_network_check; then log "🌐 NET" "Single run skipped."; tg_send "$(error_block_tg E05)"; return 1; fi
  b_name=$(get_bot_name "$bot_id")
  log "🎯 SELECT" "Single run: $b_name"
  pair_list=""
  for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$bot_id"); do pair_list="$pair_list ${bot_id}:${batch}"; done
  pair_list=$(echo "$pair_list" | xargs 2>/dev/null)
  [ -z "$pair_list" ] && { tg_send "$(T bot_not_found)"; return; }
  set -- $pair_list
  execute_macro "$@"
  local rc=$?
  [ "$rc" = "3" ] && return 3
  local first; first=$(get_bot_batches "$bot_id" | head -n1)
  int_str=$(format_seconds "$(get_batch_interval "$bot_id" "$first")")
  per_bot=$(build_bot_notify_block "$@")
  if [ "$rc" = "2" ]; then tg_send "⚠️ $(T single_done) $b_name: failed, retry in 5 min.\n\n${per_bot}"
  else tg_send "$(T single_done) $b_name\n\n${per_bot}\n$(T next_in) ${int_str}."; fi
}

# ==============================================================================
# CRASH HANDLING
# ==============================================================================
diagnose_crash() {
  local err="$1" tail_txt="$2" code="E06" wd_marker=0
  if [ -f "$WATCHDOG_KILL_MARKER" ]; then
    local mt now_ts
    mt=$(cat "$WATCHDOG_KILL_MARKER" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); now_ts=$(date +%s)
    [ -n "$mt" ] && [ $((now_ts - mt)) -lt 120 ] && wd_marker=1
  fi
  if [ "$wd_marker" = "1" ]; then rm -f "$WATCHDOG_KILL_MARKER" 2>/dev/null; echo "E06"; return 0; fi
  case "$err" in
    126) code="E01" ;; 127) code="E03" ;;
    137) code="E06" ;; 139) code="E06" ;; 143) code="E06" ;;
    124) code="E05" ;; 1) code="E05" ;;
  esac
  if echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'Permission Denial|not allowed|requires android.permission'; then code="E08"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'syntax error'; then code="E02"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'No space left on device'; then code="E26"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'termux-setup-storage|storage permission|/sdcard.*not found'; then code="E22"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'Too Many Requests|retry_after|429'; then code="E23"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'jq: error|parse error|invalid json'; then code="E21"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'tg_config|config.*corrupt'; then code="E24"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'batches.txt|bot.*corrupt'; then code="E25"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'su: not found|su: permission'; then code="E28"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'curl: \(6\)|Could not resolve host'; then code="E05"
  elif echo "$tail_txt" | grep -Eiq 'curl: \(7\)|Failed to connect'; then code="E05"
  fi
  echo "$code"
}

notify_crash() {
  local code="$1" exit_code="$2" tail_txt="$3" now last
  now=$(date +%s)
  last=$(cat "$CRASH_NOTIFY_TS" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$last" ] && last=0
  [ $((now - last)) -lt 120 ] && return 0
  send_tg_plain "$(crash_report_tg "$code" "$exit_code" "$tail_txt")"
  echo "$now" > "$CRASH_NOTIFY_TS" 2>/dev/null
}

crash_report_tg() {
  local code="$1" exit_code="$2" tail_txt="$3" sev sev_name
  sev=$(error_severity "$code"); sev_name=$(error_severity_name "$code")
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n🚨 CRASH REPORT · %s\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n' "$FARM_VERSION"
  printf '🕒 %s\n⚠️ Exit code: %s\n%s Severity: %s\n🆘 Code: %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$exit_code" "$sev" "$sev_name" "$code"
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n❓ %s\n\n🔎 %s\n🛠 %s\n' "$(E "$code" what)" "$(E "$code" why)" "$(E "$code" fix)"
  [ -n "$tail_txt" ] && printf '\n──────────────────────────────\n📄 Tail:\n%s\n' "$(printf '%s' "$tail_txt" | tail -n 6)"
  printf '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━'
}

crash_report_log() {
  local code="$1" exit_code="$2" tail_txt="$3" sev sev_name
  sev=$(error_severity "$code"); sev_name=$(error_severity_name "$code")
  printf '══════════════════════════════════════════════\n🚨 CRASH REPORT · %s · %s\n══════════════════════════════════════════════\n' "$FARM_VERSION" "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
  printf '⚠️ Exit code: %s\n🆘 Code: %s · %s %s\n❓ %s\n🔎 %s\n🛠 %s\n' "$exit_code" "$code" "$sev" "$sev_name" "$(E "$code" what)" "$(E "$code" why)" "$(E "$code" fix)"
  [ -n "$tail_txt" ] && printf '──────────────────────────────\n📄 Tail:\n%s\n' "$(printf '%s' "$tail_txt" | tail -n 10)"
  printf '══════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
}

# ==============================================================================
# MIGRATION
# ==============================================================================
migrate_old_data() {
  [ -f "$STATE_DIR/.migrated_v2914b" ] && return 0
  log "📦 MIGRATE" "Migrating old data..."
  if [ -d "$HOME_DIR/.farm_bots" ]; then
    for bd in "$HOME_DIR/.farm_bots"/*/; do
      [ -d "$bd" ] || continue
      local bid; bid=$(basename "$bd")
      mkdir -p "$BOT_DIR/$bid" 2>/dev/null
      for f in name icon domain cmd interval enabled upgradeable stock; do
        if [ -f "$bd/$f" ] && [ ! -f "$BOT_DIR/$bid/$f.txt" ]; then cp -f "$bd/$f" "$BOT_DIR/$bid/$f.txt" 2>/dev/null; fi
      done
    done
  fi
  if [ -f "$HOME_DIR/.farm_queue" ] && [ ! -f "$QUEUE_FILE" ]; then cp -f "$HOME_DIR/.farm_queue" "$QUEUE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; fi
  for f in "$HOME_DIR"/.farm_target_*; do
    [ -f "$f" ] || continue
    local id; id="${f##*_}"
    [ ! -f "${TARGET_PREFIX}${id}.txt" ] && cp -f "$f" "${TARGET_PREFIX}${id}.txt" 2>/dev/null
  done
  touch "$STATE_DIR/.migrated_v2914b" 2>/dev/null
}

# ==============================================================================
# UNIT SELFTEST
# ==============================================================================
run_unit_selftest() {
  local pass=0 fail=0 total=0
  local ok="✅" no="❌" fn r
  echo "🧪 UNIT SELFTEST · $FARM_VERSION"
  for fn in parse_interval_any parse_batches_spec get_bot_batches get_batch_interval get_batch_xcount get_batch_command set_bot_batches get_batch_target find_previous_safe_v2_version enter_safe_mode version_has_safe_mode_v2 build_safe_mode_text validate_update_features handle_document install_update_from_file rollback_by_index farm_export_send_document addbot_session_clear apply_patch_file detect_patch_or_script get_batch_type get_batch_time get_batch_remaining next_time_occurrence handle_addcmd_text handle_batch_edit_input session_check_timeout clear_all_sessions sorted_error_codes; do
    total=$((total + 1))
    if declare -f "$fn" >/dev/null 2>&1; then pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok $fn"
    else fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no $fn"; fi
  done
  total=$((total + 1))
  r=$(parse_interval_any "02:30"); [ "$r" = "9000" ] && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok 02:30"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no 02:30 got=$r"; }
  total=$((total + 1))
  r=$(parse_batches_spec "@14:30 /daily" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 | cut -d'|' -f5)
  [ "$r" = "time" ] && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok @time"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no @time got=$r"; }
  total=$((total + 1))
  r=$(parse_batches_spec "02:30 r5 /claim" 2>/dev/null | head -n1 | cut -d'|' -f7)
  [ "$r" = "5" ] && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok r5"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no r5 got=$r"; }
  total=$((total + 1))
  bash -n "$SCRIPT_PATH" >/dev/null 2>&1 && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok syntax"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no syntax"; }
  total=$((total + 1))
  grep -q 'FARM_VERSION="V2.9.2.10"' "$SCRIPT_PATH" && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok version"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no version"; }
  total=$((total + 1))
  grep -q "FARM_PATCH_V1" "$SCRIPT_PATH" && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok patch support"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no patch support"; }
  total=$((total + 1))
  grep -q "session_check_timeout" "$SCRIPT_PATH" && { pass=$((pass+1)); echo "$ok session timeout"; } || { fail=$((fail+1)); echo "$no session timeout"; }
  echo "Total: ${pass}/${total} passed, ${fail} failed"
  [ "$fail" -eq 0 ]
}

# ==============================================================================
# SAFE MODE LOOP
# ==============================================================================
safe_mode_main() {
  log "🛟 SAFE" "Safe mode core started. Language=$FARM_LANG"
  clear_all_sessions
  local OFFSET UPDATES UPD_ID TEXT CB CB_ID CB_MSG_ID DOC_ID DOC_NAME CAPTION INCOMING_CHAT_ID _mpid _CODE _pg _AV _ST _BID _BACT _BEXTRA _REST
  tg_safe_mode_menu
  while true; do
    date +%s > "$HEARTBEAT_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    sleep 1
    command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || { sleep 4; continue; }
    OFFSET=$(cat "$OFFSET_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1); [ -z "$OFFSET" ] && OFFSET=0
    UPDATES=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 4 -m 15 "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getUpdates?offset=$OFFSET&limit=1&timeout=10" 2>/dev/null)
    echo "$UPDATES" | grep -q '"ok":true' || { sleep 2; continue; }
