    echo "$UPDATES" | grep -q '"update_id"' || continue
    UPD_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].update_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    TEXT=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.text // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CB=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].callback_query.data // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CB_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].callback_query.id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CB_MSG_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].callback_query.message.message_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    DOC_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.document.file_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    DOC_NAME=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.document.file_name // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CAPTION=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.caption // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    [ -n "$UPD_ID" ] && [ "$UPD_ID" != "null" ] && echo $((UPD_ID + 1)) > "$OFFSET_FILE"
    INCOMING_CHAT_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.chat.id // .result[0].callback_query.from.id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -n "$INCOMING_CHAT_ID" ] && [ "$INCOMING_CHAT_ID" != "null" ] && [ "$INCOMING_CHAT_ID" != "$TG_CHAT_ID" ]; then
      log_error E18 "safe chat_id=$INCOMING_CHAT_ID"
      TEXT=""; CB=""; CB_ID=""; CB_MSG_ID=""; DOC_ID=""; DOC_NAME=""; CAPTION=""
    fi
    [ -n "$CB_ID" ] && [ "$CB_ID" != "null" ] && tg_answer_cb "$CB_ID" "🛟"
    if [ -n "$DOC_ID" ] && [ "$DOC_ID" != "null" ]; then handle_document "$DOC_ID" "$DOC_NAME" "$CAPTION"; continue; fi
    if [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ]; then
      log "📥 SAFE CMD" "$TEXT"
      case "$TEXT" in
        /start|/menu|/safe) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); [ -n "$_mpid" ] && tg_edit_safe_mode_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /update) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_update_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /versions) tg_versions_menu ;;
        /macrolog) echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /logs) echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_logs_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /errhelp) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_error_help_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /exp) export_current_version_to_telegram ;;
        /exit_safe) clear_safe_mode_state; log "🛟 SAFE" "Exit safe mode — restart."; tg_send "$(T safe_mode_exited)"; sleep 1; exit 77 ;;
        *) tg_safe_mode_menu ;;
      esac
    fi
    if [ -n "$CB" ] && [ "$CB" != "null" ]; then
      case "$CB" in
        safe_update) tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        safe_macrolog) echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        safe_logs) echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        safe_errhelp) tg_edit_error_help_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        safe_exit) clear_safe_mode_state; tg_send "$(T safe_mode_exited)"; sleep 1; exit 77 ;;
        err_show_*) _CODE="${CB#err_show_}"; tg_error_detail "$CB_MSG_ID" "$_CODE" ;;
        menu_update) tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        update_upload) set_update_await; tg_send "$(build_update_upload_help)"; tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        update_save) if save_current_version "safe-manual-save"; then tg_send "$(T saved)"; else tg_send "$(T save_failed)"; fi; tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        update_versions) tg_versions_menu ;;
        versions_older) _pg=$(cat "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; echo $((_pg+1)) > "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_versions_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        versions_newer) _pg=$(cat "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; [ "$_pg" -gt 0 ] && _pg=$((_pg-1)); echo "$_pg" > "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_versions_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        exp_tg) export_current_version_to_telegram; tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        rbidx_*) _CODE="${CB#rbidx_}"; rollback_by_index "$_CODE" ;;
        get_logs) echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        logs_older) _pg=$(cat "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; echo $((_pg+1)) > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        logs_newer) _pg=$(cat "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; [ "$_pg" -gt 0 ] && _pg=$((_pg-1)); echo "$_pg" > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_crash) : > "$CRASH_LOG" 2>/dev/null; echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        get_macro_log) echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        macro_page_newer) _pg=$(cat "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; [ "$_pg" -gt 0 ] && _pg=$((_pg-1)); echo "$_pg" > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        macro_page_older) _pg=$(cat "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; echo $((_pg+1)) > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_macro_report) : > "$MACRO_REPORT" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_collections) : > "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null; echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_macro_all) : > "$MACRO_REPORT" 2>/dev/null; : > "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null; echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        main_menu) tg_edit_safe_mode_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        *) tg_edit_safe_mode_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
      esac
    fi
  done
}

# ==============================================================================
# ENTRY POINT
# ==============================================================================
# Auto-prepare Termux environment if missing core utilities
for _dep in curl jq; do
  if ! command -v "$_dep" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    if command -v pkg >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      pkg install -y "$_dep" >/dev/null 2>&1
    elif command -v apt >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      apt install -y "$_dep" >/dev/null 2>&1
    fi
  fi
done

if [ "$1" = "--selftest" ]; then load_language; load_credentials 2>/dev/null || true; run_self_diagnostics check; exit $?; fi
if [ "$1" = "--unit-test" ] || [ "$1" = "--unit" ]; then load_language; run_unit_selftest; exit $?; fi
if [ "$1" = "--version" ]; then echo "$FARM_VERSION"; exit 0; fi

load_language
load_credentials
migrate_old_data
init_bot_registry >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
migrate_all_bots_to_batches

# ==============================================================================
# WATCHDOG
# ==============================================================================
if [ "$1" != "--core" ]; then
  kill_old_instances
  clear_all_sessions
  echo $$ > "$PID_FILE"
  make_immortal
  log "🐕 WATCHDOG" "Started (PID: $$), $FARM_VERSION"
  echo "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S') [🐕 WATCHDOG] PID: $$" >> "$CRASH_LOG"
  cleanup_watchdog() {
    [ -n "${CORE_PID:-}" ] && kill -9 "$CORE_PID" 2>/dev/null
    rm -f "$PID_FILE" "$CORE_PID_FILE" "$RUNNING_LOCK" "$DASHBOARD_TICK_PID_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    exit 0
  }
  trap cleanup_watchdog INT TERM
  while true; do
    if [ ! -f "$SCRIPT_PATH" ]; then
      latest=$(get_version_list | head -n1)
      if [ -n "$latest" ]; then cp -f "$latest" "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null; chmod 700 "$SCRIPT_PATH" 2>/dev/null
      else log_error E01; send_tg_plain "$(error_block_tg E01)"; exit 1; fi
    fi
    if [ -x "/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash" ]; then BASH_BIN="/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash"
    else BASH_BIN="bash"; fi
    [ -f "$SAFE_MODE_FILE" ] && export FARM_SAFE=1 || export FARM_SAFE=0
    sleep "$CORE_BOOT_DELAY"
    "$BASH_BIN" "$SCRIPT_PATH" --core >> "$CORE_OUT" 2>&1 &
    CORE_PID=$!
    echo "$CORE_PID" > "$CORE_PID_FILE"
    date +%s > "$HEARTBEAT_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    while kill -0 "$CORE_PID" 2>/dev/null; do
      sleep "$WATCHDOG_TICK"
      hb_ts=""
      [ -f "$HEARTBEAT_FILE" ] && read -r hb_ts < "$HEARTBEAT_FILE" 2>/dev/null
      hb_ts="${hb_ts//[^0-9]/}"
      now_ts=${EPOCHSECONDS:-$(date +%s)}
      if [ -n "$hb_ts" ] && [ "$hb_ts" -gt 0 ] && [ $((now_ts - hb_ts)) -gt "$HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT" ]; then
        lock_ts=""
        [ -f "$RUNNING_LOCK" ] && read -r lock_ts < "$RUNNING_LOCK" 2>/dev/null
        lock_ts="${lock_ts//[^0-9]/}"
        if [ -n "$lock_ts" ] && [ $((now_ts - lock_ts)) -lt 350 ]; then continue; fi
        log_error E06 "core PID=$CORE_PID hung $((now_ts - hb_ts))s"
        echo "$now_ts" > "$WATCHDOG_KILL_MARKER"
        kill -9 "$CORE_PID" 2>/dev/null
        sleep 1; break
      fi
    done
    wait "$CORE_PID" 2>/dev/null
    ERR=$?
    if [ "$ERR" -eq 0 ]; then rm -f "$FAIL_COUNT_FILE" "$FAIL_TS_FILE"; cleanup_watchdog; fi
    if [ "$ERR" -eq 77 ]; then rm -f "$FAIL_COUNT_FILE" "$FAIL_TS_FILE"; log "🔁 RESTART" "Restart requested."; exec "$BASH_BIN" "$SCRIPT_PATH"; fi
    [ "$ERR" -eq 130 ] && cleanup_watchdog
    NOW_CRASH=$(date +%s)
    LAST_CRASH_TS=$(cat "$FAIL_TS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$LAST_CRASH_TS" ] && LAST_CRASH_TS=0
    if [ $((NOW_CRASH - LAST_CRASH_TS)) -gt 600 ]; then FAIL_COUNT=1
    else FAIL_COUNT=$(( $(cat "$FAIL_COUNT_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1 || echo 0) + 1 )); fi
    echo "$FAIL_COUNT" > "$FAIL_COUNT_FILE"
    echo "$NOW_CRASH" > "$FAIL_TS_FILE"
    TAIL_TXT=$(tail -n 25 "$CORE_OUT" 2>/dev/null)
    CRASH_CODE=$(diagnose_crash "$ERR" "$TAIL_TXT")
    crash_report_log "$CRASH_CODE" "$ERR" "$TAIL_TXT" >> "$CRASH_LOG"
    notify_crash "$CRASH_CODE" "$ERR" "$TAIL_TXT"
    if [ "$FAIL_COUNT" -ge "$SAFE_MODE_TRIGGER_COUNT" ]; then
      enter_safe_mode "$CRASH_CODE"
      SAFE_RC=$?
      if [ "$SAFE_RC" = "77" ]; then rm -f "$FAIL_COUNT_FILE" "$FAIL_TS_FILE"; exec "$BASH_BIN" "$SCRIPT_PATH"; fi
    fi
    sleep 3
  done
  exit 0
fi

# ==============================================================================
# CORE
# ==============================================================================
START_TIME=$(date +%s)
echo $$ > "$CORE_PID_FILE"

clear_all_sessions

if [ -f "$SAFE_MODE_FILE" ] && [ -f "$SAFE_MODE_LANG_FILE" ]; then
  _sl=$(cat "$SAFE_MODE_LANG_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
  if [ "$_sl" = "EN" ] || [ "$_sl" = "RU" ]; then FARM_LANG="$_sl"; fi
fi

init_bot_registry
migrate_all_bots_to_batches
trap core_cleanup INT TERM

if [ -f "$SAFE_MODE_FILE" ]; then
  log "🚀 START" "Core $FARM_VERSION started in SAFE MODE."
  safe_mode_main
  exit 0
fi

log "🚀 START" "Core $FARM_VERSION started."
tg_send "$(T core_started) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL).\n📁 $DATA_ROOT\n$(T auto_features_on)"
tg_menu
REPORT_INTERVAL=$(cat "$STATE_INTERVAL" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1)
[ -z "$REPORT_INTERVAL" ] && REPORT_INTERVAL=$DEFAULT_REPORT_INTERVAL
if [ "$REPORT_INTERVAL" -lt 60 ]; then REPORT_INTERVAL=60; log_error E20; fi
NEXT_REPORT=$(( $(date +%s) + REPORT_INTERVAL ))
for b in $(known_bot_ids); do
  for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do get_batch_target "$b" "$batch" >/dev/null; done
done
log "🚀 CORE" "Engine started ($FARM_VERSION) — clean log mode" 

sleep 1
tg_status_menu

while true; do
  LOOP_TS=$(date +%s); NOW=$LOOP_TS
  date +%s > "$HEARTBEAT_FILE" 2>/dev/null
  LOOP_COUNTER=$((LOOP_COUNTER + 1))

  check_autostart_resume
  check_low_battery

  session_check_timeout "$ADDBOT_SESSION_DIR" "Add-bot"
  session_check_timeout "$ADDCMD_SESSION_DIR" "Commands wizard"
  check_single_input_timeout

  PROCESSED_UPDATE=0
  POLL_WAIT=$POLL_MAX_WAIT

  q_pre=$(get_queue)
  for b in $q_pre; do
    if is_bot_enabled "$b"; then
      for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do
        tgt_pre=$(get_batch_target "$b" "$batch")
        diff_pre=$((tgt_pre - NOW)); [ "$diff_pre" -lt 0 ] && diff_pre=0
        [ "$diff_pre" -lt "$POLL_WAIT" ] && POLL_WAIT=$diff_pre
      done
    fi
  done
  if echo "$NEXT_REPORT" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
    rep_diff=$((NEXT_REPORT - NOW)); [ "$rep_diff" -lt 0 ] && rep_diff=0
    [ "$rep_diff" -lt "$POLL_WAIT" ] && POLL_WAIT=$rep_diff
  fi
  [ "$POLL_WAIT" -lt 1 ] && POLL_WAIT=1
  [ "$POLL_WAIT" -gt "$POLL_MAX_WAIT" ] && POLL_WAIT=$POLL_MAX_WAIT

  if ! is_farm_paused; then
    q=$(get_queue); due_pairs=""
    for b in $q; do
      if is_bot_enabled "$b"; then
        for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do
          tgt=$(get_batch_target "$b" "$batch")
          [ "$NOW" -ge "$tgt" ] && due_pairs="$due_pairs ${b}:${batch}"
        done
      fi
    done
    due_pairs=$(echo "$due_pairs" | xargs 2>/dev/null)
    set -- $due_pairs
    if [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; then
      if pre_macro_network_check; then
        log "⏰ TIMER" "Collection time for:$*"
        execute_macro "$@"
        local_rc=$?
        if [ "$local_rc" != "1" ] && [ "$local_rc" != "3" ]; then
          per_bot_local=$(build_bot_notify_block "$@")
          if [ "$local_rc" = "2" ]; then tg_send "$(T auto_collect_fail)$per_bot_local"
          else tg_send "$(T auto_collect_done)$per_bot_local"; fi
        fi
      else
        log "🌐 NET" "Auto-collect skipped: no Telegram. +5min."
        log_error E14
        for pair in "$@"; do
          b="${pair%:*}"; batch="${pair#*:}"
          set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((NOW + 300))
        done
      fi
      sleep 1; continue
    fi
  fi

  if [ "$NOW" -ge "$NEXT_REPORT" ]; then
    generate_report
    REPORT_INTERVAL=$(cat "$STATE_INTERVAL" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1)
    [ -z "$REPORT_INTERVAL" ] && REPORT_INTERVAL=$DEFAULT_REPORT_INTERVAL
    [ "$REPORT_INTERVAL" -lt 60 ] && REPORT_INTERVAL=60
    NEXT_REPORT=$((NOW + REPORT_INTERVAL))
  fi

  if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then sleep "$POLL_WAIT"; continue; fi

  UPDATES=""; TEXT=""; CB=""; CB_ID=""; CB_MSG_ID=""; DOC_ID=""; DOC_NAME=""; CAPTION=""
  OFFSET=$(cat "$OFFSET_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]*' | head -n 1); [ -z "$OFFSET" ] && OFFSET=0
  UPDATES=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 4 -m $((POLL_WAIT + 10)) "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getUpdates?offset=$OFFSET&limit=1&timeout=$POLL_WAIT" 2>/dev/null)
  echo "$UPDATES" | grep -q '"ok":true' || {
    UPDATES=$(curl -s --connect-timeout 4 -m $((POLL_WAIT + 10)) "https://api.telegram.org/bot$TG_TOKEN/getUpdates?offset=$OFFSET&limit=1&timeout=$POLL_WAIT" 2>/dev/null)
  }
  if ! echo "$UPDATES" | grep -q '"ok":true'; then
    NET_FAIL_COUNT=$((NET_FAIL_COUNT + 1))
    [ "$NET_FAIL_COUNT" -eq 3 ] && { send_tg_plain "$(T network_problem)"; log_error E05 "poll failed $NET_FAIL_COUNT times"; }
    sleep 2; continue
  fi
  NET_FAIL_COUNT=0
  if [ -n "$UPDATES" ] && echo "$UPDATES" | grep -q '"update_id"'; then
    UPD_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].update_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    TEXT=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.text // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CB=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].callback_query.data // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CB_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].callback_query.id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CB_MSG_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].callback_query.message.message_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    DOC_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.document.file_id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    DOC_NAME=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.document.file_name // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    CAPTION=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.caption // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    INCOMING_CHAT_ID=$(echo "$UPDATES" | jq -r '.result[0].message.chat.id // .result[0].callback_query.from.id // empty' 2>/dev/null)
    [ -n "$UPD_ID" ] && [ "$UPD_ID" != "null" ] && { echo $((UPD_ID + 1)) > "$OFFSET_FILE"; PROCESSED_UPDATE=1; }
    if [ -n "$INCOMING_CHAT_ID" ] && [ "$INCOMING_CHAT_ID" != "null" ] && [ "$INCOMING_CHAT_ID" != "$TG_CHAT_ID" ]; then
      log_error E18 "chat_id=$INCOMING_CHAT_ID"
      TEXT=""; CB=""; CB_ID=""; CB_MSG_ID=""; DOC_ID=""; DOC_NAME=""; CAPTION=""
    fi
    [ -n "$CB_ID" ] && [ "$CB_ID" != "null" ] && tg_answer_cb "$CB_ID" "$(T running)"
    if [ -n "$DOC_ID" ] && [ "$DOC_ID" != "null" ]; then handle_document "$DOC_ID" "$DOC_NAME" "$CAPTION"; continue; fi
    [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ] && log "📥 CMD" "$TEXT"

    # ---- Add-bot wizard ----
    if [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ] && addbot_session_exists; then
      _step=$(addbot_get step)
      case "$TEXT" in
        /cancel) addbot_session_clear; tg_send "$(T addbot_cancelled)" ;;
        *)
          if [ "$_step" = "batch_cmd" ]; then
            handle_addbot_text_input "$TEXT"
          else
            case "$TEXT" in
              /start|/menu) addbot_session_clear; _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_main_menu "$_mpid" ;;
              /errhelp) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_error_help_menu "$_mpid" ;;
              /status) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); echo "$_mpid" > "$STATUS_MSG_ID_FILE"; tg_edit_status_menu "$_mpid" ;;
              /exp) export_current_version_to_telegram ;;
              /*) log "❓ CMD" "Ignored during addbot ($_step): $TEXT" ;;
              *) handle_addbot_text_input "$TEXT" ;;
            esac
          fi ;;
      esac
    # ---- Commands wizard ----
    elif [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ] && addcmd_session_exists; then
      _step=$(addcmd_get step)
      case "$TEXT" in
        /cancel)
          _BID=$(addcmd_get bot); addcmd_session_reset
          if [ -n "$_BID" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$_BID" ]; then tg_bot_cfg_menu "$_BID"; else tg_menu; fi ;;
        *)
          if [ "$_step" = "cmd" ] || [ "$_step" = "confirm" ]; then handle_addcmd_text "$TEXT"
          else
            case "$TEXT" in
              /*) log "❓ CMD" "Ignored during addcmd ($_step): $TEXT" ;;
              *) handle_addcmd_text "$TEXT" ;;
            esac
          fi ;;
      esac
    # ---- Single-value input ----
    elif [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ] && [ -f "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" ]; then
      _ST=$(cat "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" 2>/dev/null | head -n1)
      rm -f "$BOT_INPUT_STATE" "$BOT_INPUT_STATE.ts" 2>/dev/null
      _BID="${_ST%%:*}"; _REST="${_ST#*:}"; _BACT="${_REST%%:*}"; _BEXTRA="${_REST#*:}"
      [ "$_BACT" = "$_REST" ] && _BEXTRA=""
      case "$_BACT" in
        cbcmd|cbtime|cbx|cbrem) handle_batch_edit_input "$_BID" "$_BACT" "$_BEXTRA" "$TEXT" ;;
        *)
          if [ "$_BID" = "sys" ]; then handle_system_input "$_BACT" "$TEXT"
          else handle_bot_input "$_BID" "$_BACT" "$TEXT"; fi ;;
      esac
    # ---- Free-text time while bot cfg view ----
    elif [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ] && [ -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" ] && [ "${TEXT#/}" = "$TEXT" ]; then
      _BCV=$(cat "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \r\n')
      if [ -n "$_BCV" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$_BCV" ]; then
        _NEW=$(parse_interval_any "$TEXT")
        if [ "$_NEW" -ge 60 ] 2>/dev/null; then
          _now=$(date +%s)
          for _batch in $(get_bot_batches "$_BCV"); do set_batch_target "$_BCV" "$_batch" $((_now + _NEW)); done
          set_selected_timer_bot "$_BCV"
          tg_send "⏳ $(get_bot_icon "$_BCV") $(get_bot_name "$_BCV"): $(T delayed_by) $(format_seconds "$_NEW") $(T one_cycle)"
          tg_bot_cfg_menu "$_BCV"
        else
          tg_send "$(T invalid_interval_examples)"; tg_bot_cfg_menu "$_BCV"
        fi
      else rm -f "$BOT_CFG_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; fi
    # ---- Regular commands ----
    elif [ -n "$TEXT" ] && [ "$TEXT" != "null" ]; then
      case "$TEXT" in
        /start|/menu) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_main_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /status) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); echo "$_mpid" > "$STATUS_MSG_ID_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_status_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /errhelp) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_error_help_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /farm) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); do_farm_full; [ -n "$_mpid" ] && tg_edit_text_km "$_mpid" "$(T done) ($FARM_VERSION_FULL)" "$(build_back_keyboard)" ;;
        /farm[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/farm\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p')
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then do_farm_single "$BOT_NUM"; else tg_send "$(T bot_not_found) ($BOT_NUM)"; fi ;;
        /bots) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_bots_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /queue) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_queue_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /run) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_run_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /macrolog) echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /logs) echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_logs_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /update) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_update_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /versions) tg_versions_menu ;;
        /language) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_lang_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /autostart) _mpid=$(tg_send_progress_get_id); tg_edit_autostart_menu "$_mpid" ;;
        /pause) touch "$PAUSE_FILE"; mark_pause_ts; tg_send "$(T paused_msg)" ;;
        /start_farm) rm -f "$PAUSE_FILE"; clear_pause_ts; tg_send "$(T resumed_msg)" ;;
        /saveversion) if save_current_version "manual-save"; then tg_send "$(T saved)"; else tg_send "$(T save_failed)"; fi ;;
        /addbot) addbot_start ;;
        "/addbot "*) add_bot_from_text "$TEXT" ;;
        /res)
          for b in $(known_bot_ids); do
            for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$b"); do
              int_v="" type=""
              type=$(get_batch_type "$b" "$batch")
              if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" "$(next_time_occurrence "$(get_batch_time "$b" "$batch")")"
              else int_v=$(get_batch_interval "$b" "$batch"); set_batch_target "$b" "$batch" $((NOW + int_v)); fi
            done
          done
          tg_send "$(T timers_reset_all)" ;;
        /res[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/res\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p')
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$BOT_NUM"); do
              int_v="" type=""
              type=$(get_batch_type "$BOT_NUM" "$batch")
              if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then set_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch" "$(next_time_occurrence "$(get_batch_time "$BOT_NUM" "$batch")")"
              else int_v=$(get_batch_interval "$BOT_NUM" "$batch"); set_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch" $((NOW + int_v)); fi
            done
            set_selected_timer_bot "$BOT_NUM"
            tg_send "$(T timer_reset_for) $(get_bot_icon "$BOT_NUM") $(get_bot_name "$BOT_NUM") $(T reset_word)."
          else tg_send "$(T bot_not_found)"; fi ;;
        /rep\ *)
          VAL=$(echo "$TEXT" | awk '{print $2}'); NEW_REP=""
          NEW_REP=$(parse_interval_any "$VAL")
          if [ "$NEW_REP" -ge 60 ]; then
            echo "$NEW_REP" > "$STATE_INTERVAL"; REPORT_INTERVAL=$NEW_REP; NEXT_REPORT=$((NOW + NEW_REP))
            tg_send "$(T report_interval_set) $(format_seconds "$NEW_REP")."
          else tg_send "$(T hint_rep)"; fi ;;
        /interval[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/interval\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p'); TIME_RAW=$(echo "$TEXT" | awk '{print $2}')
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -n "$TIME_RAW" ]; then set_interval_for_bot "$BOT_NUM" "$TIME_RAW"
          else tg_send "$(T hint_interval_id)"; fi ;;
        /time[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/time\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p'); TIME_RAW=$(echo "$TEXT" | awk '{print $2}')
          NEW_WAIT=$(parse_interval_any "$TIME_RAW")
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ] && [ "$NEW_WAIT" -ge 60 ]; then
            set_selected_timer_bot "$BOT_NUM"
            for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$BOT_NUM"); do set_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch" $((NOW + NEW_WAIT)); done
            tg_send "⏳ $(get_bot_icon "$BOT_NUM") $(get_bot_name "$BOT_NUM") $(T delayed_by) $(format_seconds "$NEW_WAIT") $(T one_cycle)"
          else tg_send "$(T hint_time_id)"; fi ;;
        /delbot[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/delbot\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p')
          if [ -n "$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then request_or_do_bot_action "$BOT_NUM" delete ""
          else tg_send "$(T hint_delbot)"; fi ;;
        /botname[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/botname\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p'); _VAL=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/botname[0-9]\+[[:space:]]*||p')
          if [ -n "$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            [ -n "$_VAL" ] && request_or_do_bot_action "$BOT_NUM" name "$_VAL" || tg_send "$(T hint_botname)"
          else tg_send "$(T hint_botname)"; fi ;;
        /botcmd[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/botcmd\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p'); _VAL=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/botcmd[0-9]\+[[:space:]]*||p')
          if [ -n "$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            [ -n "$_VAL" ] && request_or_do_bot_action "$BOT_NUM" cmd "$_VAL" || tg_send "$(T hint_botcmd)"
          else tg_send "$(T hint_botcmd)"; fi ;;
        /botuser[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/botuser\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p'); _VAL=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/botuser[0-9]\+[[:space:]]*||p')
          if [ -n "$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            [ -n "$_VAL" ] && request_or_do_bot_action "$BOT_NUM" user "$_VAL" || tg_send "$(T hint_botuser)"
          else tg_send "$(T hint_botuser)"; fi ;;
        /boticon[0-9]*)
          BOT_NUM=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/boticon\([0-9]\+\).*|\1|p'); _VAL=$(echo "$TEXT" | sed -n 's|^/boticon[0-9]\+[[:space:]]*||p')
          if [ -n "$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            [ -n "$_VAL" ] && request_or_do_bot_action "$BOT_NUM" icon "$_VAL" || tg_send "$(T hint_boticon)"
          else tg_send "$(T hint_boticon)"; fi ;;
        /exp) export_current_version_to_telegram ;;
        *) log "❓ CMD" "Unknown text command: $TEXT" ;;
      esac
    fi

    if [ -n "$CB" ] && [ "$CB" != "null" ]; then
      case "$CB" in
        main_menu) tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_errhelp) tg_edit_error_help_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        err_show_*) _CODE="${CB#err_show_}"; tg_error_detail "$CB_MSG_ID" "$_CODE" ;;
        menu_lang) tg_edit_lang_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        set_lang_RU) set_language "RU"; tg_edit_lang_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        set_lang_EN) set_language "EN"; tg_edit_lang_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_run) tg_edit_run_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_bots|menu_timers) tg_edit_bots_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_queue) tg_edit_queue_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_reports) tg_edit_reports_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_addbot) addbot_start ;;
        menu_update) tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        menu_autostart) tg_edit_autostart_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        autostart_enable) set_autostart_enabled 1; is_farm_paused && mark_pause_ts; tg_send "$(T autostart_enabled_msg)"; tg_edit_autostart_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        autostart_disable) set_autostart_enabled 0; clear_pause_ts; tg_send "$(T autostart_disabled_msg)"; tg_edit_autostart_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        autostart_set_*)
          _AV="${CB#autostart_set_}"
          if echo "$_AV" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
            [ "$_AV" -lt 60 ] && _AV=60
            set_autostart_interval "$_AV"
            tg_send "$(T autostart_interval_set) $(format_seconds "$_AV")."
            tg_edit_autostart_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"
          fi ;;
        autostart_input) request_autostart_input ;;
        emergency_stop) send_emergency_stop_confirm ;;
        emergency_stop_yes) emergency_stop_handler; tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        emergency_stop_no) tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        update_upload) set_update_await; tg_send "$(build_update_upload_help)"; tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        update_save) if save_current_version "manual-save"; then tg_send "$(T saved)"; else tg_send "$(T save_failed)"; fi; tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        update_versions) tg_versions_menu ;;
        versions_older) _pg=$(cat "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; echo $((_pg+1)) > "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_versions_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        versions_newer) _pg=$(cat "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; [ "$_pg" -gt 0 ] && _pg=$((_pg-1)); echo "$_pg" > "$VERSIONS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_versions_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        get_status|refresh_status) [ -n "$CB_MSG_ID" ] && echo "$CB_MSG_ID" > "$STATUS_MSG_ID_FILE"; tg_edit_status_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        get_logs) echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        logs_older) _pg=$(cat "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; echo $((_pg+1)) > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        logs_newer) _pg=$(cat "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; [ "$_pg" -gt 0 ] && _pg=$((_pg-1)); echo "$_pg" > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_crash) : > "$CRASH_LOG" 2>/dev/null; echo 0 > "$LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_logs_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        get_macro_log) echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        macro_page_newer) _pg=$(cat "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; [ "$_pg" -gt 0 ] && _pg=$((_pg-1)); echo "$_pg" > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        macro_page_older) _pg=$(cat "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '^[0-9]*' | head -n1); [ -z "$_pg" ] && _pg=0; echo $((_pg+1)) > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_macro_report) : > "$MACRO_REPORT" 2>/dev/null; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_collections) : > "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null; echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        clear_macro_all) : > "$MACRO_REPORT" 2>/dev/null; : > "$COLLECTIONS_TODAY" 2>/dev/null; echo 0 > "$MACRO_LOGS_PAGE_FILE"; tg_edit_macro_log_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        force_full_farm) do_farm_full; tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        farm_start) rm -f "$PAUSE_FILE"; clear_pause_ts; log "▶️ START" "Farm started."; tg_send "$(T resumed_msg)"; tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        farm_stop) touch "$PAUSE_FILE"; mark_pause_ts; log "⏸ PAUSE" "Farm stopped."; tg_send "$(T paused_msg)"; tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        run_bot_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#run_bot_}"; if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then do_farm_single "$BOT_NUM"; tg_edit_run_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"; fi ;;
        btgl_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#btgl_}"; if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then toggle_bot "$BOT_NUM"; tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; fi ;;
        qup_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#qup_}"; move_bot_in_queue "$BOT_NUM" up; tg_edit_queue_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        qdn_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#qdn_}"; move_bot_in_queue "$BOT_NUM" down; tg_edit_queue_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        bcfg_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#bcfg_}"; rm -f "$CMDS_VIEW_FILE" 2>/dev/null; if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; fi ;;
        bupg_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#bupg_}"; if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then confirm_or_toggle_upgrade "$BOT_NUM"; tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; fi ;;
        biname_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#biname_}"; request_bot_input "$BOT_NUM" name ;;
        biuser_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#biuser_}"; request_bot_input "$BOT_NUM" user ;;
        bint_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#bint_}"; request_bot_input "$BOT_NUM" interval ;;
        bcmds_*)
          BOT_NUM="${CB#bcmds_}"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then tg_edit_cmds_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; fi ;;
        cadd_*)
          BOT_NUM="${CB#cadd_}"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then addcmd_start "$BOT_NUM"; fi ;;
        cedit_*)
          REST="${CB#cedit_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%%_*}"; _BID="${REST#*_}"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -n "$_BID" ]; then tg_edit_cbatch_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM" "$_BID"; fi ;;
        cbe_cmd_*) REST="${CB#cbe_cmd_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%%_*}"; _BID="${REST#*_}"; set_single_input "${BOT_NUM}:cbcmd:${_BID}"; tg_edit_text_km "$CB_MSG_ID" "$(T cmds_cmd_prompt)" "$(build_cbatch_keyboard "$BOT_NUM" "$_BID")" ;;
        cbe_time_*) REST="${CB#cbe_time_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%%_*}"; _BID="${REST#*_}"; set_single_input "${BOT_NUM}:cbtime:${_BID}"; tg_edit_text_km "$CB_MSG_ID" "$(T cmds_time_prompt_at)" "$(build_cbatch_keyboard "$BOT_NUM" "$_BID")" ;;
        cbe_x_*) REST="${CB#cbe_x_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%%_*}"; _BID="${REST#*_}"; set_single_input "${BOT_NUM}:cbx:${_BID}"; tg_edit_text_km "$CB_MSG_ID" "$(T cmds_xcount_prompt)" "$(build_cbatch_keyboard "$BOT_NUM" "$_BID")" ;;
        cbe_rem_*) REST="${CB#cbe_rem_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%%_*}"; _BID="${REST#*_}"; set_single_input "${BOT_NUM}:cbrem:${_BID}"; tg_edit_text_km "$CB_MSG_ID" "$(T cmds_remaining_prompt)" "$(build_cbatch_keyboard "$BOT_NUM" "$_BID")" ;;
        cbe_del_*)
          REST="${CB#cbe_del_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%%_*}"; _BID="${REST#*_}"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ] && [ -n "$_BID" ]; then
            delete_batch "$BOT_NUM" "$_BID"
            tg_send "$(T cmds_deleted)"
            tg_edit_cmds_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"
          fi ;;
        acd_*) handle_addcmd_callback "$CB" "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        delay_*)
          REST="${CB#delay_}"; BOT_NUM="${REST%_}"; D_SEC="${REST##*_}"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ] && echo "$D_SEC" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
            for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$BOT_NUM"); do
              cur_t=$(get_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch"); [ "$cur_t" -lt "$NOW" ] && cur_t=$NOW
              set_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch" $((cur_t + D_SEC))
            done
            set_selected_timer_bot "$BOT_NUM"
            tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"
          fi ;;
        res_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#res_}"; if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            for batch in $(get_bot_batches "$BOT_NUM"); do
              int_v="" type=""
              type=$(get_batch_type "$BOT_NUM" "$batch")
              if [ "$type" = "time" ]; then set_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch" "$(next_time_occurrence "$(get_batch_time "$BOT_NUM" "$batch")")"
              else int_v=$(get_batch_interval "$BOT_NUM" "$batch"); set_batch_target "$BOT_NUM" "$batch" $((NOW + int_v)); fi
            done
            set_selected_timer_bot "$BOT_NUM"; tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; fi ;;
        rep_*) VAL="${CB#rep_}"
          if echo "$VAL" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$'; then
            [ "$VAL" -lt 60 ] && { VAL=60; log_error E20; }
            echo "$VAL" > "$STATE_INTERVAL"; REPORT_INTERVAL=$VAL; NEXT_REPORT=$((NOW + REPORT_INTERVAL))
            tg_edit_reports_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"
          fi ;;
        rbidx_*) IDX="${CB#rbidx_}"; rollback_by_index "$IDX" ;;
        bdel_*) BOT_NUM="${CB#bdel_}"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then
            if is_stock_bot "$BOT_NUM"; then set_pending_action "$BOT_NUM" delete ""; send_stock_confirm "$BOT_NUM" delete
            else delete_bot "$BOT_NUM"; tg_edit_bots_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"; fi
          fi ;;
        pcy_*)
          BOT_NUM="${CB#pcy_}"
          _P_ACT=$(cat "${PENDING_ACTION_PREFIX}${BOT_NUM}.txt" 2>/dev/null | head -n1)
          _P_VAL=$(cat "${PENDING_VALUE_PREFIX}${BOT_NUM}.txt" 2>/dev/null)
          clear_pending_action "$BOT_NUM"
          [ -n "$_P_ACT" ] && execute_bot_action "$BOT_NUM" "$_P_ACT" "$_P_VAL"
          if [ "$_P_ACT" = "delete" ]; then tg_edit_bots_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"
          else if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; else tg_edit_bots_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"; fi; fi ;;
        pcn_*)
          BOT_NUM="${CB#pcn_}"; clear_pending_action "$BOT_NUM"
          if [ -d "$BOT_DIR/$BOT_NUM" ]; then tg_edit_bot_cfg_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" "$BOT_NUM"; else tg_edit_bots_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"; fi ;;
        exp_tg) export_current_version_to_telegram; tg_edit_update_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        show_commands) tg_send "$(build_main_menu_text)" ;;
        addbot_cancel) addbot_session_clear; tg_send "$(T addbot_cancelled)"; tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
        addbot_back) if addbot_session_exists; then addbot_go_back; else tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID"; fi ;;
        abq_up_*) _BID="${CB#abq_up_}"; addbot_session_move_batch "$_BID" up; addbot_show_queue_menu ;;
        abq_dn_*) _BID="${CB#abq_dn_}"; addbot_session_move_batch "$_BID" down; addbot_show_queue_menu ;;
        abq_save) addbot_finalize; tg_edit_main_menu "$CB_MSG_ID" ;;
      esac
    fi
  fi
  if [ "$PROCESSED_UPDATE" = "1" ]; then continue; fi
  NOW2=$(date +%s)
  EL=$((NOW2 - LOOP_TS))
  [ "$EL" -lt 1 ] && sleep $((1 - EL))
done
